On resumption, finish positive catalogue/property descriptions, per-protocol round-ledger summaries, qualified ancestry and actual blocked-document requests in geturl.txt. Then reconcile counts and progress. Expected collected count after admission is 544 including WIP, not 549; five earlier identity-only registrations remain excluded. Do not rerun old registration helpers. TimeLapse-seq was already documented and was excluded from this batch before duplicate admission; ClickSeq is the independently verified tenth selection.

Critical source decisions preserved:

- Mod-seq uses the official correction's GTGACT arm, rather than archived TGTACT. Official full correction text was read; normal original caching returned HTTP403. Keep its single correction-backed reverse-primer exemption narrow.
- SHAPE-JuMP distinguishes TBIA crosslinking from IA monoadduct and DMSO controls. SHARC-exo ligates before reversal.
- MINCE retains saved input before affinity capture; fCAB keeps physical oxime distinct from bisulfite reporting. fC-CET distinguishes NaOH washing from DTT release and retained residual adduct from copied base. Its publisher update concerned affiliations only. Source-printed 400 mM is retained with qualification, not silently normalized.
- scGR keeps UV tag supernatant separate from RNA pellet. TRAPS has positive published SI chemistry but an unread main workflow; covalent antibody–streptavidin coupling precedes biotin-DNA association. TetTCR's institutional original and published Table 10 were verified; no Han2014 ancestry was invented.
- ClickSeq's selected default is the FHV library: 100 ng RNA, 1:20 AzNTP:dNTP, 300–500 nt selection, HiSeq SE100. CrPV is a separate library and host mRNA mapping comes from the FHV dataset. Active Cu(I)-TBTA is known; stock formulation and exact gated S1 oligos remain unresolved. Preserve triazole/template/off-product distinctions.

`tools/scrape_primers.py` now recognizes two tightly bounded original formats: standalone modifier cells inside a wrapped oligo (TRAPS), and TetTCR's `(N:25252525)` balanced-mixture directive followed by eleven explicit `(N)` tokens (12 bases total). Whole-row matching rejects truncated/concrete/wrong-width candidates. Both span parsers use bounded immutable-result caches. Preserve exact modification/source mapping and negative regression tests; do not loosen the general source guard.

# Protocol audit status

Snapshot: 2026-10-17. This is the persistent curation worklist; use it with the
round ledgers instead of reconstructing progress from conversation memory.

## Handover: intended result for every protocol

The task is to make each page a trustworthy, source-specific explanation of library
construction: the oligos and their modifications, molecular intermediates, reactions,
products and off-products, final library and acquired reads. Check the existing model
against the original source; building successfully alone does not validate its chemistry.

The longer-term design is a mostly declarative, protocol-owned graph analyzed by a
small set of protocol-neutral molecular validators. Those validators check explicit
relations such as pairing, primer binding, junction compatibility and sequence
propagation. They do not derive an experimental recipe, prove reaction efficiency,
choose a preferred fold, or turn missing model data into published absence. No DSL
is required. Capped source passes bound investigation; the active validation migration
also replaces queued recipes with protocol-owned native `ProtocolGraph`s. Building
those graphs is part of the migration goal. Neither a graph nor a capped pass
certifies complete chemistry, and conversion to one Python file is not required.

### What to do with each kind of file

`<slug>` below is the exact directory from `catalogue/ours.tsv`. Resolve actual
filenames in that directory; legacy wrappers and single-module protocols coexist.

| File or location | Work to perform | Evidence or completion condition |
|---|---|---|
| `<slug>/tools/<protocol>.py` or its existing declaration module | Correct source-specific oligos, terminal modifications, selected variant, enzyme/reaction order, intermediate states, products/fates, identifier meaning and read layout. Put source choices and caveats here. | Every new assertion traces to an inspected original passage. Keep unassigned values explicit. Do not import another chemistry's assumptions to fill gaps. |
| Module's `protocol_graph()` | Connect each declared operation to the actual preceding substrate. Declare reagents, branch inputs, expected/candidate off-products and their fates. Use existing typed geometry and validation APIs. | Narrow molecular claims can be checked from supplied states; unresolved claims remain visible. Candidate products are labelled as such. Physical removal requires a represented separation, not just failure to amplify or a later computational filter. |
| Page content in that module, or existing `tools/build_page.py` / `tools/page_parts.py` | Make the corrected graph, final library, primer sites, off-products and warnings visible on the public schematic. Keep the existing entry point unless a local fix needs adjustment. | The page renders graph-owned products and warnings; it does not maintain a second hand-written list that can drift. Published discrepancies name locations/values, the model's choice and remaining uncertainty. Repository mistakes are described as model corrections. |
| `<slug>/01_*.md` main research note | Record the original citation/version, exact sections read, verbatim modified oligos, derivations, step order, final/read layout and remaining questions. Correct active claims copied from superseded preprints; preserve the historical review trail separately. | A reader can distinguish source facts, derived claims and unverified assignments. Computable facts use the model rather than duplicated numbers. Explain why each unresolved item remains open. |
| `<slug>/tools/selftest.py`, or module `selftest()` | Run the affected suite. Add a check only for a meaningful source transcription or behavior the construction API cannot enforce. | Checks pass or explicitly skip absent optional source material. Do not add tests that merely restate constants, step counts or labels to make the protocol look validated. |
| `catalogue/source_reviews/<slug>.json` | Append a bounded check with reviewer/date, exact original identity and locations, findings, deferred sources/questions and verification results. Preserve previous independent passes. | The record states what was actually read and whether linked chemistry information was followed, inaccessible or deferred. A broad historical `blocked` label is not a new finding. Do not promote a capped pass to full acceptance. |
| `catalogue/curation_rounds/<round>.tsv` | Update only this protocol's row with inspected scope, positive corrections or no-change finding, named deferrals and actual checks. | No completed row has placeholder evidence. Timeouts, source skips and symbolic inputs are recorded separately from passing exact-sequence checks. Unread originals stay pending/deferred. |
| `<slug>/ref/MANIFEST.md` | Update re-obtaining instructions only when a source locator or identity actually changes. | The checkout contains provenance/instructions, not downloaded third-party material. |
| External `sources/<slug>/MANIFEST.tsv`, originals and `.txt` twins | Inspect availability/provenance; use existing source fetchers for missing originals. Read/write directly under `/husky/henriksson/NGS101_papers/`. Scrape article/supplement text as a reading aid. | Validate the returned document and cited revision. No repository-local copies or compatibility links. No-hit scraping is not evidence of absence; inspect the pointed-to source before using a hit. |
| Generated `<slug>/*.html` and `_site/` | Rebuild affected pages and inspect relevant output; never hand-edit or stage them. | Diagrams, inferred styling, warnings and source notices agree with the declaration; relevant relative links and duplex pairing work. |
| `catalogue/ours.tsv`, `properties.tsv`, citation snapshot | Leave alone during ordinary corrections. Update consistently only when evidence warrants a protocol identity/boundary or catalogue change. | A later implementation is not silently relabelled as its predecessor. Record boundary work outside the cap rather than casually moving directories or merging protocols. |
| `lib/`, `protocol_families/` | For this round, prefer local corrections. Change shared code only to fix a demonstrated neutral computation/validation/rendering bug, checking affected consumers. | Shared code does not choose protocol-specific oligos, conditions, products or evidence defaults. Similarity is an analysis result, not permission to extrapolate chemistry. |
| `AUDIT.md` | Update counts, ownership, completed scopes and the next actions after changing the ledger. | Another worker can resume from these files without conversation memory or temporary scripts. |

### Source-reading checklist for each listed protocol

1. Establish the defining published article or applicable vendor manual and its exact
   revision. Distinguish standard, low-input, single-cell, organism and kit variants.
   Use the published supplements; normally disregard superseded preprints.
2. Read Methods and the relevant chemistry/oligo supplement within the cap. Check
   source manifests and run the oligo scraper, including modifications and wrapped
   table sequences. Read the original passage behind each fact used in an edit.
3. Compare the declaration with those passages: oligo identity and polarity; RNA/DNA
   residues and blocked/activated ends; annealing and exposed ends; catalysts and
   conditions; step order; branches; cleanup/recovery; and the final junctions.
4. Check identifier features and readout: sample versus cell versus molecular IDs,
   primer orientation/landing, index lengths, informative cycles and actual acquired
   cycles. Presence of a standard primer site does not establish that it was read.
5. Check products and fates. Degradation is not physical discard; computational
   exclusion is not experimental separation. Include source-supported products and
   explicitly scoped candidates without claiming experimental observation from a
   sequence calculation alone.
6. Keep uncertainty in structured model data. An unresolved operation may use a
   warned “magic happens” boundary with a reason. Do not invent a mechanism to make
   validation pass or hide a known intermediate just to reduce warnings.
7. If an original appears inconsistent, verify the specific source locations, display
   the discrepancy on every affected schematic, state what the model uses and why,
   and retain uncertainty. An ordinary preprint-to-publication revision is not a
   published-source discrepancy.
8. Record what remains uninspected and move on when the search cap is reached.
   Detailed protocols, predecessor methods, vendor originals and chemistry-relevant
   links are named follow-ups. Two independent readers and the complete linked-source
   checklist are required before accepting confirmed absence; this bounded pass
   does not supply that gate.

### What counts as finishing one bounded pass

- The ledger and review identify the source scope actually inspected. A no-change
  result is valid when backed by inspected passages; cosmetic edits are not required.
- Justified edits are consistent across the declaration, graph, note and public page.
  Source uncertainty and discrepancy notices remain visible where relevant.
- Run the affected local suite and page builder. For wrapper-free modules use
  `python3 tools/protocol.py test <slug>` and `build <slug>`; legacy protocols use
  their existing `tools/selftest.py` and `tools/build_page.py` entry points.
- Run `tools/lint_pairing.py` on affected schematics, scoped
  `tools/check_chemistry.py <slug>`, `tools/selftest_source_oligos.py <slug>`, and
  `tools/audit_protocol_sources.py --check <slug>`. Rebuild/check changed Markdown
  with `build_docs.py`. Shared tests are needed for shared-code changes, not repeated
  once per protocol.
- Bound source-guard runtime per protocol. A timeout is an incomplete check, not a
  pass or a safety rejection. Report exact oligos checked and symbolic/unrepresented
  inputs; zero contradictions is not a proof of the whole experimental chemistry.
- Stage named batch files only, excluding fetched originals and generated output.
  Only one maintainer agent edits this repository (as of 2026-10-17); it may run
  parallel sub-workers, each confined to its own protocol directory and review JSON.

verification; no bounded pass satisfies the two-reader missing-information gate.

Those statuses are recorded here as a census, not revalidated by this snapshot.

inspected. `deferred*` means attempted but the intended original-source pass was

corrections, remaining questions and verification limits.

## Deferred defining-source passes — 2026-10-17 follow-up

Ledger: [2026-10-17-deferred-followup.tsv](catalogue/curation_rounds/2026-10-17-deferred-followup.tsv).

ChIP-seq stays `deferred`, now with three originals read. Round 2 retrieved nothing
because the catalogued DOI is wrong: it is `10.1038/nmeth1068`. The main article is
paywalled. Its Supplementary Methods, the 2007 Illumina ChIP-Seq guide 11257047 and
Schmidt 2009 were read. Chromatin conditions now come from the supplement; library
enzymes, 200±25 bp gel and 18-cycle Phusion PCR come from the guide, marked assumed
for this study. Adapter/primer bases are inferred; read length 27 unverified; a
constant-restating selftest check was removed. Chemistry 3 conditional/10 unresolved/
0 contradictory; guard checks no exact inputs. Extra-source rows added.

DisCo is `checked_with_corrections`: the author bench protocol (EPFL, Sep 2021) was
read in full; the defining Nature Methods full text is still paywalled. The generic
RT/PCR route was replaced by cp-chip RT with rGrGrG TSO, ExoI, SMART PCR, 0.6x AMPure,
p7|ME Tn5, SDS stop, P5 SMART + i7 PCR and 0.7x AMPure. Four printed oligos shown with
modifications; custom SeqB Read 1 landing computed. Superseded bioRxiv v3 used only as
a labelled lead. Chemistry 17 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 3 exact/3 unrepresented.
Still open: article Methods, bead oligo, Tn5 adapter, i7/Read 2 primers, second reader.

RiboMethSeq stays `deferred_no_original`: Birkedal 2015 article and Wiley SI remain
unavailable (publisher 403, no repository copy). Marchand 2016 main text and Tables
S2/S4 were inspected; the page caveat shows Marchand's second-hand account of the
original Ion Torrent direct-ligation chemistry, the graph gains the RNeasy MinElute
cleanup after end repair, and the note no longer marks unread claims green.
Chemistry 9 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 0 exact checked, 5 without exact states.

10x-chromium-gem-x-single-cell-3-ge-v4 — checked_with_corrections. Read CG000731 Rev A kit contents, Introduction figures p14/p16, §1.5, §2.1–2.3, §3.1–3.6, Step 4 run parameters and the p80 appendix; Rev B appendix identical. Bead primer, final library and 28/10/10/90 confirmed. Graph now shows Recovery Agent/oil removal, Dynabeads and SPRIselect cleanups, both double-sided 0.6X/0.8X size selections with discarded fractions, and one Fragmentation Mix for fragmentation and ER/A-tailing; source conditions and cycle ranges in labels; sequenced product is the size-selected library. Deferred: TSO B and Ligation Mix structures, TT physical oligos, PN-2000089 current-kit identity, linked 10x documents. Suite 4/4, pairing 0, chemistry 16 unresolved/0 contradictory, source guard 2 checked/0 failed.

10x-chromium-gem-x-single-cell-5-ge-v3 — checked_with_corrections. Read CG000733 Rev A kit contents, Introduction (13-nt bead TSO, Master Mix poly(dT) primers), §1.5, §2.2–2.3, Step 5 §5.1–5.7, Step 6 run parameters and the appendix. Bead TSO, final GEX library and 28/10/10/90 confirmed. Graph now shows Recovery Agent/oil removal, Dynabeads and SPRIselect cleanups, both double-sided 0.6X/0.8X size selections with discarded fractions, and one Fragmentation Mix for fragmentation and ER/A-tailing; 12–17 cDNA cycles and SI 5–16 cycles in labels; sequenced product is the size-selected library. Deferred: RT Primer B and Ligation Mix structures, TT physical oligos, PN-2000089 current-kit identity, linked 10x documents. Suite 4/4, pairing 0, chemistry 16 unresolved/0 contradictory, source guard 2 checked/0 failed.

10x GEM-X 5' V(D)J v3 — checked with corrections. Read CG000734 Rev A kit tables pp.8-9, overview pp.17-20, sequencing p.98 and Appendix pp.111-113. Its image-only p.113 prints the forward primer and all 46 Mix 1/2 v2 enrichment primers. All 46 match CG000331 Rev E pp.73-74, so the v3 guide is now the primary source for the pools. A local GemXV3Chemistry subclass marks Poly-dT RT Primer B (PN-2001110, sequence not printed) as inferred from v2 PN-2000007. It also corrects the cDNA-primer provenance (PN-2000089, sequences in CG000331 p.72) and labels the index-primer ends as derived from the product. Deferred: Primer B sequence, cleanup/size-split graph steps, TT Set A primer original. Verification: suite, page, pairing 0, chemistry 1/1/12/0, guard 0 exact/11 symbolic, Markdown and schema pass.

10x Chromium 3' GE v1 — checked with corrections. Read published PMC5241818 Results l.25-32 and Methods l.252, plus CG00026 Rev B Steps 1-3 (GEM-RT, cleanups, cDNA amp, shearing, ER/A-tail, R1 Adaptor ligation, SI-PCR). The shared ChromiumRNAOps graph had drawn enzymatic fragmentation and a 3'-dT Read-2 adapter ligation. Both contradict the sources: Covaris M220 shearing, and ligation of the R1 Adaptor Mix with P7/R2 bead-derived. chromium_v1.py now owns a local 17-step graph with source cleanups, unprinted primer inputs and unavailable adaptor geometry. Deferred: R1 Adaptor, SI-PCR/Sample Index, cDNA Primer and RT Primer identities; bead oligo/TSO; kit P/N mapping. Verification: suite, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/19/0, guard 0 exact/0 symbolic, Markdown and schema pass.

10x 3' v2: re-read CG000108 Rev A Fig.4 (lines68–143) and CG00052 Rev D Steps2.1–3.6 plus sequencing table with the bundle scraper. Every printed oligo (bead, TSO, cDNA primers, both adapter strands, index primers) and the final product match the model; 26/8/0/98 confirmed. Added the Rev D bead cleanups (Silane; 0.6X; 0.6X/0.8X; 0.8X; 0.6X/0.8X) as graph CLEANUP steps with discarded waste fractions; the sequenced library is the post-index size-selected product. The page now shows the CG000108 PN-22016 vs CG00052 Rev D PN-220106 cDNA Primer Mix part-number discrepancy. Deferred: pre-Rev D guide revisions, exact RT primer/TSO input states (shared op), one-tube frag/ER drawn as two steps.

10x 3' v3: re-read CG000183 Rev A Appendix oligonucleotide sequences (lines4868–5016, pp54–55), Steps2.1–3.6 and the sequencing table with the bundle scraper. All printed GE oligos match the model; 28/8/0/91 confirmed; the two existing published-discrepancy notices still hold. Added the guide's bead cleanups (SILANE; 0.6X; 0.6X/0.8X; 0.8X; 0.6X/0.8X) as graph CLEANUP steps with discarded waste fractions, sequencing the post-index size-selected product. The page now shows the printed Capture Seq 1 gel-bead primer labelled unused for GE, with a transcription check. Deferred: later guide revisions, exact RT primer/TSO input states (shared op), Capture Seq 1 side-product behaviour.

**10x-chromium-single-cell-3-v3-cell-surface-protein** — checked with corrections. Read the vendor guide CG000185 Rev B: the stepwise overview, GEM-RT, steps 2.2–2.3, 4.1–4.2, the step 5 run parameters and the oligo appendix. Also read the CG000149 Rev D labeling overview. The printed Feature cDNA Primers 2, Feature SI Primers 2 and Chromium i7 primers replace inferred placeholders, with exact PCR geometry. The amplified feature DNA now carries the 27-nt Partial Read 1N. Added post GEM-RT recovery and the 2.0X supernatant and 1.2X final SPRI cleanups. The 0.6X pellet is now a stored gene-expression branch rather than waste. Read 2 changed from the 25-bp minimum to the recommended 91 cycles. Deferred: the Rev A→B delta, antibody 3' blocking, antibody barcode assignments and the GEX branch.

**10x-chromium-single-cell-3-v3-crispr-screening** — checked with corrections. Read the vendor guide CG000184 Rev A: run parameters, GEM-RT, steps 2.2–2.3 and 4.1–4.5, and the sgRNA and oligo appendices (p.66–70). Also read the CG000197 Rev A introduction. The Feature PCR product now carries P5/Read 1N and the Read 2 arm. Sample Index PCR uses the printed Feature SI Primers 2 and Chromium i7 with exact geometry. Added recovery and all five SPRI selections, with the 0.6X pellet stored for GEX. Read 2 changed from 50 cycles, below the guide's 70-bp minimum, to the recommended 91. The page now shows two appendix discrepancies: the Feature PCR heading names PN-2000099 while the step 4.2 table uses PN-2000098, and the Step 4.4 drawing omits the TSO bases. Deferred: v3.1 CG000316, the TSO exact state, hairpin/CS2 guides and guide library assignment.

**10x-chromium-single-cell-5-ge-v1** — checked with corrections. Re-read CG000109 Rev D text lines 1–70, 100–115, 288–312 and 490–590 (Table 2B: Step 1.5, Protocol 4.1, 6.3, 6.5). The graph no longer uses the legacy ChromiumRNAOps recipe. It declares the printed Table 2B oligos as reagent states, checks both PCRs with exact primer geometry and declares the adaptor's 3'-T end. It uses the printed RT product as the template-switch output and adds the 98-cycle Read 2. The page shows the 20- vs 22-nt PN-220106 reverse-primer discrepancy (Table 2B line 526 vs Table 2A line 312) and uses Table 2B. Deferred: the revision-matched 2017 CG000086, adaptor/oligo end modifications and the fate of non-bead-end fragments. Suite 8/8 passes; chemistry 3/1/9/0; source guard 6/0 (checked against Rev K only).

**10x-chromium-single-cell-5-vdj-v2** — checked with corrections. Read the CG000331 Rev F appendix lines 6790–7190, sequencing parameters (lines 6025–6050, 6125–6150), kit tables and Step 2.1/4.4 text. Every modelled oligo and the 26/10/10/90 run agree with the guide. The graph no longer uses the legacy tenx_graph helpers. It declares the printed oligos and named reagents as inputs and adds the missing Step 2.1 Recovery Agent/Dynabeads cleanup. The printed PN-2000094 adaptor's 3'-T end is now declared. Deferred: the first-strand states still come from shared vdj_common, SPRI cleanups and the GEX branch are not drawn, end modifications are unstated, and the Rev F copy is a public mirror. Suite passes; chemistry 3/1/13/0; source guard 6/0 (checked against the Rev E bundle).

**10x-chromium-single-cell-atac** — checked with corrections. Re-read CG000168 Rev A Stepwise Objectives, GEM/SI-PCR conditions, Sequencing and Appendix (lines 3343–3441) plus the Set A CSV. The final library had a concrete i7 word TAAGGCGA found in no 10x source; it is now an 8-N placeholder. Sample Index PCR primers, printed in the Appendix, are declared with exact geometry instead of 'not asserted'. Gap fill is marked INFERRED; same-adaptor Tn5 products have an unresolved fate rather than discard. Deferred: Tn5 release/Barcoding Enzyme, CG000169 nuclei protocol, v1.1/v2 step differences, bead attachment chemistry.

**10x-chromium-single-cell-multiome-atac-gene-expression** — checked with corrections. Read CG000338 Rev G reagent lists, Steps 1–7, Sequencing and Appendix pp. 80–81, plus the CG000168 Rev A Appendix entry for PN-2000128. The graph now follows the guide: both GEM reactions, cleanup and one pooled Pre-Amplification PCR come before the ATAC/cDNA split, followed by cDNA amplification, fragmentation and adaptor ligation. The undocumented bridge extension is now a warned unresolved join. A false 'primer printed' claim was corrected, and acquired cycles were added (ATAC 50/8/24/49, GEX 28/10/10/90). Deferred: undisclosed kit oligo bases, ATAC joining chemistry, Rev B/F step differences, nuclei protocol.

10x-fixed-rna-profiling-flex — checked with corrections. Read CG000674 Rev C workflow pp. 14–19, sequencing pp. 72–73 and Appendix p. 85, CG000529 Rev C p. 4 and CG000621 Rev D Table 7. The RNA final library now follows the printed p. 85 shell with the Small RNA Read 2 arm. This replaces a generic TruSeq endpoint that had a dA junction the source does not print. The Read 2S and i7 sites are derived from the printed constant. A local LHS/RHS Scene uses the CG000621 architecture. A post-hybridization wash and 28/10/10/90 read lengths were added. Deferred: commercial physical probe inventory, the AB oligo attachment (MAGIC), PCR primer sequences and Illumina Read 2S primer originals.

10x-visium-spatial-gene-expression — checked with corrections. Read CG000239 Rev F introduction pp. 16–17, Steps 2.1–2.2, the Step 4 contents and Appendix pp. 69–70. Fixed a repository slicing error: the slide and cDNA forward primers had an extra 5′ C (23 nt); the source prints 22 nt. The graph now follows the source order: second-strand synthesis on the slide, then 0.08 M KOH denaturation and transfer. The released strand is labelled inferred, and the discarded slide is shown. SPRIselect cDNA cleanup, post-fragmentation size selection and post-ligation cleanup were added with waste fates. Deferred: slide attachment chemistry, the qPCR and post-SI size-selection steps, base-level cDNA PCR geometry and the rate-limited Space Ranger pages.

4C-seq (Krijger et al., Methods 2020): checked, no chemistry change. Re-read the published author-hosted PDF §2.3–2.4, §4.1–4.8, §5.1–5.2, §6, §8 and Appendix A. The declared fixation, DpnII/Csp6I example digests, ligations, ProtK/P-bead purifications, 16- and 20-cycle Expand Long Template PCRs with 0.8x AMPure/QIAquick cleanups, and SR 50/75 reads with a 6-nt index agree with the source. The previously pending Appendix A supplement (Elsevier mmc2/mmc3; get_sources does not reach it) was fetched by hand: Supplemental figs 1–2 cover analysis only. Note and ref/MANIFEST updated. Deferred: pipe4C/peakC repositories, historical Illumina pooling-guide revision, PCR1 remainder/PCR2 input-fraction fates.

5' direct-capture Perturb-seq (Replogle et al., Nat Biotechnol 2020): checked with corrections. Read the published JATS Methods (UPR lanes, library preparation, sequencing), the Springer Supplementary Information Notes 1–3, and Supp Table 1. CR1 now comes from the printed pBA900 scaffold upstream of cs2 (A at position 13); the earlier model used the F+E scaffold from lib/crispr, a repository error. The 10x bead oligo bases are now drawn INFERRED with a caveat. The model adds a 0.8x SPRI library cleanup and records the source amounts, cycles and ratios. The page shows the published 0.6 uM (Supp Note 3) vs 0.6 mM (Methods, cs1 branch) primer-concentration discrepancy; the model uses 0.6 µM. Note claims are retraced from the unbundled lab protocol to Supp Note 3. Deferred: 10x CG000086 and kit oligo specifications, Adamson lab PDF, Supp Fig. 1g.

AIRR UMI 5' RACE is `checked_with_corrections`: the defining chapter, which earlier rounds lacked, was obtained from the NCBI Bookshelf open-access package (NBK586949 in book NBK586966). Read: reagents, index tables 2.2.2–2.2.3, RT, PCR1/PCR2 with Table 1, bead purification, sequencing, Fig. 5 and the read layout in 3.10. Added the printed D701–D712 i7 and D501–D504 i5 index lists with transcription checks. Replaced invented segment lengths with the Fig. 5 C-region primer+7N, relabelled the 7-nt linker plus template switch, and added RT/PCR conditions and two-sided NucleoMag selection with discarded fractions. The caveat shows the Fig. 5 4-bp linker label against the text's 12+7. Still open: the Takara kit manual (primer and adapter bases), the Immcantation primer files and a re-run of the two-pass gate.

ARTIC tiled-amplicon sequencing is `checked_with_corrections`: the published typeset article (co-author mirror) is now the authority instead of the PMC author manuscript. Read: Experimental design, Fig. 3, Materials and Procedure Steps 4–12, plus published ESM Table 1. Withdrew the 0.02 µM 'discrepancy', which appears only in the manuscript. The graph now keeps the pools separate through AMPure cleanup and per-pool barcoding/indexing. Added Ultra II/Blunt-TA native barcoding, per-pool KAPA/SureSelectXT2 libraries with 0.8× SPRI removal of adaptor-dimers, and equimolar pooling. The 95 °C vs 98 °C denaturation conflict is shown on the page. Still open: the KAPA, SureSelectXT2 and ONT kit originals and the ARTIC site versions.

ASAP-seq (asap-seq__10.1038+s41587-021-00927-2) — checked with corrections. Read published PMC8763625 Methods 225–242 and Results 122–132, the MOESM1 ASAP protocol, MOESM3 barcodes, and the historical 10x CG000209 Rev A Appendix p48 (fetched this pass). The graph now follows the source order: staining before fixation, tagmentation, then the bridge in the GEM barcoding mix with 40°C annealing, silane, and a SPRI split into the ATAC and tag fractions. The ATAC library is corrected to P5–cell barcode–Read 1N with no i5 sample index, and its PCR geometry is exact. The TSA tag library is rebuilt from the bead primer, BOA and RPI-x; the barcode adjacency is inferred. Unsourced read lengths were removed. Deferred: the cited Rev D guides, PMC author supplements, TotalSeq conjugate structure, the unprinted P5 primer, and the TSB/hashtag libraries.

DOGMA-seq (dogma-seq__10.1038+s41587-021-00927-2) — checked with corrections. Read published PMC8763625 Methods 244–262, the MOESM1 DOGMA protocol, MOESM3 barcodes, and the cited 10x CG000338 Rev A (pp17–18, p68, Appendix p79). The ATAC library is corrected to P5–cell barcode–CGCGTCTG spacer–Read 1N with no i5 index. The RNA library is transcribed as printed. The tag library is rebuilt from the Multiome RNA bead primer with SI-PCR/RPI-x geometry and a 12-nt UMI. The invented tag-capture primer is replaced by GEM poly(dT) capture, preamplification with the exact ADT additive, and the 25/35/35% allocation; the GEX cDNA/ligation steps are now explicit. Unsourced read lengths were removed. Deferred: PMC author supplements, TotalSeq conjugate structure, 10x index primer sequences, the GEX P5-prefix check, and the HTO library.

| [ChAIR](chair__10.1038+s41592-025-02658-7/) | `checked_with_corrections` | PMC NIHMS supplements 1–5 and Supplement_and_Figures (download gate; identity vs Springer SI not compared). CG000338 revision used, kit oligos, sample indices and read lengths. Fate of non-biotinylated DNA in the M-280 supernatant and of half-ligated linker products. ChIATAC predecessor (ref 11) and Wang 2021 Current Protocols linker source. Springer MOESM2 is image-only and was not inspected. |

**ASTAR-seq** (`astar-seq__10.1101+829960`) — checked with corrections. Read the published Genome Research JATS (lines 82, 162, 166), published Supplemental Table 5 and C1 worksheet, and the bioRxiv v1 Supplementary Experimental Procedures (media-6 1562–1665). The published Supplemental Methods PDF returned 404/403, so preprint detail is used with a label. The citation now points to the published article. cDNA elution no longer claims a non-biotinylated strand, because both strands are biotinylated after on-chip PCR. Added the missing 9+11-cycle C1-P2-PCR2 re-amplification and AMPure cleanup. The harvest volume (5.5 vs ~3.5 µL) is now marked unverified. Deferred: publisher Supplemental Methods PDF, the Fluidigm C1 mRNA-seq manual and Nextera XT originals, and the strand form of the eluate.

**ATAC-seq** (`atac-seq__10.1038+nmeth.2688`) — checked with corrections. Read the PMC author-manuscript Online Methods (lines 71–85, 99) and publisher MOESM200 Supplementary Table 1 (lines 1–103). Ad1_noMX and Ad2.1 match exactly and are now exact graph inputs checked by the source guard. Added the MinElute and Qiagen PCR cleanups, with discarded flow-through. The NEBNext 72 °C extension and the qPCR-limited 10–12 cycles are now stated. Declared 50×8×50 HiSeq reads with a single i7 index; the MiSeq 34×8×34 run is named. The thin note was rewritten with verbatim oligos and steps. Deferred: the paywalled typeset article and the historical Nextera kit guide (loaded adapter sequences).

**Atrandi WGS** (`atrandi-wgs__10.1101+2025.06.20.660799`) — checked with corrections. Preprint v2 is still the latest version (no published article). Read v2 Methods 1322–1404, the barcoding kit V3 steps 1–44 and the library-prep V3 sections I–VI. Corrections: the graph now has the 0.8X AMPure step after Release, NEBNext Ultra II ligation reagents kept separate from the kit's barcode ligase, the post-ligation 0.8X cleanup, the Ultra II Q5 PCR and the double-sided 0.6X/0.8X cleanup. The single End Prep mix is shown as an inferred split into repair and dA-tailing. The source guard flags the three V3 oligos only because the vendor text is not declared in extra_sources; this was already the case before this pass. Deferred: the ResolveDNA manual, acquired read lengths, the custom-index endpoint, cassette geometry and the v1.3 identity.

**BacDrop** (`bacdrop__10.1016+j.cell.2023.01.002`) — checked with corrections. Read PMC10014032 STAR Methods 177–217, the Key Resources Table and the Figure 1B image, plus the 10x CG000209 Rev G bead oligo (newly fetched). In Figure 1B the UMI comes before CB1, so Read 1 now reads UMI 1–8, then CB1 9–21. The batch TruSeq dA-ligation endpoint was replaced with a local Nextera XT endpoint. Its PBS1 Read-1 remainder is inferred, because Table S1 is still behind the PMC download gate. The graph now has DNase I, SMRT_dT second strand, bead capture, cleanups, P5/SMRT_PCR enrichment, tagmentation and P5/N7 PCR. Deferred: Table S1/supplements, the Nextera XT/N7 originals, NEB E7850 and the 10x whitelist.

**BD Rhapsody** — checked with corrections. I read the Yoon 2019 Methods (JATS lines 418–447), the BD WTA alpha protocol 23-21179-00 (Randomer line 78, RPE, RPE PCR, index PCR, sequencing), the bead cartoon and Exonuclease I steps in instrument guide 214062 Rev 2.0, and the PMC Cloud supplements, which hold qPCR primers only. The oligo set and graph topology are unchanged. I removed leftover details of the withdrawn reconstruction from the note ((T)18, CLS blocks, AGAGAGAGGGGGG end). The page now shows a graph-owned discrepancy between the paper's ligation route and BD's RPE route. The read-to-feature assignment is now marked inferred. One revision caveat is recorded: the alpha protocol cites guide Rev 1.0, but the copy on disk is Rev 2.0. Still deferred: Rev 1.0, BD guides 214063/47395, Fan 2015, and every bead or primer base.

**BLESS** — checked with corrections. I read the Crosetto 2013 Online Methods (JATS lines 125–153) and the published Springer Supplementary Information: Table 1 for run modes, and Table 5 on the rendered page, which marks the biotin by cyan only. The graph now fragments with HaeIII and no longer has the unsourced distal blunting step. It adds the P1/P3 Phusion PCR (18 cycles) before the XhoI digest; the old graph cut with XhoI first. TruSeq Kit v2 is now a warned kit boundary. I removed the unsourced 75+6+75 read lengths and the /5Phos/ on L1/L3. The sequencing intro now cites the GAII single-end and HiSeq paired-end modes. The source guard checks P1 and P3 exactly. Still deferred: the TruSeq v2 original, linker phosphorylation, read lengths, and the L2 and 454 branches.

bliss__10.1038+ncomms15058 — checked_with_corrections. Read the published PMC5437291 JATS (workflow l.86, adapter prep l.132, R1 prefix l.148/158), the Supplementary Fig. 1a caption, Supplementary Data 1 A1–A11 and the Protocol Exchange Research Square v2 procedure (l.160–290). Repository transcription error: A1 had an extra G at the T7/RA5 junction. It is fixed, and a Supplementary Data 1 transcription check was added. The graph now covers the ProtEx v2 route from in-situ lysis through high-salt washes, extraction, sonication, IVT, DNase/RNAClean, RA3/RT/RP1+RPI PCR and the final AMPure cleanup, with explicit waste fates. Legacy finish_rt_pcr and molecule callers were removed. The page shows the published A3/A6 duplicate-barcode discrepancy. Deferred: the cited protex.2017.018 original revision, ProtEx Supplementary Table 2, RA3 end modifications, the RPI member, and the exact final library and read cycles.

botseqs__10.1073+pnas.1607794113 — deferred (partial original). Read the published PMC5024639 HTML: dilution rationale l.1243, library generation, Watson/Crick definition and sequencing l.1246–1256, Methods l.1460–1463. The SI Appendix and Dataset S1 remain gated (PMC download gate, pnas.org 403, PMC Cloud 404). Added the source shearing, input and kit details, the MiSeq dilution-series basis, and an inferred 350-bp size selection placed per the 2017 guide (caveated). Corrected the unsupported 'indexed reverse PCR primer' labels; the PCR primers, polymerase and cycles are now unidentified pending the SI. Reads limited to the stated 2x100 R1/R2. Deferred: the SI Appendix, Dataset S1, and the 2016 kit revision (index length, size-selection placement).

Butt-seq (Yu and Rosbash 2023, Genes Dev, PMC10270195): this capped pass read the published Methods library-preparation section (XML lines 175–195), the Supplemental Detailed Protocol (DOCX text lines 289–420, 570–600 and 725–739) and the primer workbook. It added the two missing bead cleanups: EtOH-supplemented AMPure after NaOH/HCl, and SDS TGIRT dissociation followed by AMPure after the second strand. It named the Ultra II Q5 PCR mix and its 72 C meR1R fill-in. The generic size rule was replaced by 8% TBE PAGE excision (150 to about 700 bp) and crush-and-soak recovery. The page now shows the article versus detailed-protocol differences in bead volume (30 vs 25 uL) and PCR program without choosing either. Still open: the R1/R2 cycle split within the 75-cycle kit, the physical Read 2 primer, and reconditioning PCR, which is not drawn.

CAGE (Takahashi et al. 2012, Nat Protoc, PMC4094379): this capped pass read the experimental design, Materials, Procedure steps 1–61, Table 1 and Box 1 from the author-manuscript JATS. It corrected a repository modelling error: the final construct was 98 bp but labelled 96 bp. The 13-nt forward-primer tail is restored, and the 3′-linker NN now sits at positions 26–27 of the 27-nt tag window. This placement is derived and is shown as a DetailView. PCR primer binding is now computed exactly on a separate pre-PCR state. Added steps: RNase ONE digestion, NaOH release of the cDNA, double AMPure purification with pooling, streptavidin removal of 3′-linker dimers, and Exonuclease I/QIAquick. Reagents are now named. The page shows the published N6:GN5 ratio conflict (5:1 vs 1:4) and the duplicate Table 1 lower-AGT label. Still deferred: the gated PMC supplement PDF and the predecessor SSLLM/cap-trapper methods.

**CAGEscan** (`cagescan__10.1038+nmeth.1470`) — checked with corrections. We re-read the author-manuscript Online Methods (text 1–118 and 225–241), Supplementary Table 8 (MOESM315 5134–5170) and the JATS Results/Fig. 1 legend. We added the source agarose purity check after the first AMPure cleanup as a QC aliquot; the source does not give its volume. The oligos, operation order and mM-unit discrepancy notice were re-verified and are unchanged. The note now records inferred primer landings computed with lib/. Deferred: assembling the exact primer geometry, the ref 15 barcode set, GAII cycle counts, the pooling step and the bead-kit manuals. Suite 6/6; chemistry 0/0/17/0; source guard 4 exact checked, 0 failed, 2 without exact states.

**CAP-seq** (`cap-seq__10.1371+journal.pgen.1001134`) — checked with corrections. We re-read the published PMC2944787 Methods 218 and 224–226, Results 79 and the Table S1 header. We also re-read the predecessor Skene 2010 JATS 113–135. Its Supplemental Data, NIHMS62106-supplement-01.pdf, is listed in that file but is missing from PMC Cloud, and the PMC page returns HTML. The graph now starts from genomic DNA and follows the source order: a warned delegated MAP-seq preparation, then Solexa adaptor ligation (unavailable geometry), then binding in 0.1 M NaCl to DNaseI-treated CXXC on Ni-Sepharose. Next come the 600/560 mM wash, the 1 M elution and the pool/concentrate/precipitate cleanup. A highlight says that any post-elution amplification is delegated to ref 57. Deferred: the Skene supplement (manual), Illingworth 2008 ref 7, adaptor bases and read length. Suite 1/1; chemistry 0/0/6/0; source guard 0 exact checked, 1 without exact state.

capseq (notes-only; deferred). The published Science article and its supplement are still unobtainable: get_sources records a (manual) row and science.org returns 403. This pass re-verified the author-preprint Supplement 1 CapSeq procedure (lines 276–340; modified TSO at line 282) and independently read the GEO GSM8830586 record. The adapter pair, ligation conditions, 12-cycle KAPA indexing PCR and STARsolo layout match the note, which gains the PCR detail. Only the PatSnap rendering of WO2026190526A1 was reachable. Its Example 3 matches Supplement 1, and its R1 43/i7 6/R2 89 cycles are recorded only as an unverified lead. No sequences were transcribed from it. Deferred: Science Tables S21–S25, the patent PDF, GSM9578506/CapMux read layout and the author repositories. No absence claim.

captrap-seq (checked with corrections). The published Nat Commun full text was fetched through pmccloud:PMC11211341. It shows the paper is by Carbonell-Sala et al., not 'Glinos et al.' as the repository said; corrected in the module, page, note and MANIFEST. Oligos now come from the published Methods (JATS lines 206–238), including the 61-nt UMI8 primer ending ...GAGTAC. Each printed lower linker strand pairs antiparallel with its upper strand (derived, checked by Scene). This gives declared splinted G-N5 (5′ linker) and A5 (3′ linker) ligation geometries in place of unavailable ones. The graph now follows the published order: periodate/biotin hydrazide/RNase ONE cap trapping, M-270 capture, two Mighty Mix ligations, SAP/USER, UMI8 second strand, Exo I, exact 16-cycle LA-PCR and the inferred ONT LSK108/109 endpoint. Results: chemistry 5 established/13 unresolved; source guard 8/8 exact. Deferred: Protocol Exchange, the ref. 46 linker method, ONT/PacBio kit manuals and the cDNA bases at the splint tails.

CEL-Seq (cel-seq__10.1016+j.celrep.2012.08.003) — deferred_no_original. The 2012 Cell Reports article could not be read: get_sources leaves a manual row, Europe PMC has no full text, and both cell.com and the ResearchGate copy cited by earlier reviews return 403. Table S2 and the Results/Methods of the published CEL-Seq2 package were checked instead; the modelled CEL-Seq RT primer matches Table S2 and no schematic change followed. The note's stale source paths and status sentence were corrected and ref/MANIFEST.md added. Deferred: the original article, its extended supplement (barcodes, RA3 ligation, PCR, reads) and the MessageAmp II kit original.

CEL-Seq2 (cel-seq2__10.1186+s13059-016-0938-8) — checked_with_corrections. Read the published PMC4848782 Results/Methods, the Additional file 4 detailed protocol (lines 1–49, 166–339) and Table S2. The manual-well graph is now declared locally in tools/chem.py in protocol order: RT with ERCC, heat inactivation, second strand, pooling, bead cleanup, T7 IVT, ExoSAP-IT on carried-over primers, Mg2+ fragmentation with EDTA stop, RNAClean XP, randomhexRT RT, Phusion RP1/RPIX PCR and two AMPure cleanups. The page shows the 48/96/168 barcode-set inconsistency. Deferred: heat-inactivation branch, internal-fragment off-product, RPIX indices, C1 branch.

CELLO-seq (round 7, checked with corrections). Defining author manuscript PMC7614850 is now fetched via PMC Cloud. Read Methods 'CELLO-seq' lines176-181, ONT sequencing line207, Fig.1A (p35) and Table S5. Corrected repository transcription errors (oligo(dT) is T25VN not T30VN; bottom-splint tail is T15 not T14) and added RT/ExoI/ligation/PCR/ONT kit conditions. PCR primer identity is now inferred. The splint-to-cDNA nick template is undescribed and shown as a model interpretation. Deferred: image-only SI PDF, ONT LSK109/110 guides, 2025 protocol. Checks: suite 5/5, pairing 0, chemistry 2/0/6/0, source guard 1 exact checked/0 failed.

cfMeDIP-seq (round 7, deferred, no original). The defining Nature Protocols Methods are paywalled; Europe PMC has no full text, and the manual URL is recorded. Only the published Reporting Summary was read. Its 'Antibodies used' section names the Diagenode MagMeDIP 5-mC mAb 33D3 (C15200081, 0.16 ug/reaction), which is now on the graph. The page's TruSeq adapter/index/read shell is now labelled an INFERRED platform shell, and the adapter input is marked inferred. Nano-MeDIP draft oligos are not transferred. Deferred: defining Methods, MagMeDIP manual, Shen 2018 predecessor. Checks: pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/8/0, source guard 0 exact checked.

**CH-ATAC-seq** — deferred (defining article unread). The Dev Cell STAR Methods are open archive since 2025-03-25 according to Crossref, but the fetcher gets a Cloudflare 403; the manual URL is recorded. Every modelled base was re-verified against the published Supplementary Table 1 (scg-mirrored publisher workbook). The table's Block_tail_primer_oligo is now an exact-state graph input that blocks free HY duplexes before pooling. The unpublished gap fill moved after pooling into the final PCR wells, as inferred, following the order CH-RNA-seq publishes. The waste fate no longer implies physical removal. Deferred: STAR Methods conditions, fill/ligation enzyme, read lengths, Research Square record identity.

**ChAIR** — checked with corrections. Read the published JATS Methods (lines 179, 187), the newly fetched Springer step-by-step Supplementary Information and the CG000338 Rev G sequence appendix. The shared multimodal recipe is replaced by a protocol-owned graph. Its chemistry runs: AluI(+HpyCH4V) digestion, Klenow dA-tailing, then ligation of the printed biotinylated bridge linker (computed 3'-T/3'-T, 18 bp). Next come Multiome tagmentation/GEMs as a warned boundary and M-280 capture separating the DNA from the cDNA, then PCR/SPRI steps and a warned GEX boundary. There are two physical libraries (DNA, RNA); ATAC vs PET is computational. Three Methods-vs-SI condition discrepancies are on the page. Deferred: gated NIHMS supplements, kit revision/oligos/read lengths, supernatant DNA fate, ChIATAC predecessor.

| [CRISPR-sciATAC](crispr-sciatac__10.1038+s41587-021-00902-x/) | `checked_with_corrections` | Supplementary Fig. 1d,e read lengths are graphical (no text in the extraction) and were not re-inspected; U6 outer PCR1 primer's one-base mismatch to CROPseq-Guide-Puro (table typo vs vector variant) still unresolved; spike-in purpose/ratio/step unknown (two-reader missing-information gate not run); CROP-seq, sci-ATAC and Perturb-ATAC predecessors not re-read; actual acquired cycle counts per library |
| [CRISPR-UMI (Schmierer)](crispr-umi-schmierer__10.15252+msb.20177834/) | `checked_with_corrections` | Addgene #196709 verified full map (login-gated), reconstruction-vs-deposit identity unchecked; library-construction front end (array oligo annealing, second-strand synthesis, Gibson, electroporation, packaging, transduction, puromycin selection) not drawn; bases of the underlined outer primers that double-strand the 119-bp RSL oligo; identity of the off-size PCR3 species removed on the gel; Wang et al. 2014 predecessor guide library. |

| [CUT&Tag](cut-tag__10.1038+s41467-019-09982-5/) | `checked_with_corrections` | Exact universal i5 oligo and Nextera vs Buenrostro family choice; Selected i7 members/sample mapping and acquired bulk index cycles; E. coli carry-over calibration branch; Single-cell ICELL8 72x72 (25x8x8x25) branch not drawn; protocols.io z6hf9b6 v2 and bcuhiwt6 v3 revisions; High-throughput 96-well variant |
| [CUTAC](cutac__10.7554+eLife.63274/) | `checked_with_corrections` | Commercial Epicypher pAG-Tn5 loaded adapter identity; Selected i5/i7 members, universal-i5 branch and acquired HiSeq2500 cycles; Low-salt dilution/removal/post-wash/add-back variants not drawn separately; Nuclei-prep bcuhiwt6 and Kaya-Okur2020 CUT&Tag-direct predecessor; supp1 dataset workbook extraction-protocol names |
| [DART-seq](dart-seq__10.1038+s41592-019-0570-0/) | `checked_with_corrections` | NEBNext Ultra II Directional (E7760) and Single Cell/Low Input (E6420) manuals (fragmentation, adaptor/USER geometry, PCR primers); NEBNext Multiplex Oligos set and index-read cycles (GEO GSE125780 run metadata not inspected); PacBio Iso-Seq long-read branch; low-input branch and in vitro deamination assay not drawn |
| [DBiT-seq](dbit-seq__10.1016+j.cell.2020.10.026/) | `checked_with_corrections` | Cell 2020 Tables S2/S3 workbooks (NIHMS1645400-supplement-10.xlsx etc.; PMC download gate, manual URLs in MANIFEST.tsv): 2020-vs-2021 oligo identity and 2020 index primers; ADT branch library not drawn; index-read cycles not stated (8/8 kept from oligo index lengths); bioRxiv 788992v2 superseded, not re-read |

**change-seq__10.1038+s41587-020-0555-7 — checked with corrections.** Read the published PMC7652380 JATS CHANGE-seq Methods paragraph (line 172) and the Supplementary Protocol MOESM1 text (reagent setup lines 100-130, procedure steps 1-30 lines 155-500, sequencing step 40 line 589). The two custom transposon oligos are unchanged and verbatim. Added the proteinase K deproteinization of the Cas9 RNP (step 17) ahead of dA tailing, and replaced generic reagent labels with the source reagents: Kapa HiFi HotStart Uracil+ Ready Mix with Taq DNA ligase, a combined USER/T4 PNK reaction, T4 DNA ligase at 5 ng/ul for 16 h, the ExoI/lambda/Plasmid-Safe cocktail, Kapa A-tailing enzyme and Kapa DNA ligase (the hairpin is NEB's, the tailing and ligation enzymes are Kapa), and the USER supplied with the NEBNext Dual Index Primers Set 1 kit. Sequencing now distinguishes the protocol's MiSeq 600-cycle v3 run (150 bp PE, 8 bp dual index) from the article's MiSeq-or-NextSeq-550 statement. Deferred: a version-matched 2020 E7600S manual, the Kapa HTP PCR-free kit manual, the Piccelli Tn5 purification method, the selected index pair, and the qPCR/pooling steps. Suite 2 checks pass; pairing 0 problems; chemistry 0/0/13/0; source guard 0 checked, 5 symbolic inputs.

**char-seq__10.7554+eLife.27024 — checked with corrections.** Read the published eLife v2 JATS bridge-design Results paragraph (lines 157-161) and the article's own extended protocol, steps 1-27 (lines 292-900). The 2018 bridge strands and the single-end 152-base readout are confirmed unchanged. Three corrections came out of the extended protocol: the crosslink reversal with SDS/NaCl/proteinase K at 70 C overnight plus ethanol precipitation (steps 16-17) was missing between second-strand synthesis and Covaris shearing; the library amplification is two rounds, eight on-bead cycles (step 23) followed by an off-bead PCR of a qPCR-determined cycle number (steps 24-25), not one; and the reagents are now named from the source (T4 trKQ ligase in 25% PEG, E. coli Pol I with limiting RNase H, Dynabeads MyOne Streptavidin T1, NEBNext End Prep, Ultra II Ligation Master Mix, High-Fidelity master mix). The step-24 note that the adaptor is a 65-nt hairpin is recorded without asserting its bases. Deferred: the kit revision and index member, the desthiobiotin-TEG bond in the figure image, the Hi-C/mock branch, and the bead-wash and AMPure selections. Suite 5 checks pass; pairing 0 problems; chemistry 2/6/17/0; source guard 2 checked, 2 failed on a pre-existing locator exclusion.

**ChIL-seq** (deferred). The defining Nature Protocols article and the Harada 2019 predecessor are paywalled and not in PMC. Manual URL: doi.org/10.1038/s41596-020-0375-8. Only the publisher Supplementary Information was read: Tables 2–4, with Table 4 checked on the rendered p6, and the Figs 1–7 captions. The probe, MEDS-B, Read2+N11, Ad1_noMX and Ad2_index sequences and their modifications were verified. An unsourced extension step and its invented oligo were removed; the interval from integration to T7 is now a warned MAGIC boundary. The transposome and antibody inputs are now source-scoped. The final insert lost a stray 'ChIL-probe end' placeholder and gained the N11 priming site. The mtChIL v3 probes and Ad1_mt are listed but not drawn. The page caveat records the source scope and two supplement caption slips. Checks: suite, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/1/6/0, source guard 3 exact checked/0 failed.

**ChRO-seq** (checked_with_corrections). Re-read the PMC6204104 Methods (lines 148–158), the Supplementary Table 1 UMI column and Mahat 2016 procedure steps 55–82. In Mahat 2016, each enrichment repeats steps 23–48, which include the Trizol release, so the graph's three capture/release cycles are supported. The reaction order otherwise agreed. Run-on conditions (300 mM KCl, 1% Sarkosyl, 37 °C 5 min) and hydrolysis conditions (65 °C 40 s, then 0.2 N NaOH on ice 8 min) were completed from Methods line 154. Still deferred: study adapter/primer variants, 5′ ligase, PCR cycles, read lengths, the Wuarin 1994 PDF (PMC gate), GEO/pipeline settings and the supplementary figures. Checks: suite 7/7, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/19/0, source guard 0 exact checked with 5 role-level inputs.

**cirseq__10.1038+nprot.2014.118** — checked_with_corrections. Read PMC4418788 author-manuscript JATS (Overview, Experimental design line46, Reagents, Procedure Steps1–56) and spot-checked the eScholarship author PDF for Steps18/25/31/43/47. Step18 phosphorylation and circularization are one PNK + T4 RNA ligase 1 mix; RT adds RNase H; an unsupported random second-strand primer was replaced by the NEBNext E6111 module (priming unstated). Added the missing ligated-library 450–600-nt urea-PAGE excision, 15-cycle Phusion PCR with the unprinted FC-121-4001 cocktail, and 7.5% PAGE purification. Read layout is now the recommended 300-nt read (single read derived). Deferred: FC-121-4001 kit original (adapter/cocktail sequences; drawn adapters total fewer than the stated 135 bases), E6111 original, Acevedo2014 Nature SI, index member/cycles. Suite, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/13/0, source guard 0 exact/2 symbolic, Markdown and review schema pass.

**cite-seq__10.1038+nmeth.4380** — checked_with_corrections. Read published PMC5669064 Online Methods (conjugation, oligo lists, staining, Drop-seq/10x sections) and the MOESM3 author protocol (2017-07-11) staining, SPRI separation, ADT PCR, sequencing and oligo pages. The graph previously split cDNA from ADTs before RT; it now runs RT in GEMs, 10-cycle cDNA amplification, 0.6X SPRI split and two 2X SPRI ADT cleanups before KAPA PE1.0/RPIx PCR. The ADT library was a dA-ligated TruSeq DNA shell with a forward handle and 8-nt i7; it is now assembled from the printed v2_BC, PE1.0 P5 and RPI1 oligos (6-nt RPI index, inferred poly(dT) length). Unsourced read cycles were removed; only TruSeq Read1 is declared on ADTs. Deferred: 10x CG00052 vendor guide, ADT fate in cDNA PCR, small-RNA-arm sequencing primers/cycles, Drop-seq/v1 branches. Suite, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/11/0, source guard 0 exact/4 symbolic, Markdown and review schema pass.

CoBATCH — deferred (final article paywalled). Read original publisher Table S1 (mmc2 sheets Mosaic-A&B, PA-Tn5_barcodes, Nextera_index, raw cells), Tables S2–S4 headers, and author preprint 590661v1 Methods 340–375/423–452/470–499 as a labelled fallback. Adapters and PCR arms agree with Table S1. Graph now places gap fill in the in-well KAPA PCR after redistribution, adds sort of 20–25 cells/well, proteinase K lysis, 10–18 cycles and AMPure 200–1000 bp selection, all marked preprint-scoped. Page shows the published Table S1 one-row offset of the equivalence notes (C4–C6 vs B3–B5). Deferred: final STAR Methods, sequencing primers/cycle spans, Tn5 predecessors, unmodelled branches.

CODEC — checked with corrections. Re-read final PMC10181940 JATS Results104–112 and Methods202–220 plus Supplementary Table 5 lines484–500; set-1 adapter/bridge oligos still equal source (selftest 4/4). The graph lacked source-stated steps: added Covaris shearing to 150 bp, 1.8x pre-ligation AMPure, 0.75x post-phi29 AMPure and two 0.65x post-PCR AMPure cleanups with discarded outputs, and carried adapter annealing, 500 nM adapter, 1 h ligation, phi29 30 C 20 min and KAPA 2 min extension into reagent labels. PCR cycle number remains unassigned. Deferred: KAPA manual, xGen primer strands, NNNA/NNNT variants, optional repair/enrichment branches, CODECsuite scope.

cotech__10.1038+s41592-021-01129-z — checked_with_corrections. Read the published Supplementary Table 1 (MOESM3: Custom_primers 1-11, PA-Tn5_barcodes 12-63, barcoded_oligo_dT 64-162, index sheets 163-) verbatim; the defining article text stays paywalled, so the bioRxiv preprint (Results 150-172, Fig.1 legend 347-358) supplies step order provisionally, labelled as such on the page. Corrected the second combinatorial coordinate from a redistribution-time sample index to the secondary well's library-PCR cell index, split first-round chromatin identity into T5 and T7 barcode features beside the RT barcode and 8-nt UMI, and added preamplification, AMPure purification, the half split, cDNA tagmentation and the two-round DNA PCR. Removed the fabricated physically-discarded collision waste; preamp primers and cDNA Tn5 loading are INFERRED. Suite 12/12 (oligo transcription), page builds, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/14/0, guard 0 exact/9 unrepresented, Markdown built, review appended.

crispr-circle-seq__10.1038+nmeth.4278 — checked_with_corrections. Re-read the defining Methods (PMC5924695 lines 115-116), the Fig.1 legend (450-451) and the Supplementary Protocol (MOESM84 141-200, 296-320). The graph was missing the Lambda Exonuclease/Exonuclease I selection that precedes USER/T4 PNK; it and its digested free-end waste are now represented, and the opening step states the 4-nt palindromic overhangs. Declared the actual reagents and conditions (T4 ligase 5 ng/ul 16 C 16 h, Plasmid-Safe ATP-dependent DNase, 90 nM SpCas9/gRNA on 250 ng, NEBNext i5/i7 with KAPA HiFi HotStart 22 cycles) and made oSQT1288 an exact input state, so the source guard now checks it. Sequencing plan carries 150-bp paired-end MiSeq reads with index lengths left unstated; the E7601A 64 vs 65-nt published discrepancy notice is unchanged. Suite 1/1, page builds, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/13/0, guard 1 exact/3 unrepresented, Markdown built, review appended.

**CRISPR-MIP** (`crispr-mip__10.1101+2024.03.28.587082`, wip, bioRxiv v1). Re-read the preprint design overview (lines 118-136), probe design and the four reaction steps (458-493), the comparator PCR and read extraction (494-523), and Table S2. Sequences, 5'-phosphate, N13 UMI, nine 8-nt i7 probes, the 112-nt gap and the 60/15 read profile agree with the model. Corrected missing reaction detail across the module's PROTOCOL, the page and the note: 5 U Ampligase in a 10 ul mix into 20 ul, one 100 ul KAPA HiFi HotStart ReadyMix PCR of the whole 31 ul reaction with its cycling (n by qPCR), and a post-PCR AMPure XP cleanup with Qubit/Bioanalyzer QC; the old blanket 'no bead cleanup' line is now scoped to the enzymatic selection. The 134-nt Methods text vs 126-nt Table S2 discrepancy notice stays. Deferred: a published version was not searched, the MIP predecessor papers were not re-read, and the AMPure ratio and index-read cycles are not stated in what was read. No protocol_graph declaration exists, so check_chemistry does not apply to this protocol.

**CRISPR-sciATAC** (`crispr-sciatac__10.1038+s41587-021-00902-x`, published). Re-read the author manuscript PMC8516442 (line 134 MEDS A/B annealing and TnY assembly; 146-158 fixation through PCR2, cleanups and MiSeq; 160 K562 ATAC on HiSeq 2500 and gRNA on MiSeq), the Supp. Fig. 1 legend in MOESM1 (lines 20-60) and Supplementary Table 1, with the MOESM4 oligo workbook re-scraped. Reaction order, oligo identities, PCR cycling and cleanups match the model. One correction: the graph and note said nuclei were redistributed at 15-20 per well, while the Methods specify 2 ul at 10 nuclei/ul and Supp. Table 1 gives 2x10^1 per well, so that is now ~20; the RT Stop-and-Stain step and the per-step plate counts were added, and the spike-in 'never mentioned' sentence is now stated as a search result, not an absence claim. Deferred: the graphical Supp. Fig. 1d,e read lengths, the U6 outer primer's one-base mismatch to the CROPseq vector, and the spike-ins' purpose. An older checklist row still labels this paper 'Pierce et al. Nat Methods'; the correct citation is Liscovitch-Brauer et al., Nat Biotechnol 39:1270-1277 (2021).

CRISPR-UMI (Schmierer) — capped pass on the published MSB 13:945 JATS (PMC5658704), Materials and Methods lines 169–245: AU-flip/3xBsmBI vector edit, the printed full insert, the three nested PCRs and the primer table. Every module primer was re-derived and matched the printed table base-for-base, as were 288 bp and the HiSeq4000 20+6+6 layout with i7 reading the RSL. One real gap was fixed: the graph stopped at PCR3 and never represented the Methods' gel purification, so a CLEANUP step with an excised-band selection input, a declared off-size waste product and an explicit discard now sits between PCR3 and the library. The paper's own naming inconsistency (Methods CRISPRSeq vs primer table CRIPSRSEQ for one sequence) is now stated on the public page and in the note. Deferred: the login-gated Addgene #196709 map, the whole cloning/packaging/transduction front end, the unlisted outer primers of the 119-bp RSL oligo, and the identity of the gel-excluded species. Suite 206 checks, pairing 0 problems, chemistry 6 steps / 9 unresolved, source guard 0 exact checked with 6 symbolic inputs.

CROP-seq — capped pass on the published Supplementary Protocol (NIHMS71051): §10.3 SMART PCR, §11.1–11.2 tagmentation/enrichment, §12.3 run setup and the oligonucleotide order table. Two model-versus-source mismatches were corrected. The bead-cDNA amplification had a fabricated forward/reverse primer pair; the master mix prints one oligo only, so it is now a single symmetric species in 2× KAPA HiFi HotStart ReadyMix with the 4+10 cycling named. The Nextera XT neutralization that stops tagmentation was missing entirely and is now a quench step of its own between tagmentation and the New-P5-SMART/N70X enrichment PCR. Three printed oligos (TSO, SMART PCR primer, New-P5-SMART hybrid) now carry exact states, so the transcription guard checks 3 oligos where it previously checked none; their rGrGrG and *A*C modifications are recorded in comments and the note. Read layout 20/8/64 reconfirmed. The bead-barcode absence claim remains blocked and the prepared bead batch stays a family. Suite 2 checks, pairing 0 problems, chemistry 8 steps / 10 unresolved, source guard 3 exact checked and 3 symbolic inputs.

CG000735/CG000733, the in-situ and T7-IVT oligos and the analysis repository remain deferred.

Digenome-seq (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-09): the defining 2015 Nature Methods Methods remains paywalled and unread, so the protocol stays blocked. Read within the cap: the defining supplement's figure legends and Supplementary Note 1 (lines 7250-7265, "Illumina HiSeq X 10"), the 2016 Genome Research successor Methods lines 132/136 and Discussion line 114, and Illumina Adapter Sequences v22. One correction: the page declared 150/6/150 acquired cycles with no source behind them, so READ_LENGTHS is now None and the sequencing intro attributes the instrument to the two passages actually read. The inferred TruSeq shell with a 6-nt i7 was checked against the Illumina adapter document and left inferred; the note's stale "unsubstantiated" list became a model-choices section. Deferred: the defining Methods, the 2021 Nature Protocols article, which TruSeq kit was used, and the acquired cycle counts.

Digital Telomere Measurement (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-09): read the published Methods lines 154-160, Supplementary Data 3 in full and Fig. 1a in the rendered PDF. Two repository errors corrected. The capture-oligo partition was modelled as 22+25+6 nt with a CTAACC arm; comparing the printed canonical and TSQ-variant rows gives 22+24+7 with CCTAACC, and the selftest now derives that partition from the table instead of restating constants. The capture panel also claimed T4 ligase seals the capture 5'-phosphate at the native C-rich terminus, which the oligo's polarity forbids; the panel now shows capture oligo plus seqTether at the telomere end and records that the Methods do not identify the sealed nick, with the graph geometry declared unavailable. Source-stated ligase and polymerase conditions and the ProNex 1.6x cleanup were added to the graph. Deferred: the unreachable TelometerLibraryPrep.docx, the proprietary AMII/NA motor adapters, and the multiplex barcode branch.

DiMeLo-seq (checked_with_corrections). Read Methods in the published PMC9189060 JATS (DiMeLo-seq, Nanopore library prep, PacBio) and the author protocols.io v2 materials list and steps 10-14. The graph now runs in source order: digitonin permeabilization, antibody and pA-Hia5 binding with wash waste, SAM activation (v1/v2 conditions), Monarch extraction, NEBNext end prep, SRE pelleting, Quick Ligation adapter ligation, LFB washes and a v9.4 MinION run. The drawn path is the LSK110 method (2). A graph highlight names the LSK109+NBD104/114 barcoded method (1) and the PacBio branch, which are not drawn. Deferred: the 2024 Nature Protocols full text, the ONT 2021 kit documents (end geometry, adapter and barcode bases) and the PacBio branch. Chemistry 0/0/9/0; source guard 0 exact checked.

Direct-seq (checked_with_corrections). Read the published PMC7278172 Methods for the 10x 3-prime and C1 platforms, cross-checked against the PDF. Also read Table S3d rows 45-48 and the Table S2 Tail-8A8G row. The graph now includes the CG000184 pre-amplification (1st PCR), the two 40ul aliquots, the 0.6x / 0.6-1.2x size selection into the mRNA and gRNA library I fractions, and the 1.2x AMPure gRNA library II, declared as an unresolved terminal. It adds the stated Q5 2nd PCR (8x, 14 cycles) and 3rd PCR (5x, 5 cycles), each with a 0.7-1.0x bead selection. Unsourced 28/90 acquired read lengths were removed. Deferred: the Protocol Exchange pex-952 protocol, CG000184 cDNA primers, the C1/5-prime branches and the sequencing run lengths. Chemistry 2/0/13/0; source guard 3 exact checked, 0 failed.

DISCOVER-Seq (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-09, capped): read the Science author manuscript PMC6589096 (no library-prep text outside the gated supplement), the authors' Nature Protocols PMC7305837 and, newly, their protocols.io V.2 bench version (ID 69486). No oligo change — the scraper returns nothing anywhere in the bundle, and the only published bases remain the TruSeq ChIP six-base index table. Added the actual reaction conditions (1% formaldehyde/glycine, Covaris S2, NB100-142 or ab208020 on Protein A Dynabeads, 65 °C reversal, RNase A/proteinase K, MinElute, 0.8× AMPure on the pool, 300–500 bp library, 100-bp PE at 30–40 M pairs). The page now shows a published discrepancy inside the same detailed protocol: ~150–500 bp in the sonication overview versus a 200–500 bp median in Anticipated results, with the shearing window left unassigned. Deferred: the gated Science supplement and tables, per-library index selection and the Covaris settings; absence of adapter oligos is not asserted.

DNBSEQ library circularization (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-09, capped): read the applicable vendor revision, MGIEasy Dual Barcode Circularization Kit User Manual 7.0 (SOP-013-B02-185, kit V1.0, Sep. 2024), end to end. The manual's own endpoint is the bead-cleaned, Qubit-ssDNA-QC'd ssCir library (≥80 fmol); it contains no DNB, rolling-circle, polymerase or sequencing-primer text, so the missing cleanup step and purified-circle product were added and the nanoball steps are now flagged on the page as platform architecture rather than kit chemistry. The unsupported 'linear-DNA exonuclease' became the kit's undisclosed Digestion Enzyme with its real volumes and 37 °C 30 min conditions, the denaturation is now 1 pmol in 48 µL TE at 95 °C for 3 min, and the complementary strand's fate changed from 'discarded waste' to an unresolved terminal, because the manual never says which strand is circularized. Dual-barcode-only input (1 pmol, 0.5 pmol minimum) is now stated on the page. Deferred: the named sequencing-primer reference guide, a DNB-preparation source, the 503-blocked adapter guide and the Fast FS upstream manual.

DR-seq (dr-seq__10.1038+nbt.3129) — checked_with_corrections. Read PMC4374170 Online Methods (PDF.txt 330–470) and the main-text workflow (68–92), publisher Supplementary Fig.3/Note (268, 1404) and the MALBAC article (line40). Ad-2 now ends in a named GGG/TTT triplet after N5; the page shows the published discrepancy between the article ('8 random nt') and the supplement ('3′ end GGG or TTT'). The biotin-P3 PCR is now a single-primer P3/P3 amplicon, and the page highlights that P3 and P2 differ at positions 6–10. Added named polymerases and cycle numbers, barcode pooling before IVT, RT after aRNA ligation, and 100+100 transcriptome reads. Deferred: CEL-Seq original, MALBAC supplement, NEBNext guide, gDNA read length. Suite 10/10, pairing 0, chemistry 0/1/19/0, source guard 5 exact checked/0 failed.

DRIP-seq (drip-seq__10.1016+j.molcel.2012.01.017) — checked_with_corrections. Read PMC3319272 Results 74–77 and Experimental Procedures line113, plus the authors' 2019 Nat Protoc DRIP-seq library branch (PMC6615061 130–140, 420–446, 600–621, 1259). Removed the unsupported Prep2Seq-adapter and single-end claims; the 2012 text says only 'Illumina barcoded adaptors' on GAIIx/HiSeq. Added the source size-selection step with its waste. End repair, dA-tailing and PCR are now INFERRED from the 2019 protocol. Highlights cover the RNase H/input control libraries and the 2012-vs-2019 library difference. Deferred: gated 2012 supplements, MeDIP predecessor. Pairing 0, chemistry 0/5/10/0, source guard 0 exact inputs.

DRIPc-seq (round 09): read the defining Mol Cell author manuscript PMC4955522 (Results l61, Experimental Procedures l137) and the 2019 Nat Protoc PMC6615061 (overview l403–446, reagents l528–621, steps 26–31 and 39–58, Table 1). Corrections: the invented second-strand primer oligo is now the protocol's RNase H/E. coli ligase/Pol I dUTP nick translation. Added end repair, dA-tailing and two-step AMPure size selection, with named enzymes. P5/P7 PCR primers now have exact states. The unsupported single-end read claim was removed. The page shows the article's 4 U/35 min DNase I against the protocol's 6 U/45 min. Deferred: gated 2016 supplement, Illumina adapter document (selected TruSeq index), GEO read length, Ginno predecessor. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 0/9/14/0; source guard 2 exact checked, 0 failed.

Drop-BS (round 09): read published PMC10400590 Methods l189–219 and MOESM1 Supplementary Fig. 1 (l29–130); the preprint was not used. The graph now follows the Methods order: complement annealing, fusion, End-It, UV + Fast-Link ligation, recovery, droplet MethylEdge conversion, Klenow random priming, the previously missing 5-cycle P5/P7-transposon pre-PCR, N5XX/Ad2.XX index PCR, and SPRI with waste fates. The final library now carries the converted bead ligation-arm complement, H15 barcode and converted bead PCR-site complement. These are derived by computation, match Fig. S1, and construction checks that the P7 BS primer lands on the converted site. Reads are NovaSeq 150PE. The page shows the D15-vs-V15 barcode-symbol discrepancy. Deferred: ChemGenes lot spec, i5/i7 members and lengths, remaining datasets, MethylEdge manual. Suite 4/4, page/pairing pass; chemistry 1/0/15/0; source guard 3 exact checked, 0 failed.

Drop-seq (deferred). Read the published Cell PMC4481139 JATS: bead oligo parts (line 100), Experimental Procedures (line 190) and Fig. 2 legends (lines 422–426). Also read the inventor-lab v3.1 primer table, RT/ExoI/PCR/Nextera and sequencing passages, and Seq-Well Supp Table 1. All six modelled oligo states match the printed bases, and the source guard checked 6 exact states with 0 failures. The graph now names the source reagents and conditions. It adds the proteinase K cross-link reversal and isopropanol extraction, waste for the lambda-exonuclease-digested adapter, bead discard after the on-bead PCR, and MinElute/BluePippin 250–1200 bp size selection. The PCR finish is now local instead of the shared _finish_pcr. Deferred: the TruSeq pooling guide, index-read acquisition, and the EcoRV pilot and negative-control branches.

EMP ITS is `checked_with_corrections`: re-fetched EMP ITS web page, 2018 protocols.io PDF and November 2016 workbook inspected; Platinum Hot Start mix and 35-cycle program, triplicate PCR, 240 ng equal pooling and UltraClean clean-up added; workbook sheet citations corrected; page now shows the published amplicon size discrepancy (~250–600 vs ~230 bp) beside the 10-N/12-nt barcode one. EasySci (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-09): read the published Nature Genetics Methods, which defer to Supplementary Protocol 1 (MOESM1 text 21-515), plus MOESM3 Tables 16-19. The role-token final library carried a spurious 8-nt sample index and was replaced with a source-sequence construct. easySHARE-seq (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-09): read the published eLife VOR (PMC13384510) Key resources table, Methods 404-470 and Appendix 1, plus Supplementary file 1 (md5-identical to bioRxiv media-6). Round-1 (8-nt) and round-2 (TCT/AGA) ligations are now Scene-checked, and the reversal, streptavidin split, template switch, cDNA PCR, Tn5-A-only tagmentation and both final PCRs were added. The RNA library gains the UMI and A26, the TCT linker is literal, and the 150/17/8/150 read lengths are shown. FISSEQ (fisseq__10.1126+science.1250212) — checked with corrections.
