Deferred FS-LA/FS-UMI graphs, commercial ME geometry, enrichment cycles and Research Square protocol.

G&T-seq — deferred (partial). The defining 2015 Online Methods PDF returned HTTP 403 and has no open Europe PMC copy, so its oligo values rest on the earlier web reading. Read the 2015 figure supplement (legends only) and the 2016 author protocol oligo list, separation, on-bead RT, ISPCR WTA and AMPure/WGA steps. Corrected the template-switch panel: the shared generic scene drew a symbolic handle, dT14 and rGrGrG, whereas both sources print rGrG+G; a local scene now draws the printed capture oligo and 2015 TSO with inferred untemplated C. 2016-only details are not imported. Deferred 2015 Methods re-read, Nextera XT indexes, WGA kit primers and Smart-seq2 predecessor.

GAGE-seq (gage-seq__10.1101+2023.07.20.549578) — checked_with_corrections. Read published PMC11323187 Methods 141–171/177, Supplementary Methods MOESM1 91–131 and Fig. S1 caption, and Supplementary Table 1a–1i A1 rows. Corrected order (RT first; MseI/CviQI, ligation without fill-in, DdeI), poly(G) TdT tail, /iBiodT/ base in the RT primers, Hi-C AD-1 A1 barcode/3' GA, and added reverse crosslinking, Nextera-P5-AD ligation and AD-1/AD-2 duplex scenes. Final constructs follow the Methods read structure (DNA read 2 BC2-joint-BC1; RNA read 1 UMI-BC2-linker-BC1). Open: DNA read-1 12-nt trim vs Tn5 end, Hi-C-AD1-Block sequence. Suite 7/7, pairing 0, chemistry 9/0/17/0, guard 0 exact/13 symbolic.

GAM (gam__10.1038+nature21411) — checked_with_corrections. Sources fetched this pass (PMC Cloud author manuscript, NIHMS supplements, Springer ESM). Read PMC5366070 Methods 171–201, Supplementary Guide, and Supplementary Table 1 kit/index columns. Split WGA4 into proteinase K lysis and a warned proprietary library-prep boundary; Read 1 corrected from an unsupported 130 to the printed single-end 100 bp, with dark-cycle accounting left open; index lengths now cite Table 1 (8+8 for NP/negative libraries, LT 6-nt positive control). The page shows the positive-control discrepancy (E14 mESC vs human gDNA). Deferred: WGA4 bulletin, Baslan 2012, GEO GSE64881. Suite 2/2, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/8/0, guard 0 exact/2 symbolic.

GenIE (2026-10-17, round 10): read PMC7736817 JATS Methods lines 53-130 and Supplementary Table S3/S4 in the author workbook. Table S3 prints one tail per orientation: the forward tail is exactly TruSeq Read 1, while the reverse tail TCGGCATTCCTGCTGAACCGCTCTTCCGATCT is the Illumina PE/Genome-Analyzer arm belonging to the cited ref-8 barcoding method, not TruSeq Read 2. The page previously drew a generic dual-index TruSeq ligation library with a dA junction an amplicon never has; the library is now built explicitly from both exact tails plus the MUL1_rs6700034 primer pair, with the flow-cell/index shell inferred and index cycles no longer claimed as acquired. The graph's unsourced outer+nested PCR pair was replaced by the source's PCR1/PCR2 route and the matched genomic-DNA branch was added. Ref 8 barcoding primers, ref 7 donor-orientation predecessor and the SDF4 spliced amplicon remain deferred; the ref-8 absence stays blocked.

GenIE-ATAC (2026-10-17, round 10): read PMC10287956 JATS lines 63-166 and Supplementary Tables 1-2 in the author workbook. The nested forward oligos all carry the tail TCGGCATTCCTGCTGAACCGCTCTTCCGATCT, the Illumina PE/Genome-Analyzer arm of the cited ref-31 barcoding method, while the TruSeq Read 1 tail appears only on the reverse oligos of the matched gDNA branch; the drawn TruSeq amplicon shell therefore misrepresented the amplicon's P5-side arm. The library now carries the exact tail next to the nested locus primer, keeps the MEDS-A/Tn5A end, and marks only the flow-cell/index shell inferred, with Read 1 and Read 2 declared as the tail and MEDS-A primers at 150 cycles and index cycles dropped from acquired reads. Linear-PCR cycling, streptavidin capture and nested-PCR conditions were added to the note. Ref 31 barcoding primers, ref 32 Tn5 purification, the reverse-direction primer sets and the P5-end assignment remain deferred.

GeoMx DSP (deferred, partial defining original). The Nature 2020 article is paywalled: only the abstract preview, MOESM1 legends and the MOESM2 Reporting Summary could be read. The authority for library prep, MAN-10153-01 (Apr 2022), was inspected at lines 555–660, 1100–1460 and 1599–1607, with Figure 2 checked as the rendered page; the lengths match the declaration. The graph now includes the dry-down/rehydration step (Table 5), the UDG pre-incubation from the Table 6 master-mix program, and 4 µL per-well pooling ahead of two 1.2× AMPure XP cleanups. Deferred: published Methods, preprint NGS Methods, master-mix composition, proprietary bases. Suite 2/2, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/6/0, source guard 0 exact.

GLORI (deferred, supplements only). Neither the 2023 Methods nor the 2024 Nature Protocols main text is available (publisher-only). From the 2024 MOESM3 XLSX, the Supplementary Table 1 oligos were re-verified verbatim; the 2023 MOESM1 Supp Figs 1/14 and the rendered MOESM2 Reporting Summary were also read. The adaptor sites, computed with lib/illumina, put the N10 UMI beside the Read 2 site, which corrects a repository model that had placed the UMI next to P5; the P5 side is marked inferred, and the final step is INFERRED. The instrument is now HiSeq X Ten + NovaSeq. Nitrite/deprotection conditions are carried claims only. Deferred: main texts, GEO, PCR primers/read layout, enzymes. Pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/9/0, source guard 0 exact, 5 symbolic.

- **GoT** (`got__10.1038+s41586-019-1367-0`) — checked with corrections. Read PMC6782071 JATS Methods line196 (linear GoT), 200–202 (circularization), 216, and Supplementary Table 5 rows1–74. Target PCR now uses source roles (10x SI-PCR forward, staggered locus-specific reverse), adds the SPRI primer-removal cleanup, and declares P5_generic (exact) and RPI-x as source-identified inputs. Read cycles distinguish 26 (3' v2/5') from 28 (3' v3). Page shows the circGoT PCR#2 handle swap between Methods and Table 5 and the missing Rv#1 phosphorothioate. Deferred: 10x guides (SI-PCR sequence), ED Fig1 primer positions, circGoT branch, target-PCR polymerase, refs44/45.

- **GoT-ChA** (`got-cha__10.1038+s41586-024-07388-y`) — checked with corrections. Fetched and read the published Nature 2024 article (PMC11139586 JATS lines205–229, 243–245) and Supplementary Table 1; the directory's bioRxiv preprint is superseded and the old 'Namikawa' citation was a repository error. The graph now has both in-droplet GoT-ChA primers, the 5/45.5 µL split, 15-cycle biotinylated hemi-nested PCR, 1.2X SPRI, M-280 pull-down and on-bead P5/RPI-X indexing (6–10 cycles), with Read1N 50/i7 8/i5 16/Read2N 50. Page shows the Rev-handle and P5 29-vs-22-nt discrepancies. Deferred: 10x CG000209 Rev F/CG000169 Rev D, PCR polymerase, multiplex/ASAP routes, SI MOESM1/2.

**GRID-seq** (`grid-seq__10.1038+nbt.3968`) — checked with corrections. Read the published PMC5953555 Methods lines127–144 and the Zhou2019 Nat Protoc lines104–122. The final library had two spurious 6-nt MmeI-boundary blocks and a canonical P7 string appended after the Y arm (210 bp). P5/barcode/R1 are now checked against printed Primer #1, and the P7 tail is derived from Primer #2, giving the published 194-bp band. The graph now follows the source order: AluI→PNK→RNA ligation→self-primed RT→wash→in-situ DNA ligation→PK→streptavidin→NaOH release→Klenow exo−→MmeI→85-bp gel→SAP→Y ligation→nick seal→165–185-bp gel→16-cycle PCR→194-bp gel. The MmeI end is now a 3′ overhang. Deferred: the barcode index-read recipe, exact tag lengths and PCR binding geometry.

**GRO-cap** (`gro-cap__10.1038+ng.3142`) — checked with minor corrections. Re-read the Core2014 Methods (JATS534–549) and the Kruesi2013 original Methods pp21–23 (lines1080–1211). The TAP+ order is confirmed: RA3, second BrU capture, Terminator, Antarctic phosphatase, TAP, RA5, third capture, then Core2008 RT/PCR/PAGE. The labels now give the source run-on mix (Br-UTP/ATP/GTP, limiting CTP, 1% sarkosyl, 5 min 30 °C) and the Santa Cruz anti-deoxyBrU beads. They also state that GRO-cap RNA is not base-hydrolysed. Deferred: the historical primer/index pair, the complete RA3/RA5 strands, the undrawn no-TAP branch and acquired cycles.

GRO-seq (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-11): re-read the recovered original 2008 SOM (overview 381–410, Methods 629–714, read processing 715–746) and the defining article JATS 28–32 against the local declaration. The declared route — two DNase treatments, three anti-BrU capture/elution/extraction rounds, 5′-then-3′ adapter order, two 10 µL RT reactions, 15-cycle Phusion PCR, >90 nt PAGE — holds. Corrections: run-on NTP concentrations and buffer/time conditions now come from the Methods text, and a graph-owned annotation plus the final caption record the SOM-reported ~100-base average library (~190 base minus ~90 base adapters) and the 1×10⁹ complexity estimate, explicitly as a bound on, not an identification of, the unassigned historical adapter bases. The overview 1 µM versus Methods 2 µM CTP is the same value across the equal-volume mix and is not displayed as a published conflict. Deferred: the historical Illumina Small RNA Isolation Kit guide, SOM references 21/39, GSE13518 child metadata, remaining SOM figures. Suite 7/7, page builds, pairing clean, chemistry 28 unresolved, source guard 0 exact/5 symbolic, Markdown and review schema checked.

GUIDE-seq (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-11): read the defining Online Methods paragraphs (JATS 125–131) and the corrected 25 June 2015 MOESM21 library protocol (33074–33262) plus its equipment list. The dsODN, half-functional Y adapter, nested STAT-PCR primers and 151|8|16|151 readout were already source-exact; the missing physical steps were the sample-handling ones. Added the DNAdvance SPRI genomic-DNA isolation and all four AMPure XP cleanups with their removed fractions, the 400 ng shearing input and the qPCR normalization to 1.2×10¹⁰ molecules at pooling; the note records that PCR 2 receives the PCR 1 sample with beads and that the validation primer-pool branch is not drawn. A published instrument conflict (Covaris S200 in the Online Methods versus Covaris S2 in the Supplementary protocol/equipment list) is now shown on the page with the model declaring only the ~500 bp target. Existing correction-notice and molecular-index-timing notices remain. Suite 28/28, page builds, pairing clean, chemistry 2/6/24/0, source guard 11 exact and 1 symbolic input, Markdown and review schema checked.

HDST (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-11, checked with corrections). Read published PMC6765407 Online Methods (Bead production121, decoding129, library/sequencing141, preprocessing145), MOESM1 Supp.Fig.1 caption and cited Jemt 2016 Results/Methods89. UMI 5 vs 6 and read-mask discrepancy display unchanged. Deferred: Ståhl 2016 supplement oligos, Salmén 2018, Bridge3/4 split, proprietary pools/decoders, index cycles. Human NET-seq (2026-10-17-breadth-first-11): re-read the author-hosted 2015 Extended Experimental Procedures (lines1076–1100), the inherited 2012 Table4.14.1 and PCR steps, and the 2016 successor Table1. Linker, oLSC007, oLSC006, step order and SE75/SE50 reads matched the model. The depletion step now carries its hybridization condition and C1-bead inputs. The supernatant is the retained product, and bead-bound abundant cDNA is separated waste. The RT-primer concentration change is recorded. A derived comparison is added: 2015 oLSC007 dropped the oNTI230 tract present in oLSC003, so oNTI230 alone has no site, and the oMHL 3' ends match oLSC006. The forward primer remains unassigned. **inDrop v1** (`indrop-v1__10.1016+j.cell.2015.04.044`) — deferred. Read Cell 2015 PMC4441768 JATS Results 67–69, Experimental Procedures 145–155 and Fig. 2 legend; the Extended Experimental Procedures (supplement-1) and supplements 2–12 remain PMC-gated on a 2026-10-17 re-fetch, so all v1 library oligos stay secondary-source inferred. Corrected the graph: empty/multiplet droplets are now an unresolved computational exclusion rather than a discard; the v1 PCR no longer claims a sample index; RT carries the published 50 °C 2 h / 70 °C 15 min and perfluorooctanol demulsification; fragmentation is inferred; the upstream 51-cycle Read 1 is no longer an acquired length. Deferred: supplement-1, CEL-Seq/MARS-seq predecessors, Fig. 2 image. Suite, page, pairing (0), chemistry 0/0/10/0, source guard 0 exact (all inferred), Markdown and review schema pass.

**inDrop v2** (`indrop-v2__10.1038+nprot.2016.154`) — deferred. Read Nature Protocols MOESM454 Supplementary Methods 1–2 and Table 1, the MOESM455 oligo table rows 1–36 and the MOESM460 README; the main protocol text remains paywalled (ICCB mirror 403). Added a graph-owned Exonuclease I bead clean-up with unresolved-fate shorter species and a SourceDiscrepancy for the 102 vs 101 nt QC band; made empty/multiplet droplets a computational (barcode-threshold) exclusion instead of a discard; removed the upstream-only 51-cycle Read 2 and 6-cycle index acquired lengths. Oligo bases and PE1 index orientation re-verified. Deferred: main text conditions/cycles, ExoI mechanism, pre-RT fragmentation. Suite, page, pairing (0), chemistry 0/0/9/0, source guard 4 exact/0 failed/1 symbolic, Markdown and review schema pass.

inDrops-2 (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-11, 2 originals). Read the published NAR article PMC11724362 Methods lines 105, 113, 117 and 121-125 and the corrected Supplementary Tables workbook (Table S1 lines 16-55, S2-S4 heads). The earlier model tagmented both branches with Tn5 and placed template switching after a shared RT; neither is in the source. The declaration now splits the IVT (v1 bead) and TS (v2 bead) implementations before encapsulation and follows each: second strand + T7 IVT + RNA fragmentation + PE2-N6 RT + PE1/PE2-Ind PCR, versus in-droplet TSO + cDNA PCR + NEB FS fragmentation + annealed modified-adapter ligation + indexed PCR. Read cycles are recorded per branch (IVT 54/8/>=35; TS 28/8/35-92) and the printed PE2 indexes are 6 nt against an 8-cycle i7 read. Two published discrepancies are shown on the page: the v1 full-length primer differs between Methods and Table S1 (5' stub, barcode windows, N6 vs N8, T19V), and the v2 stub ends ...CTCTTC in Methods but ...CCGATCT in Table S1 with T18 vs T19. Supplementary Protocols 1-4 and base-level placement remain deferred.

INSIGHT (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-11, 1 original). Read the published Science Advances article via PMC7880595: Table 1 reagents, Table 2 oligonucleotides, the stage-1 NASBA steps with Tables 3-4, stage-2 Step 3 with Table 5, and the Step 4 analysis. The model declared a separate NASBA DNA polymerase that the reagent table does not list, so second-strand synthesis now runs on ProtoScript II reverse transcriptase. The pooled stage is named end to end (QIAquick column, 15-cycle KAPA HiFi with long elongation to limit barcode hopping, 0.55x/0.75x AMPure double size selection drawn as a selection with its rejected fraction, 150-bp paired-end MiSeq v2 with 30% PhiX, reads trimmed to 80 nt). The drawn amplicon is no longer a 46-nt placeholder but the 92-nt S-gene region from the published 102-nt reference, with the downstream barcode relabelled as the left barcode's reverse complement; the selftest pins that transcription and the primer-site ends. The page shows the Table 2 discrepancy where the barcoded forward primer handle is one base shorter than the TruSeq Read 2 arm in the same table's P7 primer. DC1 and protocols.io remain unavailable.

INVADEseq (checked_with_corrections). Read the published Nat Protoc manuscript PMC10790651 (Materials, Equipment setup, Steps 70–121, analysis) and the 2022 Nature MOESM1 Methods. The three published primers match the model verbatim. The bacterial branch now shows the repeated 955–1,215-bp BluePippin selection after the 15-cycle TT Set A PCR as its own graph step. The page and note now attribute the GEX 26×10×10×90 run to the 2022 application; the protocol itself names HiSeq4000 PE75 or user-chosen NextSeq/NovaSeq runs. Deferred: CG000086 Rev J, the Next GEM v2 guide, TT Set A sequences/well and the post-index length. Suite, page, pairing (0), chemistry (2/2/14/0), source guard (0 exact, 3 symbolic), Markdown and review schema pass.

ISSAAC-seq (checked_with_corrections). The published main article is still paywalled (manual DOI URL). Read published MOESM1 Supplementary Protocol 2 (plate, steps 1–67) and the MOESM4 Supplementary Table 4 oligos, which match the model verbatim. The plate graph previously drew a split dual-index PCR. It now follows the source: Maxima H Minus + Exo I gap fill in nuclei, sort into Truseq_S5_short/N7xxx lysis wells, 4 RNA then 6 ATAC+RNA in-well pre-PCR cycles that add i7, pooling, Exo I, and one 8-cycle final PCR with both i5 primers and illumina P7. The RNA_plate primer now uses its published TruSeq R1 prefix. Deferred: the main-article Methods, the droplet branch and its CG000209 revision, the scATAC i7 plate predecessor, and the index members used. Suite 11/11, page, pairing (0), chemistry (2/4/11/0), source guard (2 exact, 8 symbolic), Markdown and review schema pass.

Read Linder 2015 PMC4487409 Methods lines 143 and 153–177 plus the MOESM79 README, Grozhik 2017 PMC5562447 §2.3/§3.4–3.12/Notes 25 and 27, and the König 2010 iCLIP Supplementary Methods, newly fetched. The earlier model placed an unsourced small-RNA RA5 Read 1 site. It is replaced by the TruSeq Read 1 arm computed from the same lab's 2017 oligos, marked inferred for 2015. The model adds PNK, membrane selection, proteinase K, TBE-urea size selection, cut oligo + BamHI, and printed T4 RNA ligase, SSIII, 18–21 cycles and HiSeq 2500. It removes a false restriction-fragment waste. The 2010 supplement shows an older NNN+2-nt generation; Linder's read processing matches NN-BBBB-NNN. Deferred: per-sample RT barcode, 2015 read lengths and Read 2 primer, 2015 circularization conditions.

Microbe-seq (microbe-seq__10.1126+science.abm1483) — deferred_no_original. The Science article and supplement are paywalled, and the OSTI green accepted manuscript 1959222 timed out. That manual URL is recorded. Read the superseded bioRxiv v1 supplementary Methods (media-1 lines 40–113) and Table S6 (media-3 lines 217–700), used only as labelled, inferred preprint-derived input. The unsourced dual-index Nextera/150+150 model is replaced. The new model has a bead barcode 1 (8–11 nt) + 22-nt linker + barcode 2 (8 nt) upstream of a computed S5/ME arm, a single Nextera i7 index, and a custom unprinted Read-1 primer with a read table (R1 45 barcode, I1 8, R2 genomic). It adds the droplet size filter, AMPure cleanups and the P5PE1/i7 PCR. Deferred: published-version comparison, hydrogel/P5PE1/Read-1 oligos, bead predecessors.

microSPLiT (checked_with_corrections). Re-read published Science PMC8269303 Methods149–175 and the mirrored 2021 Table S3 Round 1 sheet, with the 2024 Nature Protocols procedure as a later cross-check. The graph had begun RT directly on permeabilized cells with only the dT primer; it now declares the published in-cell E. coli PAP polyadenylation, the Round1_49 random-hexamer primer alongside dT15VN (1:2 per well, Maxima H Minus), T4 DNA ligase, and sublibrary SDS/proteinase K lysis before streptavidin capture. The 2021 hexamer design (barcode+T+N6) differs from the 2024 protocol; recorded as a revision. Read lengths 74/86/6 trace to the preprint supplement and 2024 step 172; the published supplement DOCX/Table S3/S4 remain gated.

Microwell-seq (deferred). The defining Cell 2018 article is still out of reach: Cloudflare 403 on cell.com, and Europe PMC reports it is neither in PMC nor open access. The manual URL is recorded. Supplementary Table S2 (publisher mmc2 mirror) was compared row by row with the model and no sequence changed. Model correction: the sequencing plan had claimed acquired cycles (Read 1 54, Index 8) that are only minima computed from the oligos. These were removed, and the limitation and the page now say the 2018 run lengths are unread. The STAR Methods reaction details, the Tn5 adaptor and the run lengths remain deferred.

MIRACL-seq — checked with corrections. Read PMC8358727 STAR Methods 1074–1103 (droplet RAISIN, 7,000 RAISINs/channel, 2×–8× overloading), 1127–1145 and 1595–1625, Results 442–470, Key Resources 2963–2972, plus the 10x CG000108 Rev A v2 assay note lines 1–139. The graph used to jump from barcoded RT straight to index PCR through the queued shared _finish_pcr. It now has a local warned MAGIC kit boundary (template switch, cDNA PCR, fragmentation, size selection, Read 2 ligation) and then a sample-index PCR. Removed the unsupported Read 1 = 26 cycles, since the paper prints only NextSeq 500 and Cell Ranger v2.0. Deferred: CG00052 user guide revision; gated Figures S1–S7 and Tables S1–S6; sample indexes and acquired reads. Verification: suite 1/1, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/5/0, guard 0 exact/3 symbolic, Markdown, review schema.

RAISIN RNA-seq — checked with corrections. Read PMC8358727 STAR Methods 1032–1072 (CST buffer, FACS into TCL, SMART-seq2 modifications, Nextera XT, NextSeq 500), Key Resources 2915–2972/3030–3041, and the cited Shekhar 2016 PMC5003425 plate methods 288–294. Added RNAClean XP and double AMPure XP cleanups with waste fates. Named Maxima H Minus EP0753 and KAPA HiFi HotStart ReadyMix. Replaced the queued shared _finish_pcr with a local custom-adapter index PCR. Recorded the 75-cycle kit, with no read configuration printed. Shekhar's 0.075 ng input and ¼ Nextera XT volumes are marked as inherited by citation. Scraper KRT hits at 3225–3246 are template placeholders. Deferred: custom adapter sequences (on request), Picelli/Kowalczyk, gated supplements, acquired reads. Verification: suite 2/2, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/11/0, guard 2 exact/0 failed/5 symbolic, Markdown, review schema.

mNET-seq (Nojima et al. 2015, Cell). Bounded pass on the published PMC4410947 JATS Methods 195–211, original Document S1 2495–2525 and the GSE60358 extraction metadata. Route reconfirmed: MNase/EGTA, soluble supernatant, Pol II IP, on-bead T4 PNK 3′-phosphatase-minus + ATP before Trizol, 35–100-nt urea-gel window, TruSeq Small RNA kit, Read 2 first-base readout. Source conditions were added to graph inputs. A second graph-owned published discrepancy is now on the page: MNase-buffer chromatin wash 100 µl (article/Document S1) vs 1 ml (GEO); no volume is selected. Deferred: 2016 Nat Protoc successor re-read, 2015 kit revision and ordered oligos, acquired cycles, SRA run metadata.

Modified SMART-Seq (Zheng et al. 2022, Methods Mol Biol). Bounded pass on the author-hosted PDF: Table 1, Methods 3.5–3.8 and Notes 1–16. Repository modelling errors were corrected. Exonuclease I now follows per-plate pooling and the QIAquick column, as in 3.7 and Note 6. The TSO end was removed from the final 3′ library. The oligo(dT)VN tract and the universal-primer tail between P5 and the RT handle were added, derived from the printed primers. SPRI cleanups and their discarded supernatants are explicit. Tagmentation off-products are unresolved, not discarded. Index 2 is recorded as optional (Note 13). Deferred: Tn5 adapter oligos, Nextera XT original, transcription of the Table 2/3 barcodes, SMART-seq3 predecessor, publisher copy.

Moffat two-step CRISPR readout is `checked_with_corrections`: author protocol rev.20190723 sec 3.5.3, Tables 1–2 and sec 3.5.4 inspected. Graph now owns the Table 1 LCV2 PCR1 pair and full D501-F/D701-R Ultramers with exact PCR geometry, names NEBNext Ultra II Q5, records cycling/pooling and adds the 2% gel 200-bp excision with discarded remainder. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 2 established/3 unresolved, zero contradictions; source guard checks 4 exact oligos. LCV2 flanks, pLCKO2/stagger variants and structured dark cycles remain deferred.

Multi-CUT&Tag is `checked_with_corrections`: published STAR Methods (PMC8604773) inspected; sequences still from preprint Table S1 because published supplement-2 is gated. Graph now has adapter loading/antibody coupling, His-tag purification, wash, 10 mM Mg tagmentation, extraction, NEBNext HiFi gap fill/17-cycle PCR, AMPure, and single-cell 1-cycle GEM, SILANE, 14-cycle SI-PCR and double size selection. Model error fixed: bulk i5/i7 primers are not in Table S1, so their arms are inferred. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 16 unresolved, zero contradictions; source guard 0 exact, 1 input without exact state.

multi-tag__10.1038+s41587-022-01522-9: checked with corrections. Read the published PMC10188359 JATS Methods (l.105–121) and Supplementary Table 1 (published MOESM2, MD5-identical to local media-2.xlsx). The model had drawn a generic primary-antibody-then-barcoded-pA–Tn5 route. It now follows the published single-cell route: antibody–P5_i5 conjugate transposome rounds, secondary antibody, i7-only transposome tagmentation in 96 barcoded wells, DNase, strainers, ICELL8 dispense, SDS/Triton, nanowell PCR with 72×72 i5/i7 primers, 2×1.3× HighPrep, then HiSeq PE 25×25. Exact example-row oligos are graph inputs. The target barcode and three-part cell identity are now features. An unsupported protocols.io DOI was removed from the note. Deferred: MOESM1 (image-only), ICELL8 ref.62 PCR conditions, sequencing primers/index-read lengths, bulk branch. Source guard: 4 of 6 fail on a locator exclusion; the selftest verifies them.

music__10.1038+s41586-024-07239-w: checked with corrections. Read the published PMC11023937 JATS Critical reagents (l.169–203) and procedure (l.221–258), Supplementary Note 2 and the Supplementary Table 2 sheet. Repository modelling correction: the graph had ligated the DNA linker before the RNA linker and lacked digestion, dA-tailing, PNK, poly(A), sonication, GEM RT, crosslink-reversal, Bst, cleanup and gel steps. It now follows the published order, declares the RNA-linker single-strand junction and draws separate RNA- and DNA-derived final molecules. Transcription error fixed: the DNA-linker top strand has A30, not A31. The 15/14-bp linker discrepancy stays on the page. Deferred: 10x CG000315 guide (manual), Bst purpose, barcode overhang junctions. Source guard: 2 fail on a locator exclusion; the selftest verifies them.

**nano-CUT&Tag** — checked with corrections. Re-read the published PMC10264246 Methods: Tn5 loading, nano-CT, the ATAC branch, single-cell indexing and library prep, and sequencing setup (JATS 177–209). Also read published Supplementary Data Fig. 2 (MOESM1 134–181) and the linked CG000209 Rev G Step 2.5 GEM program and Appendix A1. The graph now draws the source-stated post-GEM SILANE/SPRIselect recovery, the Zymo DCC-5 column after the MeB-only tagmentation, and the 0.4×/1.2× two-sided SPRIselect before sequencing. It records the MgCl2/EDTA first-tagmentation conditions and 11–15 SI-PCR cycles. A repository modelling error was corrected: an unsourced separate gap-fill polymerase is now the 10x Barcoding Enzyme's initial 72 °C GEM hold, labelled as the model's reading. Supp Fig. 2 matches the drawn library and the 36-8-48-36 reads. Deferred: full CG000209 procedure text, the ATAC branch, orphan-product fate and sample-index members.

**nCATS** — checked with corrections. Re-read published PMC7145730 Results line 58 and Online Methods lines 102–118 (guide design, RNP assembly, cleavage/library prep, sequencing), Supplementary Table 1 guide rows, and the cited protocols.io V.2 steps 7–21. The graph now draws the 0.3× AMPure XP cleanup with LFB washes and EB elution, and names the CIP/Cas9 RNP/Taq/Quick Ligase reagents and conditions. The outward-flank discard now cites Cas9 staying bound 5′ of the gRNA (preferential 3′-side ligation). The page shows the article's 20 min vs protocols.io V.2's 15 min Cas9 cleavage discrepancy; the graph fixes no time. LSK109 adapter bases stay a proprietary role token. Deferred: the LSK109 guide, Sup Tables 2–7, a bound-Cas9 state, and off-target/one-sided product fates.

**NanoSeq** (`nanoseq__10.1038+s41586-021-03477-4`) — checked with corrections. The published article is paywalled. Read: the accepted author manuscript (Apollo 1810/319175, md5 re-verified) Methods lines 1268–1386/1461–1464/1590–1622, and the newly fetched published SI Notes 2–4. The old 'restrictive end repair' step was replaced by the source chain: on-bead HpyCH4V, 2.5x AMPure, Klenow exo- A-tailing with dATP/ddBTPs, xGen Duplex Seq ligation, 1x AMPure, qPCR (NanoqPCR1/2), dilution bottleneck, Q5 UDI PCR, 2x 0.7x AMPure, 150PE. Also added: a ddBTP-blocked nick candidate with unresolved fate, a NNN-T cohesive adapter end, and a NNN-T-CA…TG-A-nnn final library with umi features and an inferred TruSeq-type shell. The source guard fails on NanoqPCR1/2 only because the AM is not a declared source; it needs an extra_sources row. Deferred: published Methods, Protocol Exchange pex-1298, xGen adapter strands, UDI primers, and the BotSeqS/Mung Bean variants.

**NEBNext Enzymatic Methyl-seq v2** (`nebnext-enzymatic-methyl-seq-v2`) — checked with corrections. Read: the E8015 manual v2.0_7/26 (now fetched locally) pp. 1–14, including §1.1–1.11, the trimming sequences and the revision history, plus the LV UDI manual v2.0_6/26 compatibility and index lines. The omitted formamide/NaOH denaturation before APOBEC was added. Also added: control spike-ins, fragmentation, Stop Reagent, and the 1X/1X/0.8X bead clean-ups with no cleanup before PCR. Pre-PCR intermediates no longer carry the PCR-added P5/P7/index shell. The adaptor end is declared unavailable because the manual prints no 3'-dT or conversion-resistance claim. The selftest checks the printed trimming sequences against the library. The E3390/E3900 discrepancy is retained, with corrected revision wording. Deferred: earlier revisions 1.2/1.3, the FAQ/sample sheets, adaptor and UDI strands, and the UltraShear manual.

**nebnext-low-bias-small-rna** (round 13, checked with corrections). Read the E3420 manual v2.0_5/26 steps 1-8 and Sequencing and Data Processing (lines 875-890), the Overview of the LV UDI manual v2.0_6/26, and Maguire 2020 Table S2. The step-6 RT bead selection removes large cDNA and unligated adaptor. The miRNA-focused (8A) versus broad (8B) choice comes after PCR and is now modelled there, with waste fates. The undisclosed kit-remnant placeholders were removed: the printed trim motifs place the insert directly between the TruSeq Read 1 and Index 1 arms, and final_library() enforces this. Adaptor/splint/UDI strands, enzyme identities, the 403 NEB pages, FAQs and patents remain deferred. Suite 100 pass; pairing 0; chemistry 0/0/8/0; guard 0 checked.

**nebnext-single-cell-low-input-rna** (round 13, checked with corrections). Read the E6420 manual v6.0_01/24 Section 1 steps 1.2-1.13 and the Oligo Sequences, Adaptor Trimming and Read Length passages (lines 1840-1870). Corrected the amplified-cDNA state: the RT handle is now the reverse complement after the poly(A) copy, so the single printed cDNA PCR primer lands symmetrically (exact geometry), and the TSO 5' extension is absent from products. Added the double cDNA cleanup, USER treatment (mechanism not drawn), and the 0.8X/0.9X cleanups. The final library is marked inferred because it depends on the oligo kit; reads are 2x75. Oligo-kit sequences and enzyme identities are deferred. Suite 102 pass; pairing 0; chemistry 1/2/13/0; guard 2 exact checked, 1 RT primer unrepresented.

NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA: checked with corrections (round 13). Read the E7760/E7765 manual v7.0_4/25 kit contents, trim sequences and Section 4 (lines 3129-3780), and compared the USER steps in the other sections. The graph now follows the manual's order: 94C fragmentation, RT with Strand Specificity Reagent, dUTP second strand, 1.8X cleanup, End Prep, ligation with Enhancer, USER in the ligation tube, 0.9X cleanup, Q5 PCR for 6-13 cycles, 0.9X cleanup. Removed the unsupported claim that the reagent 'reinforces' USER, and the dU-strand excision mechanism is now labelled inferred. Highlights on the page say the adaptor is non-indexed and its USER action is not drawn, and that the dual-index layout is one oligo-kit choice. Deferred: the oligo kit manual, the NEB workflow page, reagent compositions, and the enrichment/FFPE branches. Suite, page, pairing and review schema pass; chemistry 0/2/12/0; source guard 0 exact.

NEBNext Ultra II DNA (USER adaptor): checked with corrections (round 13). Read the E7103/E7645 v7.0_9/22 End Prep, ligation/USER, Section 3A/3B, PCR tables and Section 5 cleanup, plus E7600 v6.0_6/24 p2-3 and the i501/i701 entries. Confirmed the hairpin, the USER timing in the ligation tube and the minimum of 3 PCR cycles. Corrected the graph: one End Prep Enzyme Mix (no separate tailing polymerase), Ligation Enhancer, named master mixes, conditions, and 0.9X cleanups after USER and after PCR. Page highlights now cover the >50 ng 0.4X/0.2X size-selection branch and the truncated-adaptor rule. 5' phosphorylation is marked inferred. Deferred: the USER product page, the Illumina adapter document, End Prep mix composition, and the 3A branch path. Suite, page, pairing and review schema pass; chemistry 0/1/8/0; source guard 3 exact.

**nebnext-ultra-ii-fs-dna-pcr-free** — checked with corrections. Read NEB E7430/E7435 manual Version 2.0_6/25 in full (high- and low-input sections, figure captions, revision history) and the E7395/E7874/E7876/E7878 UDI-UMI manual Version 4.0_6/26 (workflow, Figure 1, p23 run segments, revision history; hand-fetched because the catalogue URL lacks `.pdf`). The previously cited E7430 v3.0 9/25 was not located; citation now names the inspected revision. Graph splits the bead cleanup (50 µl) from size selection (65/58 µl), names ligation reagents/conditions, and highlights retained single-stranded forked adaptor ends and the undrawn low-input branch; i7-before-UMI order is labelled inferred. Deferred: v3.0 identity, proprietary adaptor/fork, i7/UMI order, high-input adaptor concentration. Suite 102 pass; pairing 0; chemistry 0/2/6/0; source guard 0 exact.

**nextera-mate-pair** — checked with corrections. Read the Illumina reference guide 15035209 v02 protocol chapters (tagmentation through library cleanup, TruSeq DNA LT index table; gel-plus selection skimmed) and the data-processing technote (biotinylated junction adapter, Table 1 sequences, incomplete enrichment). Removed the unsupported nine-base gap wording, moved short-fragment removal to the 0.4x AMPure step after strand displacement, routed the mixed shear product through M-280 capture, added the post-PCR AMPure cleanup, and named reagents/conditions. Highlights show incomplete-enrichment FR carryover, single-junction products and the undrawn gel-plus branch. Deferred: TruSeq LT guide (PCR primer cocktail), biotin position, gel-plus branch, read lengths. Suite 1 pass; pairing 0; chemistry 0/2/13/0; source guard 0 exact, 4 symbolic inputs.

nextera-xt-dna-library-prep - capped vendor pass on Nextera XT guide 15031942 v07 (Tagment 917-1023, Amplify 1025-1170, Clean Up 1175-1313), Adapter Sequences v22 (Nextera adapters/PCR primers 249-283, N7xx/S5xx tables) and Tips note 770-2015-015. Corrections: local exact index PCR with printed Index1/Index2 primers (8-N windows) replaces the shared unavailable-geometry PCR; IPB single-sided cleanup with <200 bp waste is represented; TAG 55C 5 min, immediate NT and NXT program conditions on steps; transposome carries generic Nextera Read1/Read2 transposase adapters, still inferred. Caveat names unprinted ATM/non-transferred strands and the closed tagmented drawing. Deferred: pooling guide (404), gap geometry, bead-normalized endpoint, UD indexes.

ntt-seq__10.1038+s41587-022-01588-5 - capped pass on published PMC10272075 Methods (MEDS assembly 100, targeting/tagmentation 120-126, single-cell 130-140, sequencing 144), Extended Data Table 2 (278-300) and 10x CG000209 Rev G GEM incubation (2286-2350). Corrections: removed a separate bulk gap-fill step that the single-cell Methods do not describe; gap fill now sits in the GEM 72C 5-min step, marked inferred; Mg2+ 37C tagmentation condition, binding/wash conditions and pMENT 1:1 loading caption added; SI-PCR primers now placed exactly at computed sites. pMENT sequence attributed to Amini 2014 Supp Table 4. Deferred: CUT&Tag protocols.io predecessor, scCUT&Tag-pro/TotalSeq-A, kit revision, 10x cleanups, ADT library.

## Round 14 — 20 protocols

Ledger: [2026-10-17-breadth-first-14.tsv](catalogue/curation_rounds/2026-10-17-breadth-first-14.tsv).

| Protocol | Recorded pass status | Deferred follow-up from its ledger |

OAK is `checked_with_corrections`: published JATS Methods (scRNA and paired snRNA/snATAC) read; example i7 index corrected to the underlined 10-base CGCATGTTAC. Graph adds silane cleanups, exact cDNA-PCR primer landing, vendor library-construction and pre-amp MAGIC boundaries, SPRI, ATAC split/index PCR/size selection and read lengths. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 2 established, 29 unresolved, 0 contradictory; source guard 9 exact checked, 3 symbolic inputs. 10x kit revisions and lineage/hashing branches remain deferred.

OK-seq is `checked_with_corrections`: published JATS Methods and typeset PDF primer passage read. Graph adds RNA-primer alkaline hydrolysis/P-30, the repeated on-bead ligation, MinElute and 150–400bp gel selection, exact-state adapters and primers; misattribution to the uninspected Nat Protoc protocol and an unprinted 6-cycle index read removed. Page shows the 'truncated by 5 bases' vs printed ATGATAC… forward-primer discrepancy. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 4 established/1 conditional/15 unresolved/0 contradictory; source guard 9 exact, 1 symbolic.

Olink Explore (olink-explore__10.1016+j.mcpro.2021.100168) — checked with corrections. Re-read PMC8633680 Experimental Procedures (JATS 158–176) and the WO2021191442A1 worked PCR1/PCR2 example (lines 4117–4195). Step order, 25-cycle PCR1, block pooling, 10-cycle PCR2, 148-bp product and 66-cycle Read 1 confirmed. Added the 4 °C 16–24 h immunoreaction condition, PCR1-mix extension catalyst, panel pooling and AMPure XP cleanup with discarded supernatant. The page now shows the article's 'complementary to P5' versus the patent's 'comprises P5' forward-primer wording; the model uses P5 sense. Deferred: complete probe/primer/index/barcode sequences, the lengths of regions inside the 66-nt insert, the vendor archive and guide, and enzyme identity.

Omni-ATAC (omni-atac__10.1038+nmeth.4396) — checked with corrections. Read PMC5623106 Online Methods (JATS 265–360), the Author Contributions and reference list, and Supplementary Protocol 1 (MOESM3). The page wrongly said the transposase was Illumina TDE1. The source instead cites Picelli 2014 Tn5 produced in-house for all experiments, used in a home-made DMF TD buffer. The caveat, inputs and oligo list are corrected, and the Tn5 adapter is marked inferred. Added the Zymo/NEBNext conditions, qPCR-chosen extra cycles, final Zymo cleanup and PAGE >100-bp selection. Reads changed to 2×75. Deferred: Picelli loading oligos, Protocol Exchange, Ad2 members/Index 1 cycles, and the frozen-tissue and 500-cell variants.

Ovation RNA-Seq V2 is `checked_with_corrections`. We read the published NAR article (JATS Intro/Methods/Results) and its Supplementary Material, which holds mapping statistics only. The unsupported chimeric second-strand primer is gone. The model now has heat partial RNA degradation, RNAClean XP, random-hexamer second-strand amplification, Covaris/gel selection, end repair/dA-tail/bar-coded ligation, purification, gel and 15-cycle PCR. The unpublished 8-nt index length was removed. The page shows the Intro vs Methods second-strand wording discrepancy. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry has 14 unresolved claims and zero contradictions. The source guard checks zero exact inputs because no bases are printed. The V2 guide, the Kurn/Dafforn predecessors and the NEB kit remain deferred.

ONT cDNA-PCR V14 is `checked_with_corrections`. We read SQK-PCS114 Rev F (31 Mar 2026) and CHTD_500 rev AV §15–16. The cPRM reverse primer had lost a published CTTTGTG in the shared lib constant. That was a repository transcription error, and the module now declares both published primers. The graph now follows the Rev F order: CRTA anneal/T3 ligation, Lambda exo+USER, RNAClean XP, Maxima RT/SSPII switch, heat kill, AMPure, the 4×22-cycle Ex Premier PCR, ExoI, pooled AMPure and RA addition. The PCR substrate, UMI polarity and computed anneal lengths are corrected. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry has 12 unresolved claims and zero contradictions. The source guard checks 3 exact oligos with 2 inputs unrepresented. The CRTA overhang strand and the RA motor/attachment chemistry remain deferred.

Oxford Nanopore Direct cDNA (SQK-DCS109) is `deferred`: tech document CHTD_500 revAV DCS109 paragraph and adapter table plus 2020 brochure kit table inspected; the kit protocol itself was unobtainable (URLs 404, community login). Missing strand-switching primer added to RT, poly(A) priming and end repair marked inferred, current-kit adapter table explicitly not used. Suite/page/pairing/Markdown/review schema pass; chemistry 5 unresolved, 0 contradictory; source guard 0 exact inputs (all oligos symbolic).

Oxford Nanopore Direct RNA (SQK-RNA004) is `checked_with_corrections`: vendor protocol DRS_9195 v4 revL (newly cached) library-prep steps and tech document RNA004 paragraph inspected. RLA now a T4 DNA ligase ligation, Induro RT conditions, both RNAClean XP cleanups with WSB/REB and waste, and the optional RCS spike-in represented; RTA poly(A) targeting marked inferred. Suite/page/pairing/Markdown/review schema pass; chemistry 5 unresolved, 0 contradictory; source guard 0 exact inputs (adapters symbolic).

**oxford-nanopore-ligation-sequencing-v14** — checked with corrections. Read SQK-LSK114 GDE_9161_v114 revAC (end prep and cleanup at lines 490–611, ligation and cleanup at 615–800, priming at 817–931, troubleshooting at 1207–1212) and CHTD_500 v1 revAV (LA strands at 2067–2081, reagent roles at 2122–2134, both-ends potency at 813). LA strands and end-prep programme verified. The graph had gone straight from end prep to ligation to sequencing. It now has both AMPure cleanups with waste outputs: 60 µl AXP with ethanol, then 40 µl AXP with LFB/SFB washes and no ethanol. Reagents and the 10 min RT ligation are named. Deferred: complete motor/Y-leader structure, tether chemistry, NEB module documents. Suite, page, pairing (0), chemistry (4 unresolved), source guard (0 exact), Markdown and review schema pass.

**oxford-nanopore-native-barcoding-v14** — checked with corrections. Read SQK-NBD114.24 NBE_9169_v114 revX at 642–1214 and the barcode table at 452–549 (all 24 pairs match), CHTD_500 revAV §14 flanks (1139–1145) and §15 NA strands, and kit page 258/290. Step order now follows revX. The model had put equimolar pooling after barcoding. Now the end-prep cleanup is followed by an equimolar mass into barcode ligation, then the EDTA stop, the pooling of all barcoded samples and a 0.4X AXP/SFB cleanup, then NA ligation and an LFB/SFB/EB cleanup, each cleanup with waste outputs. The CHTD-printed flanks replace the placeholder flanks, and the spurious [cohesive hook] token is gone. Treating the final T as the dT tail and pairing the NA 3′ overhang ATTGCT with the reverse-flank AGCAAT as the hook are construction-checked derivations and are labelled as such. Hook geometry remains unavailable. Deferred: barcode-adapter oligo inventory, motor assembly, 96-plex variant applicability. Suite (7), page, pairing (0), chemistry (9 unresolved), source guard (0 exact), Markdown and review schema pass.

**oxford-nanopore-rapid-sequencing-v14** — checked with corrections. Re-read the SQK-RAD114 protocol (RSE_9177_v114 Rev Q, 12 Dec 2025; text lines 164–515) and the Chemistry Technical Document (CHTD_500 v1 rev AV; lines 248–251, 379–417, 2068–2087, 2120–2126). The model already matched the fragmentation programme, the 5-minute attachment and the RA top strand. Adapter Buffer is now an attachment condition, and the 1.5 + 3.5 µl dilution is checked against the source text. Candidate unadapted tagmentation products are now an unresolved carried fate instead of a discard, because the protocol has no clean-up before loading. Still deferred: FRA tag bases and the attachment end, the complete motor/RA assembly, and flow-cell loading. Suite 8/8, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/2/0, source guard 1 checked/0 failed.

**oxford-nanopore-ultra-long-v14** — checked with corrections. Re-read the SQK-ULK114 protocol (ULK_9177_v114 Rev Q, 6 Nov 2025; lines 164–297, 1166–1403, 1453–1480) and CHTD_500 v1 rev AV (lines 248–251, 379–423, 2068–2126). Model correction: the endpoint no longer draws the published RA strand joined 5′ of the FRA tag. The RA strand is now a local graph input checked against the source, replacing the shared nanopore_library import. FDB and EB are now conditions. Unadapted products are an unresolved carried fate, and only the aspirated supernatant is discarded. The selftest now checks values against the source text instead of restating constants. Still deferred: tag bases and the attachment end, the motor assembly and the unnamed enzyme protected by the ice step, and extraction/loading steps. Suite 8/8, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/3/0, source guard 1 checked/0 failed.

pacbio-isoseq-smrtbell-prep-kit-3-0 (checked with corrections). Re-read PacBio 102-396-000 Rev10 (Iso-Seq express 2.0 cDNA). It names the multiplexed amplicon procedure 102-359-000 Rev08 as the monomer SMRTbell route (pp. 1/13). Its revision history shows the SPK 3.0 section was removed at Rev09, so the 404 'kit-specific' URL is earlier Rev07 of the same document. 102-359-000 and the 2022 training overview were fetched; the training covers an earlier NEB-based cDNA version and is now cited only as historical. The graph now has RT/TS conditions, the 12-cycle PCR, 1.3X/1.1X cleanups, repair & A-tailing 37/65 °C, 20 °C ligation, nuclease treatment and a final 1.3X cleanup. The hairpin end remains unavailable. Deferred: Rev07, adapter/primer/RT-oligo bases, nuclease specificity. Chemistry 1/0/10/0; guard 0 exact (2 FASTA-only inputs).

pacbio-kinnex-full-length-rna (checked with corrections). Re-read PacBio 103-238-700 Rev09 sections 2–7 and application note 102-326-591 Rev03 pp. 2–3, and re-compared the Kinnex adapter index FASTA. The graph now follows the procedure: RT/TS conditions, 10-cycle Iso-Seq PCR, 0.9X cleanup, and eight 9-cycle Kinnex PCRs (primer mixes A–G, HQ; mix split inferred). Then come the 23 µL pool with 1.05X cleanup, 45 °C 'primer digestion/ligation' linear-array formation with a bc01–04 adapter, 45 °C repair, 1X cleanup, nuclease, and a final 1X cleanup that physically removes the digested partial arrays. The sequencing primer is corrected to the Kinnex sequencing primer. Deferred: primer-mix/adapter/sequencing-primer bases and end geometry, rendered conceptual figures, newer 8/12-fold kits. Chemistry 0/0/30/0; guard 0 exact.

**pacbio-smrtbell-express-template-prep-kit-2-0** — checked with corrections. Read procedure 101-693-800 v01 reaction mixes 1–4, post-ligation purification, size-selection and anneal/bind sections, plus training PN 101-742-500 v01 kit-content and workflow-comparison slides. The model drew a post-ligation nuclease digestion that v01 does not contain (the ExoIII/ExoVII step belongs to TPK 1.0 in the training comparison); the graph now draws the 0.45X AMPure PB cleanup, and the purified library is no longer claimed to be topology-selected. Reaction conditions and the T-overhang/A-T ligation were verified. The note marks 5′ phosphorylation as inferred. Deferred: adapter body/primer bases, SMRT Link v6 Sample Setup, the undrawn BluePippin branch. Suite 4/4, pairing 0, chemistry 3 unresolved, source guard 0 exact.

**pacbio-smrtbell-prep-kit-3-0** — checked with corrections. Read procedure 102-166-600 Rev09 §§4–8 (repair/A-tailing, ligation and 1X bead cleanup, nuclease, 3.1X 35% AMPure PB size selection, ABC) and training 102-390-900 Rev03 p.6 workflow figure plus the index-plate and adapter-design passages. Incubations were verified. The graph now includes the two omitted bead cleanups/size selection with discarded supernatants, so the nuclease product is no longer the final library. The adapter 3′ T is inferred, not printed: it is now marked in the geometry statement, page caveat/caption and note. Unstated 5′ phosphorylation was removed from the labels. Deferred: index plate 96A–D documents, adapter/primer bases, undrawn optional branches, AMPure PB technical note. Suite 101/101, pairing 0, chemistry 5 unresolved, source guard 0 exact.

**PacBio telobait** — checked with corrections. The slug source dir held only the Makarova 2025 review; get_sources --slugs fetched the published PMC9845338 article and MOESM1–10 there. Read Methods l218–224 and l230–232, Fig. S2a, and Supplementary Data 2 F01/R01-1..6. The model had the phase arm on F01 and ligation to a C-rich end, which the guard failed. It now draws the F01/R01-1 duplex with the G-rich 3′ end ligated to the F01 5′-P, plus ligated and on-bead gap-filled states, an EcoRI release that leaves the biotin stub on the beads with a 5′-AATT end, and a 0.45× AMPure PB cleanup. The page shows the Results (l128) vs Methods (l224) gap-fill/capture order discrepancy; the model follows Methods. The SMRTbell kit guide remains deferred. Guard: 2 exact checked, 0 failed.

**Paired-seq** — checked with corrections. Read the PMC7231560 author-manuscript JATS (Results l65, Methods l117–149, l159) and publisher Supplementary Tables 1–2 (MOESM2/3, held as manifest-identical scg_ copies). BC1 matched NotI/SbfI families, the BC2/BC3 5Phos plates, the R04 UMI/BC4/phase oligos, the linker/blocker/terminator complements, and Anchor/PA/N5XX/P7XX all verified. Corrections: the RNA library keeps its oligo-dT-derived poly(A); a false 'dA junction' was relabelled as the R04 arm plus P7XX extension; Blocker-R02/R03 and the Terminator were added as round inputs; Methods conditions were added. The guard's 9 failures are locator-filter exclusions of the scg-mirrored publisher tables, not missing sequences. Deferred: TELP predecessor, Tn5-P5 loading, cleanup/off-product fates.

PairedTag (pairedtag__10.1038+s41592-021-01060-3) — checked with corrections. Read PMC7954905 Online Methods lines116–156, ExtData Fig.2a legend, all four Supp Table 1 sheets and the Supplementary Notes. All drawn oligos, 5'-phosphates, the unphosphorylated R04 and its 0–3-nt phase block match the table. Graph now names T4 ligase, NEB TdT/dCTP and KAPA HiFi HS (16 linear + 11 PA cycles); the anchor '*' is shown as printed rather than called a penultimate phosphorothioate; NotI/SbfI text kept to source operations. Page shows Methods T15 vs table T16VN and Anchor-FokI-GSH vs -GH discrepancies; Read1 50/100 disclosed. Deferred: Protocol Exchange protocol (manual), Paired-seq predecessor, '*' meaning, dataset Read1 length. Suite 11/11, pairing 0, chemistry 13/3/15/0, source guard 6 checked/0 failed.

PAIso-seq (paiso-seq__10.1038+s41467-019-13228-9) — checked with corrections. Re-read PMC6876564 Results line54, Methods lines142–148 and Supp Table 1 (lines688–745). Sequences unchanged. Graph names Klenow exo-, USER and KAPA HiFi HS ReadyMix, replaces the USER-step discard with an explicit >200 nt RNA Clean & Concentrator-5 separation, and adds the Pure PB 1x/0.4x two-fraction selection before SMRTbell preparation. Page shows two published discrepancies: Results dU+7T spacing vs table dU+8T, and 'TSO minus triple G' prose vs table RT/PCR primers; table values drawn. Deferred: 2022 protocol original (manual), SMRTbell kit/hairpin ends, exact oligo states. Suite 12/12, pairing 0, chemistry 1/0/9/0, source guard 0 exact checked/0 failed.

PARIS (2026-10-17, round breadth-first-15) — read the published Cell article (PMC5029792 JATS line144), the publisher supplement mmc1 Extended Experimental Procedures Steps 1–3 (lines 25–110) and the cited Flynn 2016 icSHAPE protocol (PMC4896316 reagents lines 132–206, procedure steps 26–66, lines 532–800). The graph now carries the source conditions: AMT 0.5 mg/ml with UV365 for 30 min, a separate S1-nuclease/proteinase K/TRIzol digestion step, ShortCut RNase III with MnCl2, T4 RNA ligase 1 overnight at room temperature and UV254 photoreversal; on the inherited adapter route it names the preadenylylated 3'-blocked linker, PEG8000 ligation, SuperScript III, CircLigase II and qPCR-monitored Phusion, and adds the two gel size selections with their discarded linker and unextended-primer waste. No published discrepancy was found. Deferred: which 3' linker block PARIS used (so whether Flynn's streptavidin capture applies), the circular-cDNA junction and read entry, the acquired NextSeq read length, and the undrawn HeLa random-hexamer branch.

PEM-seq (2026-10-17, round breadth-first-15) — read the defining Cell Discovery article's Materials and methods (PMC6434046 JATS lines 131–173) and the publisher supplement Table S11 (lines 7085–7130). The graph now states the source reagents and conditions end to end: sonication to 0.3–2 kb from 20 µg DNA, a bait primer within 200 bp annealed over five cycles at a length-dependent Ta, Bst polymerase 3.0 extension, a new AxyPrep primer-depletion and denaturation step, Streptavidin C1 capture, on-bead T4 DNA ligase bridge ligation in 15% PEG8000 overnight, 16-cycle Taq nested PCR, a new size-selection step, and a locally declared FastPfu tagging PCR with the exact P7 primer in place of the shared generic finisher. Table S11 prints P5-I5 across two table cells, so it stays a named input whose transcription the selftest checks. Deferred: exact bridge-ligation and nested-amplicon geometry, the unavailable LAM-HTGTS predecessor, the STAR Protocols vendor guide and PEM-Q repository, and the still-displayed 2019/2022 random-barcode conflict.

**phage-atac** — checked_with_corrections. Fetched the published Nat Biotechnol 40:374 article (PMC10549956 JATS) and the Springer MOESM1, and moved them into the slug source dir. Read Methods lines 106–108, 136–148 and 154–156, plus Supplementary Tables 1 and 4. Active claims moved off the preprint and the citation page was corrected. The shared _finish_pcr recipe is replaced by local steps: silane cleanup, the 5/35 µl split, EF147/EF91 PDT PCR, double-sided SPRI 65/192 µl, 10x indexing PCR (6 cycles), the final SPRI and 2×34 reads. PDT role order is inferred. Deferred: the 10x scATAC guide (bead oligo, SI-PCR B, i7), the gated NIHMS supplement and index cycles. Suite and page pass; pairing 0; chemistry 0/0/14/0; guard 2 exact, 0 failed.

**phospho-seq** — checked_with_corrections. Read the published Nat Commun 16:1346 (PMC11794950) Methods lines 213–217, 241–245, 255 and 281–287, plus the cited ASAP-seq JATS line 234 for BOA/BOB. Fixed three transcription errors: hash poly(A) 28A+*A*A, blocking oligo N29/3ddC/, and first author Blair, not Chung. The shared _finish_pcr recipe is replaced by local steps: blocking/hashing, staining, BOA/BOB with the 40 °C step, silane elution with 3 µl set aside, saved first-SPRI supernatant, 32 µl SPRI, TSA/TSB PCRs with published P5/P7 and 1.6× SPRI. Read layout is 34/8/16/34. Deferred: the 10x scATAC v1.1 guide, the author PDFs (unreachable) and the Multiome branch. Suite and page pass; pairing 0; chemistry 0/0/18/0; guard 2 exact, 0 failed, BOB excepted.

**picoplex-dna-seq** — checked with corrections. The defining Rubicon manual QAM-106-002 (Takara-hosted PDF) was fetched for the first time and read in full: text lines 59–938, plus the rendered Figures 1, 2 and 7. The model now carries the printed D501–D508/D701–D706 index table, with strand left unassigned and a transcription self-test. It also adds the lysis, 12-cycle pre-amp and 4+7-cycle amplification conditions, 11-nt quasi-random read prefixes, and pooling with a single 1:1 AMPure cleanup. Strand displacement is no longer claimed, and the publisher is corrected to Rubicon. The page shows two discrepancies within the manual: 11 cycles vs 14-base trim, and the 6–60 pg wording. Deferred: US 8,206,913, Illumina pooling guide 15042173, index orientation and Pre-Amp Enzyme identity. Suite 3/3; chemistry 0/0/6/0; source guard 0 exact.

**pip-seq-protein-interaction-profile__10.1186+gb-2014-15-1-r3** — checked with corrections. Read the published PMC4053792 Results overview (lines 61–71), the Methods (155–171), Additional file 1, and Zheng 2010 Text S1 lines 58–59. The graph is now protocol-owned, with no rna_end_readout callers. It covers cross-linking, RIP/DNase lysis and the four-way split with the source RNase conditions. Controls are now ethanol-precipitated and reversed after digestion. Library construction is a warned MAGIC boundary per refs 29/30, followed by DSN normalization and 50-bp single-read HiSeq2000. The unsupported sample-index/P5/P7 endpoint and invented indexed PCR primer were removed. The page shows the published raw-read discrepancy: Results 31–60 M vs S1 24.6–103.7 M. Deferred: Li 2012 Cell Reports (not in PMC), the Illumina v1.5 guide, cpmb.21 and the gated Additional files 2–9. Suite 1/1; chemistry 0/0/25/0.

Pixel-seq is `checked_with_corrections`: author-manuscript STAR Methods and Key Resources oligos inspected; the version of record and gated supplements are unread. The final library is corrected to the published TruSeq libP5 + Nextera i7 hybrid (UMI, T7 anchor, barcode, polony site, poly(T)), derived from the oligos. Untraceable PE150 was removed. USER, second strand, KOH, ExoI and cleanup steps were added. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry has 18 unresolved claims and zero contradictions; source guard checks 3 exact inputs. GEO read lengths and supplements remain deferred.

PLAC-seq is `checked_with_corrections`: published article plus the published Data S1 and Table S1, retrieved from the Springer ESM links because PMC gates them, were inspected. MboI/Klenow/T4 conditions, sonication, named antibodies and the input control are now in the graph. Crosslink reversal and purification were added before the T1 pull-down. Library construction, which Data S1 delegates to in situ Hi-C, is a warned boundary. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry has 10 unresolved claims and zero contradictions; source guard checks 0 exact inputs (3 unrepresented). The Rao 2014 protocol and GEO read lengths remain deferred.

PLAYR (playr__10.1038+nmeth.3742) — checked with corrections. Read Frei 2016 PMC4767631 Results52–58 and Online Methods94–122, MOESM647 Supplementary Table 1, and Nature Protocols PMC7075472 Reagent setup/Steps 13–32 and Table 1 footnote. System-1 probe tails, P-insert, P-backbone and probe-3′-OH-primed RCA match both tables. Insert/backbone hybridization is now a separate state before T4 ligation; enzyme/detection conditions in step labels; detection oligo carries its 5′-Z (C6-thiol/fluorophore) modification; Backbone Detection oligo listed; note's table citation corrected. Deferred: Box 1 labelling, gated PMC packages/GitHub, MOESM646, graph declaration. Suite 2/2, page, pairing 0; check_chemistry n/a (no graph); source guard 0 graphs; Markdown and review schema pass.

Pore-C (pore-c__10.1038+s41587-022-01289-z) — deferred (defining article paywalled). Read published MOESM1 Supplementary Tables (samples/enzymes, no methods), ONT Restriction Pore-C extraction protocol v5 Days 1–3, and the newly fetched superseded 2019 preprint Methods as provenance only. Added the missing NlaIII 65 °C heat inactivation before in-nucleus ligation and source conditions to workflow/graph labels. Preprint SPRI >1.5 kbp and SQK-LSK109 shown only as a labelled provenance notice; drawn later-kit example unchanged. Deferred: published Methods, LSK109/PRC109 documents, published kit/size threshold, DpnII/HindIII branches. Suite 3/3, page, pairing 0; chemistry 0/2/6/0; source guard 0 checked/1 graph; Markdown and review schema pass.

PRO-cap (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-15): read the original author-hosted 2013 SOM, identity lines 1–20, library preparation 105–148, read processing 168–172 and PRO-cap analysis 364–366, with the bundle scraper run. The branch order is kept: no fragmentation, three streptavidin pulls, AP before TAP without PNK, and non-reverse-complemented 5′ reads. Model corrections: PCR now reuses the excess standard RT primer, as SOM 146–147 states. The 3′ ligase is named as T4 RNA ligase I (6 h, 20 °C), and the 5′-ligation enzyme is marked as not named. RT amount, trial PCR and 12–18 cycles were added. The second PCR primer is unassigned. Deferred: identities of the standard Illumina oligos, the 5′ ligase, acquired cycles and loaded primers, TruSeq Small RNA RevC applicability, and nuclei predecessors.

PRO-seq (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-15): read the original author-hosted 2013 SOM, identity lines 1–20, library preparation 105–148, Hsp70 branch 150–166 and read processing 168–180. Also checked the Mahat 2016 Table 1 RPI-n comparator at lines 2870–2903 and ran the scraper. Order, reagents and the RP1/TruSeq option match the SOM; VRA3 (5′p/3′InvdT), VRA5 and RP1 were checked whole. Correction: the VRA5 ligation geometry had attributed a T4 RNA ligase step to the 2016 protocol. It now cites the 2013 SOM and records that the SOM names no 5′ ligase. Ligase, RT amount and PCR cycle/trial labels were added, and the note records the SOM read trimming. Deferred: the historical RPI member, the GX2/GX1 option, the 5′ ligase, acquired cycles and loaded primers, and predecessor methods.

primeflow-rna-assay__10.1038+ncomms6641 - checked with corrections. Read MAN0019788 Rev B.0 product description, workflow figure, assay guidelines and Day 1/Day 2 procedure, Porichis 2014 Methods (JATS 137) and Supplementary Table 1 IL-2 rows. Added the second fixation and sourced hybridization conditions to the page; removed an unsupported claim of four distinct amplification structures and now display the guide's own inconsistency (p.5 three structures for up to four targets vs p.9 four types). The 20 x 20 amplifier/label split is labelled a schematic factorization of the published 400 sites per tree. Deferred: original 2014 FlowRNA manual, Player 2001, ViewRNA 18801 re-read, tail/branch sequences. Suite 98 pass, page built, pairing 0; check_chemistry has no graph (legacy renderer); source guard 0 checked.

probac-seq__10.1038+s41596-024-01002-1 - checked with corrections. Read the published Nat Protoc 2024 JATS experimental design, Steps 1-60 (PNK, three RCA/HindIII rounds, gel purification, Klenow UMI/polyA, PCR-1/2/3, NextSeq run) and Supplementary Tables 1-2 in full. The PCR handle is now the 18-nt common 3' flank of all 29,765 ordered probes (exact InDrop complement, enforced). PNK phosphorylation, two TBE-urea gel selections with discarded short products/unextended primer, and Klenow were added to the graph. The page displays the text-vs-table handle-length swap (23/17 vs 23-nt HindIII 5'/18-nt 3') and 55-60 nt variable regions vs 50 bp design. Deferred: predecessor report, 10x CG000204, GitLab code, MOESM1 figures. Suite 7 pass; pairing 0; chemistry 2/1/22/0; source guard 0 checked, 9 symbolic.

**Prox-seq** (checked with corrections). Read the published PMC11289786 JATS Results/Methods (lines 53, 131, 145–173), MOESM1 Supplementary Methods lines 233–345 and 393–415, and MOESM3 Tables 7–10. Table 10 prints the azide-modified probe A/B oligos (earlier note called them bead oligos). The model now draws probe A as U_fwd site–UMI (HHHHACHHHHACHHHN)–barcode–3′ arm and probe B as 5′-p arm–barcode–poly(A); before, the UMI was on probe B. The 26-nt connector geometry is exact (20 paired bases) and the connector is source-checked. Added T4 ligase conditions and an unresolved unligated-probe fate. The page shows the discrepancy between the article's 119-base product and the 129 nt computed from Table 10. Deferred: Nature Protocols 2024, 10x Rev B guides/Drop-seq bead oligo, branch-specific library ends.

**Proximity Network Assay** (checked with corrections). Read the bioRxiv v3 Results/Fig. 1 legend/Methods (lines 105–125, 178–187, 598–620) and the steps of Pixelgen UM002 v1.00 from STEP 3 through 6.3. The UM001 RCA step was used only for comparison. Added the 2nd-antibody stabilization step, the UM002 incubation conditions, 4-cycle pre-amp and 5/6/8-cycle indexing, both 1:1 SPRI cleanups and the ~271-bp library size. Unclosed padlocks, unlinked RCPs and digestion debris are now unresolved fates rather than discards. The page shows the discrepancy between the preprint's 10-min RCA and the 15 min at 30 °C in UM002/UM001. Deferred: UM002 Fig. 2 image, patent ST.26 listing, kit oligo molecules and the paper-era manual revision.

**Pseudo-seq** (checked_with_corrections). Sources now fetched; we read the open author manuscript PMC4224642 Methods 'Pseudo-seq Library Preparation' (JATS lines 56-64) and inventoried the Nature SI and source data. The Nature version of record is paywalled (manual row). All four oligos were confirmed verbatim with their modifications. We added a Zn fragmentation step before CMC and re-typed end treatment as CIP/PNK dephosphorylation. Captions now give the PAGE fractions, ATP-free T4 RNA ligase 1 ligation and the cDNA gel. The page's adapter discrepancy (pre-adenylated vs printed /5Phos/) now notes that the ATP-free buffer fits an adenylated donor; it stays unresolved. Deferred: GEO GSE58200 read layout, version-of-record comparison, role-level junction geometry. Guard: 0 exact checked, 4 inputs without exact states.

**Quantum Barcoding (QBC)** (checked_with_corrections). The source bundle was missing, so we fetched it this pass (PMC7205613 JATS plus the Supplementary Data workbook). We read Methods lines 216-258/286-292 and Supp Data sheets 1-2. Fixes: the RNA amplicon now ends in revcomp of the second-strand primer Illumina tail, not the AHCA tail. Added a second-strand synthesis step. SC3 is no longer called phosphorylated. Ligation geometry now uses each subcode's 3' arm, splinted onto the reporter 5' phosphate. Splint6 docking labels are corrected and its 3' C3 spacer added. The page shows two published discrepancies: the AHCA anchor missing its 5' G in Methods (model uses GCTCCCTGTCTGACG), and Splint6's 5' p appearing only in Supp Data 1. Deferred: NEBNext E7335/E7600S index layouts, Supp Data 3-11, TotalSeq-B/SNAIL variants, Author Correction. Guard: 0 exact checked, 10 inputs without exact states.

R-ChIP (r-chip__10.1016+j.molcel.2017.10.008) — checked with corrections. Read the Mol Cell 2017 STAR Methods library section (PMC5957070 lines 299–311) and the authors' Nat Protoc 2019 protocol (PMC6604627 line 139, steps 604–766, Table 1 1475–1485). Using the printed adapter and PCR primers, the final library is now N9 tail–N9–insert–A–oligo A–5-nt barcode–P5, with exact PCR geometry and a 40-nt single-end Read 1 from the ligated end. The one-end dA substrate, cleanup, phi29, Klenow exo−, T4, Phusion and gel-selection steps were added, each with waste fates. The page shows the 2017 vs 2019 cycle and size-range discrepancy (130–350 vs 140–350 bp) without choosing one. Deferred: the gated 2017 supplement DOCX, the Ji 2013 predecessor, how the i5-position barcode was read, and template-end sealing. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 3/8/10/0; source guard 5 exact checked, 0 failed.

RAGE-Seq (rage-seq__10.1038+s41467-019-11049-4) — checked with corrections. Read the published Nat Commun Methods (PMC6635368 lines 166–192), the Supplementary Fig. 1a legend and Supplementary Table 8; the preprint is superseded. The amplicon is now explicit: R1 site–16-nt cell barcode–10-nt UMI–oligo(dT)–cDNA–TSO site, from the printed primers and the demultiplexing Methods. Added: pre-capture KAPA PCR with exact geometry, AMPure cleanup, two 47 °C SeqCap EZ captures without HE oligos, post-capture PCRs of 5 and 20 cycles, and the SQK-LSK108/308 ligation with unavailable geometry. The generic TruSeq expression PCR became a warned boundary to the standard Chromium 3′ v2 library (NextSeq 150PE). An unverified kit-non-disclosure wording was corrected to 'not printed in the paper'. Deferred: ONT kit protocols, NimbleGen guide and technical note, 10x v2 guide, probe sequences. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 3/9/9/0; source guard 6 exact checked, 0 failed, motor adapter unrepresented.

scGRO-seq (round 2026-10-17-breadth-first-16): re-read the published Methods (PMC11222150 JATS lines 185–247) and MOESM1 Supplementary Tables 7–9. The graph gained the real front end — nuclei isolation or permeabilization, the 400 mM KCl/Sarkosyl run-on with four 50 µM 3'-O-propargyl NTPs, and the FACS partition of single cells into 96 wells of 8 M urea where the barcode is assigned — plus CuAAC's catalyst and conditions (CuSO4/ascorbate/BTTAA, 15% PEG 8000, 2.66 M urea, 2 h 50 °C), the EDTA quench and TRIzol/3 kDa Amicon cleanup, ZnCl2 10 mM/5 min/65 °C, and RNase H− M-MuLV RT (EP0751, 16% PEG, 2 h 50 °C) with the RT primer the hairpin design omits. The missing 10% denaturing-PAGE size selection, which physically separates unclicked adaptor and empty cDNA before pre-amplification, is now drawn with a residual empty-cDNA branch, so DASH remains a cleavage that blocks amplification rather than a physical removal. Both PCRs name NEBNext Ultra II Q5 and split dual-index primers with the Table 8 six-cycle program; the page shows that the Methods call the final PCR two-step while Table 9 reprints that same program, so no final cycle count is asserted. Deferred: the TSO/RT/index/gRNA ordering sequences, the 3×96 barcode inventories and refs 51–53. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 0/0/13/0 over 11 steps; source guard 0 exact, 1 symbolic input; Markdown and review schema checked.

sci-Plex is `checked_with_corrections`: PMC7289078 Results/Fig.1/Fig.3 legends and all twelve author IDT hash order forms inspected; Science supplement still gated. The false TruSeq dual-index endpoint is replaced by a hash-derived molecule ending in the order-form Nextera Read2 handle, with version-specific sci-RNA-seq arms as role tokens behind one warned boundary; PFA fixation added, free-hash discard made unresolved. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry has two unresolved claims, zero contradictions; source guard checks zero exact inputs (hash oligo carries literal B). Supplement and GEO GSE139944 remain deferred.

sci-RNA-seq is `checked_with_corrections`: the final author-hosted Science overview/Fig.1 and preprint Methods lines895–940/991–992 were re-read; the final supplement is still unavailable (PMC 404, Science 403). The graph now shows the RT stop solution, DAPI before sorting, the 75C second-strand stop and post-PCR 0.8x AMPure pooling before sequencing. A page highlight records that the final overview lists lysis before PCR where the preprint route uses Zymo+AMPure. Suite 3/3, page and pairing pass; chemistry has two conditional and seven unresolved claims, zero contradictions; source guard checks zero exact inputs (four placeholder-bearing oligos).

sci-RNA-seq3 (Cao 2019) — checked_with_corrections. Read the published PMC6434952 Methods library-preparation section (lines 174–190), Table S11 rows and the author primer-plate workbook. Removed the unsupported random second-strand primer. Named the source reagents and conditions: SuperScript IV, T4 ligase with betaine/PEG, NEB second-strand module, i7-only TDE1, NEBNext HF PCR with 12–14 cycles. Added the per-well 1.5x AMPure and pooled 0.8x AMPure cleanups. The USER-released hairpin arm and tagmentation by-products are now unresolved, not discarded. Both the dideoxyU/ideoxyU discrepancy and the new swapped-paragraph-opening discrepancy are graph-owned notices on the page. Deferred: 2017 predecessor supplement, PCR-group→P5/P7 mapping, NEB module original.

sci-Space (Srivatsan 2021) — deferred (partial): the published supplementary methods are behind the PMC gate. Read the main text, the linked two-level sci-RNA-seq guide (steps 15–59) and the transfer guide (steps 1.3, 8–10). Fixed our note's truncated P5 primer (TCT lost at a page break). Removed the unsupported second-strand primer, re-typed section labelling and grid transfer as permeabilization and diffusion rather than hybridization, and added PFA fixation, SPRI/AMPure cleanups and the named Tn5/PCR conditions. sci-Space now builds its graph tail and final hash library locally rather than from protocol_families.spatial. The final library adds the oligo(dT) copy of the hash poly(A) (derived from the step-7.1 hash carrying S7+ME). Guide read lengths 18/10/10/52 are shown only as a recommendation. Deferred: gated supplement, ordering workbook, RT primer and index members, section-oligo tail, acquired cycles.

**scONE-seq** (`scone-seq__10.1126+sciadv.abp8901`) — checked with corrections. Read published PMC9812385 Methods 211-229, Results 111 and supplement Text/Fig. S1C/Table S2. The graph now follows the real single-tube order (One-Tn5 tagmentation with KAPA, SSIII internal RT, ExoI/RNase, TdT C-tail, 3' adaptor second strand, ExoI, Amp-primer co-PCR, AMPure, P5-Tn5 retagmentation, index PCR, scDASH MAGIC, NextSeq500). Branch tags are fixed printed TCATG/AACTT. A derived DNA-branch library carries computed custom-primer landings. The page shows two discrepancies: Table S2 Read 2/I7 labels appear swapped (reverse complements, computed landings), and the article's 6-nt barcode vs the 5-base printed tag. Deferred: Bio-protocol, scDASH, MATQ-seq, i5 primer and read lengths.

**scRCAT-seq** (`scrcat-seq__10.1038+s41467-020-18976-7`) — checked with corrections. Read published PMC7555861 Methods 149/165/169-171, Results 65/113 and the rendered Fig. 1a. The earlier model split TSS and TES into separate inferred PCR branches. The source describes one end-capture PCR: a tag-specific P5 index primer plus a Nextera P7 index primer, with TSS and TES reads separated computationally. The graph now runs SSIII RT, TSO switch, Smart-seq2 preamp (MAGIC), Nextera XT, one exact-geometry capture PCR, 1.8x AMPure, 200-300 bp E-Gel and X Ten PE150. Derived TES- and TSS-end molecules carry computed Nextera landings. The page shows the discrepancy between the Fig. 1a/Results claim of UMI+CI on both ends and the Methods TSS tag, which has no UMI or barcode. Deferred: Smart-seq2 Nat. Protoc. (unavailable), Nextera XT kit, PCR cycles, 10x application.

scrica-seq__10.1038+s41467-024-52335-0 — checked with corrections. Read the published PMC11399327 Methods (lines 151-162 low-throughput, 170-192 high-throughput), Supplementary Data 2 (MOESM5) and Supplementary Fig. 3 as a rendered image of MOESM1 p. 4. The figure fixes the molecular order: the amplified strand is handle-N6-N12-tag1-T30VN-cDNA-CCC-TSO complement, and NEBuilder joins the TSO-complement end to the handle end, so the circle closure and the printed linker were re-derived; the earlier closure between tag1 and the TSO complement was a repository modelling error. Added the missing ExoI step, the oligo + TSO ISPCR cDNA PCR with exact geometry, the ExoI/ExoIII:Lambda linear digestion and the two AMPure size cuts, and turned the TSS/TES PCR1 and PCR2 products into exact states with exact primer geometry, which places the cell barcode and UMI on the Read 2 side. Reads are PE150 with unstated index cycles; TTE Mix adapters and the cited-method (ref. 11) ATAC branch stay inferred. Deferred: the 10x Multiome branch, ref. 11, the loaded-adapter oligos and the pipeline TES length conflict.

scsnv-seq__10.1186+s13059-024-03169-y — checked with corrections. Read the published PMC10789043 Methods lines 115 and 119 and Additional file 3 Table S2, whose appended pKLV2 region publishes the transcribed layout. The guide library now carries the vector G, 19-nt spacer, scaffold loop, 6-nt iBAR and scaffold up to the oJR160 priming site instead of one 26-nt guide placeholder, and the PuroR library the NeoUTR3 5' UTR, 12-nt barcode and PuroR start; both gene-specific RT-primer arms are checked to be reverse complements of those stretches. Both nested libraries moved to the amplicon constructor with a 10-base i5 index, since the printed nested forward primer is exactly P5 + 10N + TruSeq Read 1, and carry regional PCR geometry for the undisclosed reverse partner; the genotype library's 16-base cell barcode is now inferred because the Mission Bio length is unpublished. A suite that had no checks now has seven transcription checks. Deferred: the 5' HT kit guide revision, the Tapestri manual, the authors' code and the i7-side primer; the historical blocked status stands.

scSPLAT (scsplat__10.1101+2021.10.14.464375): published Sci Rep 2022 (PMC8986790) Methods line 131 and Table S1 read; preprint superseded. The ss1a+ss2b vs ss1b name discrepancy stays on the page. Deferred: SPLAT 2017 NAR, snmC-seq2 protocol, NEBNext primer document, index cycles. Checks: chemistry 2/3/11/0; source guard 2 exact (ss1a, ss1b) checked.
