SEC-seq is `checked_with_corrections`: published Methods lines228–272 and MOESM1 Supplementary Table 2 / Fig. 8 inspected. The front end now separates nanovial loading with strainer removal, 37 °C secretion accumulation, PE anti-IgG plus TotalSeq-B0911 anti-PE staining and the SYTOX Green-negative WOLF sort, and names the capture, blocking and detection reagents from the supplement. Suite 5/5, page, pairing and Markdown pass; chemistry has 15 unresolved claims and zero contradictions; the source guard checks zero exact inputs with eleven unrepresented. The anti-PE barcode sheet, author code repository, 5′ v2 validation guide and the 80:20 pool remain deferred.

SELECT-seq (notes-only) is `checked`: the defining bioRxiv v1 Methods lines615–813, Supplementary Table 1 sheets and the cited Smart-seq3 v3 oligo list were read. The note now records the modified multiplex PCR concentrations and 35 cycles, the unpurified-product Cas12a detection mix and its inactivation, the AMPure/Qubit/tagmentation/14-cycle index route to Element Aviti PE150, the inherited Smart-seq3 v3 oligos verbatim, and the separation of the Nextera-tailed genotyping amplicon libraries from the single-cell library. Markdown rebuilt and the review schema passes; there is no module, page or suite to run. Smart-seq3 v3 body steps, vendor reporter/enzyme documentation, the code repository and per-well index members stay deferred.

**Seq-Scope** — `checked_with_corrections`. Read the published PMC8238917 STAR Methods lines 211–285, the EcoRI UMI array 289–323 and analysis 341–343; the scraper hits all match the module. The generic dual-index TruSeq shell is replaced by a final library computed from the printed oligos: P5–TR1–HDMI–HR1–30T–transcript–N9–TR2–i7–P7, single-indexed. The graph now follows the source: 1st-Seq Read1-DraI, DraI/Exo I/NaOH array processing, tissue release, array-primed Maxima RT, Exo I, proteinase K, NaOH strip, Klenow N9 second strand, NaOH elution, two PCR rounds with exact geometry, gel/SPRI, with waste fates. The page shows that the paper labels the seed ends 'P5'/'P7' although the bases are P7/revcomp(P5). Deferred: the gated published supplements, on-instrument clustering, the i7 read length, and the 30-of-32 HDMI32 bases. Checks: suite and page pass; pairing 0; chemistry 1/1/20/0; source guard 4 exact checked, 0 failed, 3 symbolic.

**Seq-Well** — `checked_with_corrections`. Read Supplementary Table 1 (MOESM237 lines 260–300), the Master Protocol V1.0 (MOESM238 lines 470–860) and the Online Methods pointer (supplement-1 lines 17–23). Oligo transcriptions are confirmed. The graph now carries the protocol's sealing, lysis/hybridization, bead recovery, Maxima RT/TSO conditions, washes, Exo I, KAPA WTA 4+9–12 cycles, 0.6× SPRI, Nextera XT 750 pg tagmentation, 12-cycle P5-SMART/N70X PCR and final SPRI. The fate of tagmented fragments without the bead end changed from a false 'discard' to an explicit unresolved fate, citing the source's selective P5-SMART priming. Deferred: the Protocol Exchange 2016 protocol, the Macosko bead synthesis, and the unavailable Nextera XT/NextSeq custom-primer guides. Checks: suite 5/5, seq-well-s3 4/4, page passes, pairing 0, chemistry 3/1/13/0, source guard 3 exact checked, 0 failed, 3 symbolic.

sf4sU-Seq (sf4su-seq__10.1093+nar+gkag652): published PMC13309788 Methods122–124, Fig.2D legend168, Results178 and Supplementary Methods297–455/Fig.4C were re-read against the declaration, along with SRX11439177 run statistics. The capture front end, gel/dialysis, RppH/EDTA stop and E7300 oligos (manual961–1070) were unchanged. The size-window discrepancy now drops the superseded bioRxiv values and cites published ≈100 nt beside the GEO 50–100/70–100 bp records. A new page discrepancy shows Results 'sequenced from both ends' against the SINGLE 75-nt deposited layout. The model keeps Read 1=75. Still deferred: the undeposited run layout, the excision window, the study kit revision/index and the dimer fate.

SNU-Seq (snu-seq__10.1093+nar+gkag652): published PMC13309788 Methods118–120 and Supplementary Methods306–429 were re-read, along with Lexogen Ion Appendix E and FWD Appendix G. Kit arms and read layouts match chem.py. Superseded bioRxiv v1/v2 were removed as page authorities, and all displayed route values now cite the 2026 publication. The post-PAP precipitation names the published sodium acetate/GlycoBlue. Capture/DTT/miRNeasy and the Dölken wash-salt discrepancy were unchanged. Still deferred: the study guide revisions/members, the per-sample platform assignment and native-end reach.

SHAPE-Seq (shape-seq__10.1073+pnas.1106501108) — checked with corrections. Read the published PNAS PMC3131332 Results1268–1272 and Methods1362–1365, and the CaltechAUTHORS SI pp.1–2 with Fig. S1 and Table S1. Two model mistakes fixed: A_adapter_t had a duplicated ACAC (62 nt instead of the 58-nt SI sequence), and the RNA bar code was drawn at the A_adapter_b end. SI text, Fig. S1 and Table S1 place it between the 3′ linker and the RT-primer site, so Read 1 reads handle, RT primer, then bar code. Added NaOH hydrolysis, plus CircLigase/C3, AMPure and 9-or-12-cycle details. Deferred: Mortimer 2009 RT-primer/RT protocol, Ingolia 2009 PCR primers, Merino 2005 cassette. Checks: suite/page pass, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/6/0, source guard 0 exact (4 symbolic inputs).

SHARE-seq (share-seq__10.1016+j.cell.2020.09.056) — checked with corrections. Read the published PMC7669735 JATS STAR Methods 211–261 and the publisher Table S1 workbook (bulk oligos, Ad1 and round plates). Every model oligo matches Table S1. Major model correction: both final libraries had been drawn as generic TruSeq/Nextera dual-index constructs with the cell barcodes inside the insert. They are now Ad1(P5–i5–S5)–insert–ME_RC–S7 anchor–three-round chain–P7. The computed Index 1 span is exactly the published 99 cycles, and RNA Read 2 reads the UMI then dT (published TTTTTT check at 11–16). Added the on-bead cDNA PCR and Read1-only cDNA tagmentation; Tn5 off-products are now unresolved fates rather than discards. Deferred: gated PMC supplements, protocols.io v2.2, sequencing-primer identity, cleanups and gap fill. Checks: suite/page pass, pairing 0, chemistry 1/0/21/0, source guard 0 exact (19 symbolic inputs).

SIMPLE-seq (notes-only): main Nat Biotechnol article remains paywalled and absent from PMC. Read the publisher oligo workbook (Table1_1 to _1_4) and the authors' linked GitHub repo, whose Protocol_SIMPLE-seq_Feb2024.pdf (commit 13e187f; header mislabelled Paired-Damage-seq) gives a bench step order: Tn5 with DNA_#/pMENTs, two splint-ligation rounds, ExoI, 3' bridge-adapter ligation, K2RuO4/malononitrile 5hmC labelling, P7-indicator extension, mTET1CD + pic-borane, U+ PCR. Note now carries corrected citation, verbatim modified oligos, computed interlocks and labelled bench order. Open: protocol vs workbook oligo names (Linker-R03/Quencher-R03), DNA_# 5' phosphate, indicator strand assignment, read lengths. Deferred: main Methods and predecessor chemistries.

Single-stranded ancient DNA: main 2013 Nature Protocols article remains paywalled. Inspected the rendered Supplementary Fig. 1 (ssDNA double-index library with CL72/Read 4 arrows), Supplementary Table 2 P5/P7 indexing families, and the linked 2017 NAR supplement Table 2 (CL9, CL53-ddC, Pho-CL73 with phosphorothioates, Pho-CL78-C3x10-TEG-biotin, marked as published in 2013). Model corrections: CL78-derived adaptor start replaces a false 'dA junction'; CL53/CL73 blunt geometry uses real bases; the pre-PCR product no longer carries final-library arms; CL72 and index-2 primers are marked as inferred from Fig. 1 and the unsupported 'Gesaffelstein' name is removed; Key oligos are listed; the CL78 phosphate correction is shown as a graph discrepancy. Deferred: main Procedure, correction notice, reaction conditions.

**site-seq__10.1038+nmeth.4284 — checked with corrections.** Read the published Supplementary Protocol (MOESM94 lines 33-515, every procedure section) and Supplementary Table 26 (MOESM93 lines 12-27); the defining Nature Methods article remains paywalled (author mirror HTTP 403). The final library's distal end is now sliced from the published oligos rather than from the canonical INDEX1_PRIMER constant, which exposed two faults: the 15-base Adapter 2 constant arm was missing, and the published Index Reverse primer writes ...GAACTCCAGTC-[i7]-TA-P7', placing the i7 index two bases closer to the Read 2 arm than stock TruSeq so that the stock Index 1 sequencing primer has no exact site (now declared mismatching, with a Highlight, caveat and note section). Adapter 2 ligation was changed from blunt to a derived 3'-T cohesive junction, and the Proteinase K/RNase A quench plus the SPRI 2 cleanup with its residual-adapter waste branch were added, along with the named enzymes, temperatures and cycle counts. Deferred: the article full text, the i7 index-read primer and acquired index cycles, and whether the end-repair module also dA-tails. Suite 3 checks pass, page builds, pairing 0 problems, chemistry 13 unresolved, source guard 10 exact oligos checked with 0 failures.

**slide-dna-seq__10.1038+s41586-021-04217-4 — checked with corrections.** Read Supplementary Methods 1.5-1.6 (MOESM1 lines 100-131) and the full Supplementary Table 1 oligo sheet (MOESM3 lines 3-18). Assembling the final strand from the printed oligos instead of partly retyping it exposed three missing bases at the Tn5 adapter 1 handle (GAG, present in the adapter but not in the Read 1 primer) and twelve missing bases contributed by the PCR reverse primer; with both restored, the protocol's standing open question is resolved: index 1 is the exact reverse complement of the PCR reverse arm and reads the 8-cycle i7 index, while index 2 primes at the bead-handle/Tn5 junction and reads backwards across the spatial barcode, which accounts exactly for the published 44-cycle index 2 read (5+7+18+7+7). Index 2 is now declared a spatial-barcode read rather than a sample index, the sequencing limitation notice is gone, and the bead ligation is declared splinted by the bridge oligo instead of unavailable. Deferred: the Buenrostro 2015 PCR cycle number, the unpublished index bases, and the TTTT-PCT bead spacer. Suite 3 checks pass, page builds, pairing 0 problems, chemistry 6 unresolved, source guard 5 exact oligos checked with 0 failures and 2 declared exceptions.

slide-seq__10.1126+science.aaw1219 — deferred (published supplement unobtainable). Read PMC6927209 author-manuscript JATS (64–70, 137: methods delegated to supplement) and, as the only readable detailed method, bioRxiv v1 media-1 Supplementary Methods (beads, puck sequencing, library prep 97–179, Table S1 rendered p.22). Graph corrected from RT→tagmentation to RT+TSO, proteinase K digestion, bead wash, SMART/handle cDNA PCR (4+9), 0.6x AMPure, Nextera XT, Truseq5+N7xx 12 cycles. Page caveat records preprint provenance; discrepancy notice shows Table S1 TSO AAGCTGG… vs SMART primer AAGCAGTGG… and Slide-seqV2 Table 6 TSO with the same Qiagen order number. Deferred: Science supplement (403), PMC docx (gate), Drop-seq beads, SOLiD SR-75 chemistry.

slide-seqv2__10.1038+s41587-020-0739-1 — checked with corrections. Read PMC8606189 JATS Online Methods (beads, bead synthesis, puck sequencing, library prep 195–333) and Springer MOESM1 Table 6. Model corrections: final library redrawn as the printed Truseq5 x Nextera N7xx endpoint with the J8–constant–J7–UMI8–T30 bead layout and 42/8/50 reads (previously TruSeq both ends, J15 ahead of the constant, Nextera i5/i7). Graph now carries decoding, 52C RT+TSO, digestion, washes, ExoI, NaOH, Klenow dN-SMRT second strand, Terra 4+9 PCR, double 0.6x AMPure, Nextera XT, Truseq P5+N7xx and final AMPure. Discrepancy shown: vs2 bead 8+6 J vs 15 split-pool cycles. Deferred: gated PMC attachments, Supp. Fig. 1 geometry, predecessors, ExoI purpose.

Slide-tags (checked_with_corrections). Read published MOESM1 Supplementary Methods 1.3–1.6 (lines 63–92), published JATS Methods 224/238/242 and the CG000317 Rev C appendix. MOESM1 1.5 says TAGS spatial libraries were prepared as Cell Surface Protein libraries. The 3′ tag library is therefore now the derived reverse complement of the printed TAGS oligo inside the CG000317 shell, with construction-time complementarity checks. It replaces an inferred layout with reversed puck pieces. Read 2 reads J8, the printed 18-nt linker, then J7, so the author parser leaves out the seventh J7 base. Added FB1/splint/poly(A) oligos. Deferred: SLAC custom-PCR primer bases, Multiome puck design/junction, Slide-seqV2/Slide-TCR originals, acquired read lengths.

Small-seq (checked_with_corrections, presentation only). Read the 2016 Online Methods (810–885) and Supplementary Table 2 (6060–6331) against the 2018 Reagent setup, Steps 10–16 and 36–38, and Supplementary Table 1. The oligo bases and modifications are the same; 2016 labels the mask TEG-biotin. The 2016 version used 48 six-base INDEX-RPI primers, 43/51-bp reads and Zymo pooling without size selection. Both are recorded on the page and in the note as published-version differences. The model's reactions match the 2018 steps. The page and graph limitation now say the 8-cycle index length is the barcode length, not a printed acquired cycle count. Deferred: Hafner/Munafo/Landgraf supplements, loaded instrument primers, mask target reference.

smart-seq__10.1038+nbt.2282 — checked_with_corrections. Read PMC3467340 Methods111–121 and Results73, published MOESM11 Suppl Fig1 and MOESM12 Suppl Table1. The Tn5 branch is a modified Epicentre Nextera protocol, not Nextera XT. Added preamp cycles 12/15/18, 55C 5min tagmentation with HMW buffer, a PB-strip/SPRI 1:1.6 cleanup with waste, and 9-cycle standard Nextera PCR with unprinted primers labelled representative. A page Highlight records the undrawn PE branch (which produced the figure data) and that Suppl Table1 gives only read lengths (50 GAIIx/40 MiSeq), with no read mode or index reads. Deferred: PT5163-1 kit manual, Epicentre Nextera guide, acquired reads, PE branch.

smart-seq2__10.1038+nmeth.2639 — deferred (article paywalled; supplements read). Read Suppl Tables 2–5 and the MOESM299 item list. The graph now names SuperScript II with 1M betaine/12mM MgCl2, the rGrG+G TSO at 1µl 10µM, KAPA HiFi HotStart without pre-amp purification, and the Nextera DNA kit FC-121-1031 plus Nextera 24-index kit FC-121-1011 (not XT). The read plan is now Read1 + dual index with no Read2, derived from the costed single-read dual-index primer kit, and a Highlight states its basis. The AC/ACAT TSO discrepancy is unchanged. Deferred: article/Online Methods, Nat Protoc companion, Illumina kit guides, SMARTer reagent identity, read length.

smart-seq3 — published HTML is a subscription preview (line627); read published Supp Table1 (MOESM3, all 96 rows), Supp Note I (MOESM1 14–50) and author protocol V.3 (bcq4ivyw, pp2–20). The page graph is now declared locally in tools/build_page.py with Maxima H− lysis/RT conditions, KAPA asymmetric preamp (exact Fwd/Rev states), both 0.6:1 PEG-bead cleanups, ATM tagmentation 55C, SDS strip, Phusion gap fill and 12-cycle custom-index PCR; molecular states remain shared SMART helpers. Note corrected (Maxima replaces Superscript II, not MMLV). No published discrepancy. Deferred: published Methods text, run cycles, index members. Verification: suite 197, page, pairing 0, chemistry 6/7/9/0, source guard 2 exact/0 failed/5 symbolic, Markdown, schema.

smart-seq3xpress — read published PMC9546772 Methods (JATS 70–100) and author protocol v1 (yxmvmk1yng3p, RT/SeqAmp 690–960, tagmentation/index PCR 1130–1290, pooling 1990–2025). smMIP (smmip__10.1101+gr.147686.112): read published Hiatt 2013 PMC3638140 JATS Methods lines139–163, checked the supplement ZIP and PMC Cloud listing (no Table S9), and Cantsilieris et al. 2017 PMC5484527 front matter, Materials and Notes 8/15–18. Corrected the 2017 protocol's attribution (previously O'Roak). Its 70-mer, 5–8-nt-tag design differs from the 2013 panel, so the drawn PCR handles and primers are now INFERRED later-protocol stand-ins. Graph now names PNK/Phusion/Ampligase/ExoI+III/Phusion HS II with source conditions and adds 26-cycle PCR, 1.8x Ampure and the PAGE ~280 bp excision with waste. HiSeq 2x101+8 drawn; MiSeq 2x152+8 stated. Table S9 still behind Cloudflare; predecessors deferred. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 0/1/8/0; source guard 0 exact checked.

smol-seq (smol-seq__10.1038+s41587-025-02554-7): read published PMC12807852 JATS Main lines32/38/51 and Methods 61–89, plus Supplementary Data 1 lines56–75. Replaced the generic role schematic with an exact mefloquine barcode-1 model: biotin-LBO:SRO duplex (Scene-checked stem), streptavidin immobilization, ligand-driven release, and a sequenced 95°C boiled remainder. Each branch then has cleanup, two PCR rounds with source primers (14/11 then 8 cycles), NucleoMag 1.2x and MiSeq 50-bp SE. N5–7 kept as diversity bases. Index lengths and the Read 1 primer are INFERRED. No published discrepancy found. Deferred: predecessor SSA sources, polymerase/spike-in sequences, index cycles. Suite/page/pairing pass; chemistry 0/0/18/0; source guard 3 exact checked, 0 failed.

snapTotal-seq (checked_with_corrections). Read published PMC11251146 Methods lines 246–270 and Supplementary Data 12 (MOESM15). Rebuilt the final library so Read 2 reads barcode 1–8, a 35-nt primer spacer, then the UMI at 44–48, as the Methods state; removed a false dA junction and the whitelist claim. Removed an undescribed end-repair step. The second DSC is now an extension, not a discard. Added GAT27 PCR, Adapter-PCR, DSN, enrichment and exact Truseq-P7/P5 final PCRs. All oligos are verbatim with exact states (guard 11/0). Page notice: custom Read 2/i7 primers (AGAGGTGA) disagree with the cell barcode primer (AGGAGAGT) before GAT27. SNARE-seq (checked_with_corrections). Read published PMC6893138 Methods lines 56–76, Table S4 (all rows), the MOESM1/MOESM2 supplements and Drop-seq main-text pointers. The chromatin library is now derived exactly from Table S4. RNA ends beyond the bead handle are inferred. Read layout follows Methods line 72, including HP10 Index 1 genomic and 8-bp Index 2 (source-declared table). Guard 8/0. Deferred: Drop-seq Table S6/EEP, HP10/HP11 contents, Index 2 route.

**Spatial-ATAC-seq** — checked with corrections. Read published Deng 2022 Nature JATS Methods 213–235 and Supp Table 3 (MOESM5 oligos/barcodes), plus Farzad 2024 Supp_Table 1–3/Steps 16–24. The shared spatial_epigenome construct omitted the Tn5ME-A transferred strand; a local construct now derives linker 1 as the splint of barcode-A ME and the CATCGGCGTACGACT Tn5ME-A extension, moving the Read 1 genomic start from cycle 81 to 100. Graph gained fixation, EDTA stop, ligation conditions, Zymo/AMPure cleanups, exact New-P5-SMART/Ad2 PCR geometry and unresolved same-adapter candidates. Page displays the Deng 2022 vs Farzad 2024 Tn5ME-A discrepancy. Deferred: author preprocessing repo, Diagenode loading guide, Ad2 assignment/index cycles. Suite 4/4, pairing 0, chemistry 1/0/9/0, source guard 9 exact/0 failed.

**Spatial-CUT&Tag** — deferred (defining Science supplementary methods DOCX unavailable; main-text JATS read). Read Farzad 2024 Supp_Table 1–3 and CUT&Tag Steps 36–60/105–121 plus the authors' repository README. The shared construct omitted the pA-Tn5 transferred adapter; the local construct derives from linker 1 that the adapter must begin CATCGGCGTACGACT + ME and draws both as inferred. Page shows that Farzad Supp_Table 1 Tn5ME-A cannot pair with its own linker 1. Graph gained antibody/pA-Tn5 binding and washes, activation/EDTA, PCR program with exact New-P5-SMART/Ad2 geometry, AMPure and unresolved candidates. Deferred: Science DOCX, pA-Tn5 loading oligos, fixation/lysis steps, Ad2 assignment. Suite 3/3, pairing 0, chemistry 1/0/9/0, source guard 6 exact/0 failed.

SPEAC-seq — the defining Nature Protocols text, previously recorded as unobtainable, was fetched as PMC author manuscript PMC11805652 and read (Oligonucleotides 153-175; droplet breakage and gRNA amplification 636-689; library prep 691-705). It prints both ordering oligos, so the note's 🔴 'primers unpublished' claim is withdrawn: nine P5 staggered species and the indexed P7 primer are now verbatim in the module, 18 of them checked by the source guard. The reaction graph carries the real steps — 20% PFO droplet break, Qiagen gDNA, AMPure 2x, one 28-35 cycle ExTaq PCR that installs both Illumina ends, AMPure 2x, Bioanalyzer/KAPA/4 nM pooling, depth-only sequencing target. Two published inconsistencies are displayed on the page: stagger labels that do not match the bases actually inserted (plus a spaced, A-truncated '0 nt' row kept inferred and an '8 nt' row with a different 3' end), and a P7 '5 mM' stock that contradicts the table's own 0.5 uM arithmetic. PCR geometry remains unavailable because the lentiCRISPRv2 sequence is not printed; the Addgene map, the typeset version and the gated supplements are named deferrals.

Spear-ATAC — read the published Methods (PMC8137922 lines 100-124), Supplementary Notes 1 and 3 (MOESM1 2323-2502, 2590-2721) and the Supplementary Data 8 primer table (MOESM11 rows 7-11). Three real errors were fixed. The guide-library constant regions flanking the spacer existed only on the scg_lib_structs drawn page and in no original; the barcode-to-oMCB1672 stretch is now a placeholder and the post-spacer region is derived from oSP2053. The indexed P7 primer had been modelled with the full TruSeq Read 2 although the source prints only its first 22 bases. And the enrichment does not split a pre-branch pool: Note 3 takes 2.5 uL of the finished ~150 nM scATAC library, linearly amplifies with biotinylated oSP2053 for 25 cycles, MinElutes, captures on Streptavidin MyOne C1 in B&W buffer, runs 15-cycle PCR2 off the beads and keeps the supernatant, so the sequenced molecules are the new indexed copies while the biotinylated strands leave with the beads. oSP1735 is now named as the GEM-spiked backbone primer, the cell-barcode index read is marked as an extracted identifier, and the pSP618 interior (GenBank MW852482) and 10x scATAC v1 vendor oligos are named deferrals.

SPLASH (deferred). The defining 2016 Molecular Cell main article is still unreadable (cell.com PDF 403; the PMC5608146 PMC Cloud row is the 2017 JoVE record). Read: the original supplement (Fig S2F/G, Supplemental Experimental Procedures) and the JoVE protocol §5–9 with its HTML Materials table. Corrections: added NaOH RNA hydrolysis and the 200–240 nt urea-gel cDNA selection before CircLigase II, and named SuperScript III, T4 Rnl2 truncated KQ, T4 Rnl1 and Phusion HF. Photoreversal is now 254 nm, and the page shows the JoVE discrepancy between §6.1 (254 nm) and the Fig 1 legend (265 nm). Deferred: the main article, the circle/amplicon/read geometry, the other gel selections and the E7335L lot index.

SPLiT-seq (checked with corrections). Read the published main text (PMC7643870 JATS 68–80, 284) and the original Table S12 workbook through its scg mirror. The methods supplement is still gated (Europe PMC: not open access; PMC bin: 403). Corrections: the round-2/3 splinted ligation geometry is now built from Table S12 bases, with each 30-nt linker pairing both full 15-nt arms across a /5Phos/ nick, and the page shows it. The page also shows a published discrepancy: the main text gives 24 round-4 PCRs and 14 brain sublibraries, but Table S12 prints eight i7 primers. The note drops a cross-protocol microSPLiT index claim and an upstream-based step attribution. Deferred: supplement conditions and read lengths, the index set, tagmentation geometry and TSO CCC timing.

The graph previously began at a pre-decoded array. It now draws chip generation: the oligo1 (CID) + /5phos/ oligo2 splint ligation (T4, 37 °C 2 h, known splinted Scene geometry), AMPure, a 12-cycle DNB F/R PCR (exact computed landings, polymerase unnamed), a warned RCA/loading boundary per the MGI DNBSEQ-Tx manual, and SE25 CID decoding with the CID primer. Capture adds methanol fixation and pepsin permeabilization; final AMPure is 0.6×/0.15×. Existing oligo1 and poly(T) discrepancy notices kept. SUM-seq is `checked_with_corrections`. Read: published PMC12165849 Methods lines 398–427 and MOESM3 Table 1a–d. The graph had split nuclei into RNA and ATAC branches before RT. It now follows the published order on intact nuclei: transposition, RT, pooling, hybrid tagmentation, gap fill/ExoI, 10x linear barcoding with the invdT blocker, cleanups, preamp, split, then modality PCR. The final constructs are now assembled from Table 1 oligos, and the 10x bead regions and library index are marked inferred. Illumina run lengths are declared. Suite 13/13, page, pairing and Markdown pass. Chemistry has 17 unresolved claims and 0 contradictions. The source guard checked 12 exact oligos with 0 failures; the bead oligo has no exact sequence. Deferred: the 10x guide, library-index readout and the predecessor methods.

SureCell WTA 3' is `checked_with_corrections`. Read: Reference Guide 1000000021452 v01 from the RT program through Prepare for Sequencing. The invented second-strand primer and the separate RNA-removal step were replaced by the guide's single SSB/SSE reaction. Added the emulsion breaking, cDNA clean-up, tagmentation stop and both library SPB binds. Empty-droplet and unselected-fragment outcomes now have unresolved fates instead of physical discards. Suite (documented skip), page, pairing and Markdown pass. Chemistry has 11 unresolved claims and 0 contradictions. The source guard has no exact inputs because all kit oligos are inferred. Deferred: SP/TPP1/bead bases, the three unchecked instrument primer guides and run lengths.

SurePlex: re-read Illumina SurePlex Summary Protocol 15053626 v02 (lysis, 12-cycle preamplification, 14-cycle amplification, lines 475–789) and VeriSeq PGS Library Prep 15052877 v05 (tagmentation through pooling, lines 880–1776, printed pp. 9–19). The graph stopped at index PCR. This pass added the source-stated AMPure XP 1:1 clean-up, which discards short fragments and primers, and LNA1/LNB1 bead normalization with NaOH elution, which yields the single-stranded normalized library and discards unbound library. The note was updated to match. Still deferred: the proprietary preamplification primers, the LNB1 capture chemistry, the undrawn single-index branch and the Read 1 cycle split. Suite, page, pairing (0) and chemistry (0/0/9/0) checks pass; the source guard checked 0 oligos and found 0 contradictions, with 3 inputs lacking exact states.

The declaration is unchanged and the earlier 2026-10-16 checklist readings stand as history. Hand download of G9957-90000 B0 (manual URL in MANIFEST) is the named follow-up, along with the HS2 adaptor and primer arms and the fragmentation, blocker and cleanup steps. Suite (98/98), page, pairing (0) and chemistry (0/0/9/0) checks pass; the source guard checked 0 oligos, with 4 inputs lacking exact states.

tip-seq__10.1083+jcb.202103078 — checked_with_corrections. Read published PMC8600797 Methods 165–195, Table S1 and Table S2, and verified all Table S1 oligo constants. Found a published Index 2 discrepancy. Table S1 prints a P5-sequence i5 read primer (forward strand: i5 first). The Methods state r5 at index-2 cycles 1–6 and i5 at 21–29 on NovaSeq SP v1.5. The model now uses an RC-workflow Index 2 primer (r5 1–6, i5 22–29), keeps the Table S1 primer as a source oligo, and shows the discrepancy on the page. RNase H digestion and named enzymes were added. Deferred: protocols.io CUT&Tag, review history, predecessors, bulk branch, SPRI/GuHCl/size-selection steps. Selftest 5/5, page, pairing 0, chemistry 3/3/8/0, source guard 4 exact/0 failed, Markdown and review schema pass.

TIRTL-seq (tirtl-seq__10.1038+s41592-025-02907-9): checked with corrections. Read the PMC12791010 Methods lines 214–226 and the four sheets of Supplementary Data 1. The protocols.io 96-well V.2 was used only for steps 49–50. The C-segment primers carry the Nextera Read 1 arm and the plate barcode; the V primers carry Read 2. This fixes a reversed plate-barcode orientation in the model. The single well-UDI is split into grouped i5/i7 features. Source RT/PCR reagents and cycles were added, plus the pooled 1X AMPure cleanup. The plate-ID window is now the computed variable positions 34–40, because the 6-or-8-nt boundary is not marked. No read length is assigned, since the Methods give only NovaSeq at 300k reads/well. Deferred: read and index cycles, the barcode boundary, RT primer sequences, and the full protocols.io comparison.

TrAEL-seq (trael-seq__10.1371+journal.pbio.3000886): checked with corrections. Read the PMC8021198 Results lines 62–71, Methods lines 199–225 and the S2 File protocol (s009, lines 20–289). Corrected model errors: TdT/ATP rA tailing is added before the RNA ligase 2 KQ ligation of adapter 1, along with plug washes and agarase release. Streptavidin capture now comes before on-bead End Prep, adapter-2 ligation, homodimer washes and USER elution. Adapter 2 is drawn with its published single 3′ T overhang and the dA marked inferred; a page caveat shows the paper's 'blunting' wording. Read 1 now reads UMI, then the T tract, then the genomic complement. Read 2 is not acquired (single-end). E7335S is shown only as the paper's example index set. Deferred: the Ultra II vendor manual, the index actually used, the lab-website protocols, END-seq predecessor and the barcoded-adapter variant.

**TruePrime** (`trueprime__10.1038+ncomms13296`) — checked with corrections. Read published Picher 2016 PMC5141293 JATS Results 72–78 and Methods 132–156, Supplementary Fig. 2 legend (s1 lines 211–216) and ENA run metadata; scraper hits are assay/predecessor reagents only. The page had attached the Methods NEBNext/HiSeq 2500 sequencing to the purified-DNA branch; Results and ENA tie it to the single-cell/NA samples. The only purified-DNA sequencing is Supp. Fig. 2 (6 h, Ion Torrent, prep undescribed), now a warned MAGIC library step with the 3 h vs 6 h scope on the page. Denaturation volumes and source added. Deferred: Ion Torrent prep, 2016 kit handbook revision, M13 RCA branch, Dean 2001. Suite 2/2, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/5/0, source guard 0 checked, Markdown, review schema pass.

**TruePrime Single Cell** (`trueprime-single-cell__10.1038+ncomms13296`) — checked with corrections. Read published Picher 2016 PMC5141293 JATS Results 78, Methods Single-cell WGA 152 and Sequencing 156, and the SYGNIS v2.0 handbook lines 240–276/331–375. Verified L2 lysis for 10 min on ice, Buffer N, the 42.5 µl TthPrimPol/phi29 mix, 3 or 6 h at 30 °C and 65 °C inactivation. The handbook's RT+95 °C lysis difference stays on the page. Added the source-stated ethanol precipitation cleanup before Covaris fragmentation, which is now source-stated, not inferred. Recorded HiSeq 2500 v4 at GATC with 125 bp paired reads. Deferred: NEBNext kit/adapters/primer sites, handbook revision, shearing/precipitation settings, Dean 2001. Test 1/1, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/8/0, source guard 0 checked, Markdown, review schema pass.

TRIP — the source bundle did not exist before this pass; get_sources.py fetched five Springer supplements and left the full text as a (manual) paywalled row. Supplementary Figure 3 was read base by base and the two drawn libraries were corrected to it: the counting amplicon runs through a 43-nt 3′ eGFP stretch before the PB-cDNA-reverse site, and the mapping construct carries TTAA plus the CC that opens the piggyBac 3′ TR before the inverse-PCR primer site; the expression/normalization library now reproduces panel a exactly. Supplementary Table 1 verified all 48 indexed forward primers, so that reagent now carries an exact state and the source guard checks it (1 checked, 0 failed). The note's former green claim to have read the main protocol text is corrected to red with the manual URL, and the page caveat states which oligos are verified here. Remaining: the paywalled article (reagent list, cycling, read lengths), the unverifiable PB-barcode-long-7/BssH2-A/eGFP-int3 transcriptions, and the Supplementary Figure 1 chimeric-ligation off-products. Review status stays blocked; no absence claim accepted.

TruSeq DNA PCR-Free — re-read the v00 workflow guide end to end within the cap. Reaction order, reagents and conditions all confirmed (ERP 2/3 at 30 °C for 30 min; CTA + ATL/ATL 2 at 37 °C 30 min then 70 °C 5 min; CTL + LIG 2 + separately purchased adapters at 30 °C for 10 min; 5 µl STL stop; two SPB cleanup rounds; qPCR because non-qPCR methods count molecules that cannot cluster). The graph previously collapsed size selection into one step with one generic off-size discard; it now declares the guide's two sided rounds with their own waste terminals, the STL stop condition and the post-ligation cleanup step with its unbound-adapter waste. The note records the exact line ranges, the four purchasable index-adapter components and new open questions for insert length, index member and later guide revisions. No discrepancy between the workflow guide and Illumina Adapter Sequences; status stays reviewed and no new absence claim is made.

**truseq-stranded-mrna** — checked with corrections. Read the v00 (Oct 2017) Reference Guide overview 174–258 and procedure sections 716–1830 (fragmentation, first strand, SMM, A-tailing, ligation with two cleanup rounds, PCR, final AMPure) plus inline-control Table 2 2490–2567; checked the v22 CD adapter templates through scraper hits. Reaction order and the conditional CD endpoint were confirmed. Added the source-printed reaction conditions (94C 8 min fragmentation, RT program, SMM 16C 1 h, ATL 37/70C, LIG 30C 10 min, 15-cycle PCR program) to the graph and note. Deferred: PPC strands, historical adapter revision and D member, older pooling guide and supporting guides, platform and acquisition, LS branch and alternate fragmentation. Suite 10/10; page, pairing 0; chemistry 0/0/14/0; source guard 0 exact, 4 symbolic.

**tsa-seq__10.1083+jcb.201807108** — checked with corrections. Read the published JCB (PMC6219710) Results 86–102 and Methods 226–262, the KAPA Hyper Prep KR0961 v2.15 sheet (product description and ER & AT table) and supplement Table S1; the scraper found only false gene-symbol hits. Added the endogenous-peroxidase H2O2 quench step and the source conditions for antibody, tyramide/H2O2 labelling, crosslink reversal and streptavidin release. The unbound fraction is now labelled as mostly unlabelled DNA. End repair and A-tailing are merged into one KAPA ER & AT reaction. PCR is labelled KAPA HiFi, 8–16 cycles. Single-end reads and the 6-cycle index read are now marked inferred. Deferred: adaptor/primer identity and cleanups, read mode, no-primary/condition branches, Table S1 per-experiment rounds, Hopman 1998. Suite 1/1; page, pairing 0; chemistry 0/0/15/0; source guard 0 exact, 0 symbolic.

TT-seq (tt-seq__10.1126+science.aad9841): re-read the Schwalb 2016 supplement Methods lines 21–49, original Science p.1 lines 88–103 and Dölken 2008 capture Methods lines 942–971. Pulse, spike-in, split/fragment-before-capture, Biotin-HPDP/streptavidin/DTT capture, NuGEN kit and PE50+6 run were verified. Correction: the printed 2 U Turbo DNase treatment is now a graph digestion step before kit RT. The final library's dA junction is kept as an inferred TruSeq-style placeholder, now stated in the note. Scraper hits are reporter cloning primers. Deferred: NuGEN kit guide oligos/geometry, Read 1 strand and barcode index read, control arms. Suite, page, pairing, chemistry (0/0/10/0), source guard (0 exact) and Markdown pass.

TUC-seq (tuc-seq__10.1002+anie.201707465): the defining Riml 2017 full text and both SI routes stay paywalled/403 (manual URL https://doi.org/10.1002/anie.201707465). The PubMed abstract (PMID 28817234) is now in the bundle and was read: it gives OsO4-mediated 4sU-to-cytosine conversion, tRNA 4sU and seven selected genes. The model now names OsO4 in NH4Cl buffer on the recoding step. That reagent comes from the same authors' DUAL 2020 retrospective text (line 140) and is labelled that way on the page. Original conditions are left unassigned. Scraper hits are DUAL-only oligos. The preparation and library steps stay warned MAGIC boundaries. Deferred: original methods/SI, Lusser chapter and correction. Suite, page, pairing, chemistry (0/0/4/0) and Markdown pass.

txci-ATAC-seq (checked_with_corrections). Read the published Genome Biology JATS Methods lines189–221, Additional file 5 Tables S4/S5/S7/S8 and the authors' protocols.io PDF steps1–7 and 50–52. The graph now runs from the three Table S4 linker oligos through annealing and Tn5 loading. The Short SBS input has its exact sequence. Tagmentation, GEM PCR and SI-PCR conditions are added, plus the kit post-GEM cleanup and double-sided size selection. Non-amplifying A/A·B/B fragments and empty/multiplet-GEM fates are no longer shown as physically discarded. A sequencing highlight explains 8 vs 10 i7 cycles. Deferred: 10x CG000209 Rev D (bead oligo, kit P5 primer), Fig. S1c, undrawn brain/pilot/blocking/Phased branches. Checks: suite 4 pass, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/13/0, source guard 4 exact/0 failed/1 symbolic.

UDA-seq (checked_with_corrections). Read the published Nature Methods JATS Results149–155, Methods321–399 and preprocessing433/445, plus Supplementary Table 7 (MOESM8). The old shared-helper graph tagmented chromatin after in-droplet RT and put both barcodes inside Read 1. It is replaced by a local Multiome graph in Methods order, from tagmentation before loading to the separate ATAC and RNA library constructions. The round-2 well index now sits in i5 for RNA and i7 for ATAC. 10x regions are inferred, and MGISeq-T7 sequencing is a structured limitation. The page shows the Methods vs Table 7 primer-name discrepancy. Deferred: protocols.io refs 62/63, 10x kit guides, 'Partial P5' identity, MGI conversion, undrawn RNA-only/VDJ/CRISPR routes. Checks: suite 6 pass, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/27/0, source guard 6 exact/0 failed/3 symbolic.

**UDiTaS** (`uditas__10.1186+s12864-018-4561-9`) — checked with corrections. Sources: published PMC5861650 JATS (Methods, Table 2, pipeline) and Additional file 2 (sections 3–13, Appendix A). The section 13 MiSeq run sheet gives Read 1 300 / Index 1 8 / Read 2 300 / Index 2 18, and the article aligns paired reads. The model and note had said there was no Read 2; TruSeq Read 2 from the anchor tail is now declared. Appendix A prints the i5 read as barcode then UMI. Index 2 is therefore an explicit grafted-P5 forward-strand primer: barcode cycles 1–8, UMI 9–18. This replaces the earlier UMI-first reverse-complement description. The note gains source reaction conditions. Deferred: cleanup/BluePippin separations, exact round-1 intermediate, Tn5 expression original, MOESM3 image content, author code. Checks: suite 1/1, page, pairing 0, chemistry 0/0/5/0, source guard 0 exact (5 symbolic inputs), Markdown, review schema.

**wellDA-seq** (`wellda-seq__10.1101+2024.09.06.611519`) — checked with corrections. Sources: bioRxiv v1 Methods (Tn5 assembly, procedure Steps 1–3) and supplement ED Fig. 1 plus Tables 5–6; no published version is linked. The previous role-level model (one indexed Tn5, separation and WGA of residual genome) was not in the source. It is replaced by the source workflow: bulk custom Tn5-1, ICELL8 well selection, protease lysis, in-well Illumina Tn5-2, gap fill, one four-primer on-chip barcoding PCR, and pooled cleanup. Two modality enrichment PCRs follow, with exact ATAC/DNA libraries and landing ATAC custom primers. Discrepancies shown on the page: 'Suppl Table 4-5' vs Tables 5/6, and ATCACG drawn before P7 in ED Fig. 1a but absent from all N7XX primers. Deferred: read lengths, DNA sequencing primers/CB3 read, CB3 role, hybrid products, vendor originals. Checks: suite 10/10, page, pairing 0, chemistry 1/1/14/0, source guard 0 exact (8 symbolic inputs), Markdown, review schema.

**Yeast TeloTag** — checked with corrections. Read the published Genome Res PMC8997346 Methods (end tagging lines 185–193, nanopore prep 201–203, analysis 207) and Supplemental Table S5 (lines 676–709). The article was fetched into the slug source directory. The shared telomere recipe was replaced by a graph the protocol owns, with the printed TdT/dATP, TeloTag mix (five exact S5 oligos), Pol IV fill, T4 seal, AMPure cleanup, >10 kb Short Read Eliminator selection, a warned EXP-NBD104 barcoding boundary and SQK-LSK109 motor ligation. Read selection is recorded as computational. The preprint 5 vs 10 kb notice was dropped as an ordinary revision; the S6/S5 table error stays displayed. Deferred: ONT kit originals, tail length/phase pairing, the 2.5 mM concentration. Suite 101 pass; pairing 0; chemistry 0/0/7/0; source guard 5 exact, 0 failed.

**ZipSeq** — checked with corrections. Read the published PMC7891292 JATS oligo list (lines 118–206), tumor study (272) and Library Construction (276), and the cited CITE-seq 2017-07-03 protocol pp. 3–6. The graph no longer splits transcripts and zipcodes before RT. Both are RT'd in the GEM and co-amplified with the additive primer, then separated by SPRI supernatant and two 3X SPRI cleanups before CITE-seq PCR. Added caged-strand prehybridization, blocking-strand neutralization, the live Cy5+ sort and a warned 10x library-construction boundary. The tumor branch now uses the internal-Cy5 anchor. Deferred: CG000183 detail, additive-primer sequence and CITE-seq version, NPOM positions in the published PDF, RPI member/Read 2 entry. Suite 4 pass; pairing 0; chemistry 0/1/16/0; source guard 4 exact, 0 failed, 5 unsequenced inputs.

BotSeqS stays `deferred`. The PNAS SI Appendix and Dataset S1 are still unreachable: PMC returns a proof-of-work download gate (not solved), pnas.org and Europe PMC return Cloudflare 403, PMC Cloud has no copy (404), and Unpaywall/OpenAlex list nothing else. US 12,359,250 B2 is by the same group, and its Example 1 describes this study. It was OCR-read as a labelled lead. The post-dilution PCR now uses inferred TS-PCR Oligo1/Oligo2 (P5/P7 22-mers, one 3' phosphorothioate), Q5 and 18 cycles, with exact sites computed. The dilution input gains the pBlueScript-carrier serial scheme. The page caveat names the patent as the source. Chemistry 1 established/7 unresolved/0 contradictory; the guard checked 0 exact inputs (the inferred primers are excluded).

CapSeq stays `deferred`. The published Science text and Tables S21-S25 remain paywalled: there is no OA location, Europe PMC has no PMCID, bioRxiv has only v1, and the science.org supplement returns 403. The WO2026190526A1 PDF is unreachable (Google Patents 404, Espacenet 403). The GEO GSM9578506 record text was read and saved. Its raw Read 1 positions (UMI 4 + BC3 8 + linker + BC2 10 + linker + BC1 8 + UMI 4) end at cycle 43 and match GSM8830586's 34 informative bases; this is recorded only as a consistency check. GEO text conflicts on 11 versus 12 indexing cycles. Notes-only; no model change.

CEL-Seq (deferred follow-up 02). The defining 2012 Cell Reports article and its detailed CEL-Seq Protocol were obtained legitimately from the publisher's OA supplement CDN (ars.els-cdn.com …S2211124712002288-mmc5.pdf / -mmc4.pdf) after PMC, Europe PMC, the Elsevier API and cell.com/ScienceDirect (403 challenge) failed. All 40 printed RT primers match the model's spacer–T7–RA5–8-nt barcode–T24V design (minimum Hamming distance 2, computed). The page now names the TruSeq Small RNA kit reagents (RA3, RTP, RP1, RPIX), adds the phosphatase and PNK steps, the ligase/RT/PCR conditions, and the published read lengths (R1 ≥15, R2 50). Kit oligo bases are still not printed, so the outer arms stay placeholders. Deferred: the 2012 kit-guide revision and the MessageAmp II manual.

cfMeDIP-seq (deferred follow-up 02). The defining Nature Protocols Procedure is still paywalled. PMC/Europe PMC, Unpaywall/OpenAlex, Semantic Scholar, nature.com and a web search for author, protocols.io or Protocol Exchange copies all found no open copy (manual https://doi.org/10.1038/s41596-019-0202-2). The published abstract and the public Fig. 2 and Fig. 5 legend pages were read. They establish λ filler DNA (PCR amplicons, methylated and unmethylated) added before IP, spiked-in A. thaliana DNA, and the order library prep → IP → amplification (Steps 24–156). The controls input and the caveat were corrected. Summary-level end-repair, denaturation and paired-end claims were downgraded to unverified. The OICR SOP v2.5 is a later derivative recorded only as a lead. Adapter, index, PCR and read layout remain deferred.

CH-ATAC-seq (deferred follow-up 02): the defining Dev Cell 2024 article is still unread. It is open archive per Elsevier metadata, but every web route is blocked by Cloudflare 403 and the Elsevier API needs a key. The manual URL is https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S1534580724000352. Crossref shows the Research Square record rs-960203 is the CH-RNA-seq axolotl preprint, so it is no route to the CH-ATAC Methods; the note was corrected. GEO GSM7324821 gives DNBSEQ-T7 and puts the HY and Tn5 indices in one FASTQ, with no reaction conditions or read lengths. No model change. Status stays deferred until a hand download.

ChIL-seq (deferred follow-up 02): the defining Nature Protocols article remains paywalled. OpenAlex, Unpaywall, Europe PMC and protocols.io gave no author manuscript or repository copy; manual URL https://doi.org/10.1038/s41596-020-0375-8. Newly read: the 2024 Author Correction (Fig. 2c image only) and the Harada 2019 NCB predecessor's Supplementary Information plus Supplementary Table 4. These give the conjugation chemistry, default Tn5 dose, 37 °C integration, 0.5 M NaCl probe incubation and ~300 bp libraries. All are recorded as predecessor-only context in the note and the page caveat, with no graph change. Status stays deferred.

chip-seq__10.1038+nmeth1068 (deferred-followup-02): the main article is still unobtainable. OpenAlex and Unpaywall list it as closed, Europe PMC as subscription only, and nature.com shows only the abstract (manual https://doi.org/10.1038/nmeth1068). The nature.com page listed a second supplement, Supplementary Data 1 (MOESM306), which get_sources --slugs then fetched. Its README (lines 18-21) states '27 bp Illumina reads', so the read length is now sourced through FINAL_READ_LENGTH and checked by the selftest. Single-end reading, the adapter and primer bases, and the library conditions taken from the 2007 Illumina guide all stay inferred or assumed. Status stays deferred because the main Methods were not read.

cobatch__10.1016+j.molcel.2019.07.015 (deferred-followup-02): the final Mol Cell article is listed as bronze open access but sits behind a Cloudflare 403 on cell.com and ScienceDirect. The keyless Elsevier API returns metadata only and Europe PMC has no copy, so the STAR Methods remain unread. The authors' GEO records were read instead (GSE129327 series, GSM3705255 sample). They give the cell-splitting windows (first 21 bp of Read 1, first 27 bp of Read 2) and confirm KAPA PCR, AMPure 200-1000 bp and NovaSeq 6000. GEO_SPLIT_CYCLES is recorded with a model-derived overrun into the inner connectors, labelled as inference with no primer declared. Status stays deferred.

Drop-seq (deferred-followup-02): the published Cell Supplemental Information was obtained legitimately from the Elsevier CDN (mmc1.pdf; PMC copy still gated). Table S6 confirms the SeqB bead, TSO, TSO_PCR, P5-TSO_Hybrid and Read1CustomSeqB base for base and adds a SeqA bead (handle+ACGT) with Read1CustomSeqA and Nextera_N701-N703. The model now carries these as exact states (source guard 4 checked, 0 failed); only the untemplated CCC is inferred. SI conditions were attached to steps, and the page shows two published discrepancies (Read1CustSeqB 'read 2' vs 'read 1'; oligo naming). Index-read length remains unstated; second independent reading pending.

eccDNA Circle-Seq (deferred-followup-02): retrieval-only follow-up. The PNAS SI Appendix and Datasets S1-S3 remain unobtainable by legitimate routes (PMC JS download gate, no PMC Cloud keys, Europe PMC empty/500/403, PNAS Cloudflare 403, OpenAlex/Unpaywall list only PMC and a KU portal record without file). ENA PRJEB6368 metadata give no library protocol beyond 'eccDNA purification'. Current Takara PrepX documents were not used because the paper names no Apollo kit and they postdate the work. Model and page unchanged; status stays deferred with manual URLs recorded.

Fiber-seq (2026-10-17-deferred-followup-02): the Science Supplementary Materials are still unobtainable. The science.org SM URL and the Stergachis lab Science referrer link both return HTTP 403. There is no PMC, Europe PMC, OpenAlex repository, Harvard DASH or bioRxiv copy, and the Zenodo item in the article is analysis code. The GEO GSE146942 sample records, which the article's data statement links, were read instead. They give the 2020 nuclei buffer, Hia5 500/20 U for 10 min at 37 °C, shearing, a PCR-free PacBio library and Sequel/Sequel II. These values now label the graph inputs, and the caveat shows 37 °C against the current protocol's 25 °C as a version difference. Quench, extraction kit, shear device and SMRTbell kit still follow the current bench protocol. Status stays deferred.

G&T-seq (2026-10-17-deferred-followup-02): obtained the defining 2015 Online Methods as the accepted author manuscript on Zenodo (record 895966, reached from Europe PMC's free full-text link) and placed it in the bundle with a MANIFEST row. Read lines 309–367. The capture oligo biotin-TEG AAGCAGTGGTATCAACGCAGAGTAC-T30VN, the TSO …AGTACrGrG+G and the PCR primer are verified verbatim. So are the pooled washes, the on-bead SuperScript II RT at 42/50/60 °C, KAPA HiFi for 18 cycles, the AMPure ratios, GenomiPhi V2/PicoPlex and Nextera XT. Locators now point to this copy, and the enzyme inputs name the 2015 reagents. The TSO source-oligo exception was removed, so the guard checks 3 exact inputs. The 2016 TSO AT difference stays on the page. Upgraded to checked_with_corrections; the typeset article and the kit/index oligos are deferred.

Deferred follow-up 2026-10-17 (round deferred-followup-02). Tried to get the published Merritt 2020 Methods: OpenAlex/Unpaywall say closed with no repository copy, Europe PMC has a PMID but no PMCID (so no PMC or PMC Cloud manuscript), and web search found only bioRxiv 559021. Manual URL: https://www.nature.com/articles/s41587-020-0472-9. Read the preprint's NGS readout Methods (lines 854-865) as a labelled lead only. It matches MAN-10153-01's PCR, UDG step and two 1.2x AMPure cleanups but reports a prototype MiSeq 2x75 run, against the manual's 2x27. The page keeps the commercial manual's settings and is unchanged; the note records the routes and the lead. No missing-information claim accepted.

Deferred follow-up 2026-10-17 (round deferred-followup-02). Neither main text (2023 Nat Biotechnol, 2024 Nat Protoc) has an open route: OpenAlex/Unpaywall closed, no PMCID, no preprint. Manual URLs are recorded. Read instead the GEO GSE210563/GSE233875 sample records, SRA run info for PRJNA866163/PRJNA978429 and the authors' GLORI-tools README; all are placed in the sources dir with MANIFEST rows. GEO gives oligo(dT) mRNA (100-200 ng), library branches modified eCLIP/NEBNext Small RNA/SMARTer Pico v3, NovaSeq 6000 and HiSeq X Ten, and the trimming/dedup/UMI-removal pipeline. All deposited runs are single-end 150 nt, and the README takes single-end A-to-G reads with a 5-prime UMI. The sequencing text and note now carry these, labelled as deposition or derived evidence. Still deferred: Methods, library PCR primers, cycle settings, enzymes. No missing-information claim accepted.

HiRES (deferred-followup-02): defining Science article/supplement still unobtainable legitimately (Unpaywall/OpenAlex closed, science.org supplement 403, no Europe PMC copy or preprint; manual https://doi.org/10.1126/science.adg3797). Read the authors' GEO GSE223917/GSM6998595 sample text (lines 15-21): it repeats the Bio-protocol library stage and RNA-P7 sequence, adds cortex-nuclei input and Read 2-based RNA classification with UMI-tools dedup. Input label, caveat and source line updated; note records passages and routes. Status stays deferred.

inDrop v1 (deferred-followup-02): Cell supplement-1 still unobtainable legitimately (PMC gate, PMC Cloud TDM, Europe PMC not OA/403, publisher and ScienceDirect PDFs 403; manual PMC URL recorded). Read authors' GEO GSE65525 sample records: W1 printed GAGTGATTGCTTGTGACGCCTT (= model W1), Read 1 carries two barcode blocks split by W1, Read 2 cDNA; instruments HiSeq 2500/NextSeq 500/MiSeq; SRA gives only combined spot lengths. Page caveat and read-layout intro cite GEO; W1 stays inferred; no cycle counts added. Status stays deferred.

inDrop v2 (deferred-followup-02): acquisition-only pass for the paywalled Nature Protocols main text. Unpaywall and OpenAlex report it closed with no repository copy. Europe PMC has no PMCID and PubMed links no GEO series. Harvard DASH has no deposit, the Klein lab site links only the DOI, the ICCB mirror still returns 403, 1cell-bio.com returns 404, and the protocols.io API needs a token. No new original was read, so the model and page are unchanged; the note records the routes tried. RT/IVT/PCR conditions, sequencing cycles, the ExoI mechanism and aRNA fragmentation stay deferred. Manual URL: https://doi.org/10.1038/nprot.2016.154.

LamPORE (deferred-followup-02): the defining medRxiv preprint was obtained (v3, 30 Oct 2020, the latest version and unpublished) from its open medRxiv location and read: Methods 1-2, Assay design, Barcode demultiplexing, FIP barcode optimisation and references. Its Methods now source the protocol graph: NEB E1700 LAMP at 65 C for 35 min then 80 C for 5 min; column pooling; an SQK-RBK004 rapid-barcode reaction at 30 C then 80 C; 0.8x AMPure cleanup; RAP added after the cleanup (this order was previously inferred); and a 1 h MinION run. The scraper finds no oligo bases in the preprint. The commercial barcode and adapter bases, the 2020 ONT protocol revision, and the published Zhang primer table (behind Cloudflare) stay deferred. The note adds an open question: is the ORF1a AS1 set (cited to Rabe & Cepko) the same as Zhang's As1e?

lentiCRISPR v1 screening was deferred because the defining Shalem Supplementary Information could not be read. That supplement is still gated (PMC download gate, Europe PMC reports PMC4089965 not open access, the Science SOM is paywalled), and the OpenAlex green copy at MIT DSpace, fetched through the DSpace REST API, is the same Methods-free author manuscript. The pass did obtain one new author original: the Zhang-lab GeCKO readout-primer workbook (July 2014), recovered from the Internet Archive copy of genome-engineering.org and stored with a MANIFEST row. It states a single PCR for lentiCRISPR v1 and lentiGuide-Puro with mixed staggered forward primers, an extra adaptor PCR only for v2, and prints twelve primer pairs whose sample barcodes are 8-nt and inline rather than an Illumina index; the reverse priming site computes to lie downstream of the 76-nt scaffold, in cPPT, which follows the cassette in v1 only. The page dropped its unsupported claim that the original screen used a nested two-PCR readout and now carries a caveat describing the workbook and its two limits: it is headed plasmid readout and refers genomic-DNA readout back to the gated Supplementary Methods. Status moves to checked_with_corrections; the screen's own amplification and read lengths stay unassigned.

Microbe-seq stayed deferred_no_original: the published Science article and supplement are still unreadable. The pass did close the one open lead. OpenAlex and Unpaywall list a single green location, OSTI purl 1959222, previously recorded only as 'timed out'; www.osti.gov is unreachable from this host, but the Internet Archive snapshot of that purl returned the file, and it is the bioRxiv v1 preprint PDF itself (24 pages, bioRxiv cover, byte-size matching the copy already on disk), not an accepted manuscript. Europe PMC reports no PMCID and no open full text for PMID 35653470, and a plain request for the publisher supplement answers 403, which was not circumvented. The model is unchanged and remains explicitly preprint-derived and drawn inferred; the module docstring, the page caveat and the note were corrected to say that the only indexed open-access copy is the preprint, so the article and supplement must be obtained by hand. A MANIFEST note row records the identity check without storing a duplicate PDF.

Microwell-seq (deferred-followup-02): the defining Cell 2018 article is bronze OA at cell.com only and every route (cell.com, ScienceDirect) returns a Cloudflare challenge; not in PMC. GEO GSE108097/SRA give only a one-line protocol, HiSeq 2500 and 2x150 deposited spots. New original: the Guo lab's undated Microwell-seq web protocol on bis.zju.edu.cn/MCA plus its bead-making docx, read in full; its four Table 1 primers equal the Table S2-derived model. Graph gained lab-provenance reagents and an Exo I step; the Tn5 adapter is relabelled s7-ME at the N7xx end (still inferred) since the lab page names Nextera XT. Page shows the NextSeq 500 vs HiSeq 2500 instrument difference. STAR Methods, read lengths and Tn5 adapter bases remain deferred.

ONT Direct cDNA SQK-DCS109 (deferred-followup-02): the kit protocol is still unobtained. The nanoporetech.com document sitemap lists no DCS109 document, guessed document/product/store URLs return 404, the community protocol page no longer exists, Wayback was offline and protocols.io needs a token. The current successor protocol DCS_9187_v114_revK_24Feb2026 (SQK-LSK114) was read as a lead. It names Maxima H Minus RT, LongAmp second strand, Ultra II End Prep and user-supplied VN/SSP/PR2 primers but never mentions DCS109, so it is not used. Model and page are unchanged; the note records the routes. Kit primers, adapter, motor and reagents remain deferred.
