CH-ATAC-seq is `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed Dev Cell article PDF (mmc3), mmc1 and mmc2 were confirmed to be the defining originals. A third reader re-read the STAR Methods (p. e4-e5) and Results p. 795 and agreed with the earlier passes on reaction order, the 12-cycle KAPA PCR and the DNBSEQ-T7 recipe. The model is unchanged. The page and note gained a second published discrepancy: 3,000-6,000 nuclei per final well in the Results versus 3,000-10,000 in the Methods. The note and page also state, without drawing them, the reduced-index cell-line variant (96/384/32) and the bulk benchmark, and the printed 'Tween-10' is recorded verbatim. Chemistry 2 established/18 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 3 exact/0 failed. Still open: VAHTS MGI kit identity, TM primer bases/index length, 384-vs-96 P7.

SPATAC-seq is `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed Nature Cell Biology PDF and MOESM1-7 were confirmed by printed DOI, title and authors. A third reading of the Methods (transposome assembly through 11-cycle KAPA PCR and PE150) agrees with the earlier two passes, so no condition changed. MOESM5/6 are Source Data workbooks, MOESM2 is the Reporting Summary and MOESM7 is the uncropped ED Fig. 1c gel; none carries chemistry. One graph fate was corrected: same-ended Tn5 products are no longer said to be kept out of the PCR. They are carried into it, and their non-amplification is computed, not observed. Chemistry 0 established/20 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 0 exact/5 symbolic. Still open: the MGI conversion route.

sci-CAR-seq is `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed Cao 2018 Supplementary Materials PDF and table workbook were confirmed as the sci-CAR supplements. The workbook holds analysis Tables S1-S13 and no oligos, although earlier ledgers had called it the oligo workbook. SM pp. 16-18 now replace the GEO text as the authority; GEO agrees on primers and cycles. The graph now carries the printed RT, tagmentation, FACS, second-strand, DNA-binding-buffer/AMPure split and both PCR programmes and cleanups (RNA 0.8x AMPure; ATAC 1x AMPure plus >200 bp gel). Discard fates are no longer described as physical removal. The fixed-kidney order variant is stated on the page but not drawn. Chemistry 0 established/10 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 1 exact/5 symbolic. Still open: Cusanovich 2018 (ref 29) ATAC primers and index cycles, barcode lists, TDE1 adaptor bases.

ChIL-seq is `checked_with_corrections`: the hand-placed defining Nature Protocols PDF (identity verified) was read through Procedure Steps 7-84 and Reagent setup, with Harada 2019 NCB Methods for comparison. The warned post-transposition boundary is now the source in situ fill-in (T4 DNA polymerase + ligase + dNTPs). Tn5-then-MEDS-B loading, DNase I/RNeasy RNA recovery, SMART-Seq v4 TSO at first strand, SeqAmp 12-17-cycle PCR, two AMPure cleanups and E-Gel 250-350 bp selection are drawn; Read 1 is 51 cycles single-end. Chemistry 1 conditional/10 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 3 exact/3 without states. Still open: TSO identity, junction geometry, index cycles, mtChIL-seq branch.

SMART-seq2 is `checked_with_corrections`: the hand-placed Picelli 2013 article (identity verified) was read in its Online Methods, plus the 2014 Nature Protocols Reagent Setup. The graph now carries source RT and KAPA preamp programs, 1:1 AMPure after preamp, Nextera tagmentation conditions, Zymo DCC-5 cleanup, NPM/PPC 5-cycle enrichment PCR with 72C gap fill and the final AMPure. ISPCR has an exact state and Read 1 is 43 cycles. The TSO AC/ACAT discrepancy cites the 2014 Reagent Setup directly and notes the article's Supp Table 1 pointer slip. Chemistry 7/7/8/0; guard 1 exact. Still open: Nextera kit guides, index lengths.

SIMPLE-seq (notes-only) is `checked_with_corrections`: the hand-placed Bai 2024 Nat Biotechnol PDF (identity verified) was read in its Methods (Tn5 assembly, procedures, sequencing, read splitting). The note now uses the published reaction order and conditions in place of the bench protocol. The computed R02-R01-DNA_# Read 2 order reproduces the published barcode positions 11-15/48-52/79-83, and the Methods resolve the DNA_# 5'-phosphate question. Differences from the GEO/bench documents are recorded: read layout 200+6+100 vs 150+8+150, KAPA2G Robust vs KAPA HiFi, 48 vs 96 wells. Still open: predecessor methods, per-run read layout, NovaSeq Index 2.

CoBATCH (cobatch__10.1016+j.molcel.2019.07.015) — checked_with_corrections. Read the hand-fetched published Mol Cell article+SI (mmc7; identity verified) STAR Methods and Methods S1 bench protocol (mmc6). Published conditions match the preprint; source labels now cite the published Methods. Added Methods S1 step-24 well pooling with TIANGEN column cleanup before AMPure, transposome annealing/PAT loading conditions, and published lysis/PCR/PE150 details. Neither source prints loaded sequencing primers or index-read lengths, so read entry stays unresolved. Deferred: sequencing primers/index lengths, Tn5 assembly predecessors, other GEO sub-series split, low-input and native branches. Suite 6/6, page, pairing 0; chemistry 0/1/8/0; source guard 5 checked/0 failed/0 symbolic; Markdown and review schema pass.

Pore-C (pore-c__10.1038+s41587-022-01289-z) — checked_with_corrections. Confirmed the hand-placed published Nature Biotechnology PDF and re-read Results l99-131 and Methods l2525-2600. Every drawn condition matches (NlaIII digest, 65 C inactivation, T4 ligation 16 C 6 h, proteinase K 56 C 18 h, SPRI >1.5 kb, SQK-LSK109), so the model is unchanged. Rendering showed MOESM2 is the Reporting Summary with no chemistry, and the note now says so. Later LSK114 adapter example stays labelled inferred. Deferred: SQK-LSK109 kit document, DpnII/HindIII branches, per-run Supplementary Table detail. Suite 3/3, page, pairing 0; chemistry 0/2/6/0; source guard 0 checked/0 symbolic; Markdown and review schema pass.

Single-stranded ancient DNA (single-stranded-ancient-dna__10.1038+nprot.2013.038) — checked_with_corrections. Read the hand-downloaded corrected-after-print Nature Protocols PDF (identity verified): Table 1, Reagent setup, Procedure Steps 1-34 and the Corrigendum. CL72 is now source-verified from Table 1 and Step 34, and IS4 is added. The graph now carries the published enzymes and conditions for every step (Endo VIII/UDG, FastAP, CircLigase II, MyOne C1, Bst 2.0, T4 polymerase, T4 ligase, AccuPrime Pfx). New heat-release and cleanup steps carry source-stated waste fates. Six Table 1 transcription checks were added. Deferred: index-2 primer and read lengths, IS-primer predecessors, qPCR branch. Suite 104/104, page, pairing 0; chemistry 1/0/13/0; source guard 3 checked/3 symbolic; Markdown and review schema pass.

cfmedip-seq__10.1038+s41596-019-0202-2 - checked_with_corrections. Read the hand-placed defining Nature Protocols PDF (identity confirmed: printed DOI, title, authors) for Procedure Steps 46-158, which earlier passes had not curated. The graph now has the 10% input-control split (QC terminal, optional sequencing per Step 158), the MagMeDIP antibody/Magbead IP with MagWash washes, IP-EB elution and IPure Kit v2 purification, a PCR with source cycling (<=15 cycles for IP), a 1x AMPure cleanup and a 0.6x/1.2x dual size selection, all with discarded fractions. Sequencing text adds the Step 158 PE 75 bp recommendation. The NEB adaptor and primers stay inferred. Deferred: the NEB E7335L, MagMeDIP and IPure vendor manuals, the Shen 2018 predecessor, and Steps 1-21 filler details.

botseqs__10.1073+pnas.1607794113 - checked_with_corrections. Read the hand-placed PNAS SI Appendix and Dataset S1; identity was confirmed from the SI header and the matching Bot library IDs. The post-dilution PCR primers (TS-PCR Oligo1/2, one 3' phosphorothioate), Q5 Hot Start and the cycling are printed in the SI and match the patent values, so the patent-lead/inferred marking is removed. Cycles are 18 or 20 per Dataset S1. A 1.0x AMPure cleanup with waste is added. The dilution input now cites the SI serial scheme and the per-library factors (10^5 for most, 10^7 for six, 10-fold for two low-input libraries). All 43 libraries ran PE 2x100 on HiSeq 2500 Rapid. Deferred: the 2016 TruSeq PCR-Free guide revision (adapter index, size-selection placement) and index-read acquisition.

slide-seq__10.1126+science.aaw1219 - checked_with_corrections. Read the hand-placed published author-manuscript supplement (PMC NIHMS1036108 supplement-1.docx). Its title and authors match. A word-level comparison showed the library preparation is unchanged from the bioRxiv v1 lead, so provenance now points to the published supplement. Published Table S1 adds ten 5'-phosphorylated SOLiD decoding primers (Truseq/UP series), now shown on the decoder input. A new computed discrepancy notice covers Truseq -1, whose printed base differs from the recessed reverse complement of the bead handle; it is shown as printed. The TSO/SMART notice is kept and now cites the published Table S1. Deferred: the Science-hosted supplement PDF, Drop-seq bead synthesis, the SOLiD SR-75 chemistry and position layout, and typo-versus-intended for the TSO and Truseq -1.

**geomx-dsp__10.1038+s41587-020-0472-9** — checked with corrections (deferred-followup-03). The hand-placed publisher PDF is the version of record (printed DOI, title and authors verified). Its Online Methods NGS readout (lines 3526–3545) matches MAN-10153-01 on PCR and both 1.2× AMPure cleanups. The published runs were MiSeq 2×75 or NextSeq 2×37, while the manual gives 2×27+8+8. That difference is now a graph-owned source discrepancy on the page, and the model keeps the commercial run. The probe design passage (iSpPC linker; indexing oligo with PCR sites, UMI and per-tile barcode) agrees with the model. The scraper finds no bases. Deferred: master-mix composition, proprietary tag/primer/UDI bases, the article's index-read lengths and QC branches.

**chip-seq__10.1038+nmeth1068** — checked with corrections (deferred-followup-03). The hand-placed article PDF was verified as Robertson 2007 by its printed DOI, title and authors. Methods lines 750–815 replace the guide-derived order. PAGE size selection (100–300 bp) of the ChIP DNA now comes before end repair. Phenol–chloroform/ethanol cleanups follow end repair and A-tailing. The adapter mix is diluted 1/10, and QIAquick follows ligation. The guide's post-ligation agarose gel is removed. Phusion PCR uses primers 1.1/2.1 in separate 10- and 15-cycle reactions, and the run is 27 single-end cycles. The article/guide differences are a graph-owned discrepancy on the page. Adapter and primer bases remain inferred. Deferred: the historical Illumina sequence document, whether a post-ligation gel was run, which PCR product was sequenced, and the predecessor ChIP method.

**spatial-cut-tag__10.1126+science.abg7216** — checked with corrections (deferred-followup-03). The hand-placed Science Supplementary Materials DOCX was verified by its title and authors. Methods lines 887–932 and Tables S1–S3 were read. The 2022 Tn5ME-A (line 912) carries the 5'-phosphorylated CATCGGCGTACGACT extension that ligation linker 1 splints, so the formerly inferred adapter is now printed and computed. Every other oligo and all 100 barcodes are identical to the 2024 Nature Protocols tables. The published discrepancy is now 2022 vs 2024 Tn5ME-A, and the model uses 2022. Antibody, wash, lysis (SDS/TAPS), PCR and 1.3× AMPure conditions follow 2022. Deferred: Diagenode loading guidelines, the meaning of the 2024 Tn5ME-A row, Farzad bin files, the historical repository and Ad2 assignment.

inDrop v1 is `checked_with_corrections`. The published Cell supplement (mmc1) and article PDF (mmc6) were read and their identity checked. Every Table S5 oligo in the model was re-checked against mmc1 and none changed. The graph now has Bst 2.0 bead extension with NaOH stripping, SuperScript III RT, and ExoI + HinFI digestion followed by an SPRI cleanup that discards the supernatant. It also gains SAP before RLO ligation and 10–13 enrichment PCR cycles. Two discrepancies between article and supplement are now graph-owned and shown on the page: carrier oil 90 vs 80 µL/hr and PFO 0.2X vs 1 volume. Chemistry 13 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 6 exact/0 failed/1 exempted. Still open: Jaitin 2014 MARS-seq conditions, V1 read primers and cycle counts, and a second reader.

eccDNA Circle-Seq is `checked_with_corrections`. The maintainer's hand-downloaded PNAS PDF and SI Appendix were checked for identity and read; the SI contains only figure and dataset legends. Plasmid spike-ins (pBR322/pUC19/pUG72 at 1:1/1:50/1:2,500 per cell) are now a purification input. The native circle is no longer modelled as ligation-closed ssDNA; it is declared a pre-existing circle. The phi29 priming site is labelled representative. The 100 Mb vs 40–100 million reads discrepancy was re-verified. Chemistry 6 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 0 exact inputs. Still open: Datasets S1–S3 (not chemistry), Apollo 324 kit documentation, the REPLI-g/Plasmid Mini AX protocols and how the index was read.

SureSelect XT HS2 DNA is `checked_with_corrections`. These are the first located readings of the validated originals: G9957-90000 B0 (May 2026), G9985-90011 A2 (spot-check) and the XTHS2 index workbook. The checklist rows that had been withdrawn are now back to read. The MBC is corrected to a 3-nt MBC plus 2 discarded bases at each read start, per manual Step 4. The graph gains named T4 ligase, Herculase II, Blocker Mix and Post-Capture Primer Mix, AMPure cleanups with discards, and pre-capture pooling. It also gains on-bead PCR with bead removal, and the manual's temperatures and cycle ranges. Index reads are 8+8 cycles; read length is user-chosen. Chemistry 12 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 0 exact/4 inputs without exact states. Still open: adaptor and primer bases (not printed), the MBC-free branch, the Fast-Hyb guide and a second reader.

inDrop v2 (indrop-v2__10.1038+nprot.2016.154): checked_with_corrections. The publisher PDF of the Nature Protocols main text is now in the bundle; its doi, title and authors were checked before use. Read Fig. 3, Reagent Setup, Steps 43-79 and 126-166, and Table 1. All model oligos match the main text. The graph gains the bead HinfI/BA19/ExoI/alkaline clean-up, post-RT ExoI+HinfI digestion, zinc-ion fragmentation before PE2-N6 RT, and the named enzymes and conditions. The page read table now shows the Table 1 sequencing examples. A new discrepancy notes 'PE1 primer' vs '24 PE2 primer variants' in Reagent Setup. Deferred: bead HinfI purpose, removal of digestion products, MOESM459, second reader. Suite, page, pairing (0), chemistry 0/0/14/0, guard 5 exact/0 failed, Markdown and review schema pass.

Fiber-seq (fiber-seq__10.1126+science.aaz1646): checked_with_corrections. The hand-downloaded Science Supplementary Materials (printed DOI/title checked) were read: AdMTase-seq and Fiber-seq Materials and Methods. Graph inputs now carry the 2020 nuclei preparation, in-house Hia5 at 37 C for 10 min, 0.8 mM SAM, 1% SDS stop, RNase A/proteinase K + phenol-chloroform extraction, and Megaruptor 10-15 kb shearing. The SM names no SMRTbell kit, so the end-prep/hairpin/nuclease steps stay labelled as the current SPK 3.0. New published discrepancy: the SM gives 200 U Hia5, the GEO records 500 U, for the main samples. Deferred: 2020 PacBio kits, K562 nuclei reference, dose resolution, publisher main-article copy, second reader. Selftest, page, pairing (0), chemistry 0/1/7/0, guard 0 checked/0 failed, Markdown and review schema pass.

TAIL-seq (tail-seq__10.1016+j.molcel.2014.02.007): checked_with_corrections. The hand-placed Molecular Cell article and the mmc1 Supplemental Information were checked against their printed DOI/title and read: Results features, Experimental Procedures, library construction, sequence processing and the oligo list. The inferred TruSeq shell is replaced by the printed oligos: /5App/ N15 biotin-dT x2 /3ddC/ DNA 3' adapter, RNA 5' adapter, RT primer, P5 forward primer and nine indexed reverse primers (no. 2 as representative). Ligation geometry is now known single-strand. Read sites are derived because no sequencing primer is printed; the index read is not claimed as acquired. Deferred: ligases, RT/PCR conditions, loaded sequencing primers and index acquisition, second reader. Suite 8/8, page, pairing (0), chemistry 0/0/10/0, guard 5 exact/0 failed, Markdown and review schema pass.

RIC-seq is `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed defining Nature PDF and the Cao 2021 Nature Protocols PDF were verified by printed DOI and authors, then read. The Methods replace the pCp-biotin MAGIC boundary with FastAP, then T4 RNA ligase pCp-biotin ligation (overnight 16 C), a second FastAP, T4 PNK, and T4 RNA ligase EL0021 proximity ligation. Glycine is 0.125 M; adapters are 'Illumina Y-shaped adaptors' without bases; gel step is MinElute; sequencing is paired-end at Novogene. Nat Protoc Table 1 PEI adapters are shown in the note as 2021-scoped and not assumed for 2020. Chemistry 21 unresolved/0 contradictory, no MAGIC; guard 0 exact/5 without exact states. Still open: 2020 adaptor bases, read lengths, role-level final library, second reader.

GLORI is `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed Liu 2023 Nature Biotechnology article and Shen 2024 Nature Protocols were verified by printed DOI and read. The graph now carries the 2023 protection, deamination and deprotection conditions (8 h 16 C; the unsupported 4 C overnight was removed). 'repair' is corrected to a T4 PNK 3' dephosphorylation. Rnl2tr KQ/Rnl1 ligations, deadenylase+RecJf and ExoI digestions, 1.8x XP and the 165-250 bp gel are explicit, with waste. Linker, RT primer and UMI adapter are exact inputs with 3SpC3 blocks. The read layout is resolved as 2x150 PE HiSeq X Ten with R2 (UMI-first, RNA-sense) analysed. Chemistry 14 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 3 exact/0 failed. Still open: PCR primer bases (NEBNext E7335 document), index cycles, kit branches, second reader.

Telomere Profiling is `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed Science Supplementary Materials (printed DOI and authors verified) were read and now replace the superseded bioRxiv v2 as model source. Corrections: ClaI internal digest, PvuI release, the corrected C1 wash order, and SQK-LSK110 on R9.4.1. Table S3's 14 TeloTags match or extend the model (8 added), and the fig. S1A NB50 splints equal the derived family. Three published discrepancies are now on the page: Results order vs Methods, annealing units, and the 'table S2' label. Chemistry 2 established/8 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 1 exact/6 exempt. Still open: ONT kit adapter, unit inconsistency, derived non-NB50 splints, second reader.

scCARE-seq is now `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed defining NSMB PDF was verified (printed DOI, title, Qu/Sun...Ding authors) and its Methods were read (lines 2335-2485). The model credited the wrong authors (Deng) and mis-transcribed the A5-PreA primer by one missing C; both are corrected, and the source guard now passes 8/8 exact. The graph gains second-strand synthesis before sorting, a proteinase K lysis step with the LIANTI carrier ssDNA, and ExoI primer digestion after the three-primer Q5 pre-amplification. Ligation is direct T4 ligation of MboI ends with no biotin fill-in, so the junction is drawn as a derived GATC; biotin-dT and streptavidin belong only to bulk CARE-seq. The page also shows the 'barcoded adapter' wording mismatch. Still open: carrier role, read lengths, pre-amplification pairing. Chemistry 2/0/13/0.

Microwell-seq is now `checked_with_corrections`. The hand-placed Cell PDF was verified (printed DOI, title, authors, appended 2018 correction), and its STAR Methods and Key Resources Table were read (lines 2510-2565, 2670-2736). Conditions previously taken from the undated Guo-lab web page now follow the published Methods: Exo I 60 min, WTA 4 + 10-14 cycles, library PCR 14 cycles, lysis buffer, bead synthesis, HiSeq. Tagmentation is relabelled as the customized TruePrep V2 transposase with two identical insertion sequences. Its bases are not printed, so the s7-ME end stays inferred and dotted. The old tagmentation 'waste' is now the STAR Methods' self-looped internal fragments, an unresolved PCR off-product. Source guard: 7/7 exact. Still open: Vazyme insertion bases, read lengths, mmc3-mmc7. Chemistry 3/0/9/0.

TUC-seq stays `deferred`, now with the original SI read. The hand-placed Wiley SI was verified (title, authors, internal file name match PubMed 28817234). The model now carries the SI's sourced steps: HEK293 4sU pulse, TRIzol/DNase extraction, OsO4/NH4Cl conversion (Fig.S1 450 uM/180 mM; 3 h at room temperature), GoScript random-hexamer RT, barcoded gene-specific PCR with the seven Supporting Table 4 primer pairs, then gel purification and equimolar pooling. The GATC Biotech library preparation stays a warned boundary. The page shows a published cross-reference error: the primers are cited as Table S1 but printed in Table 4. The 2024 chapter correction is author-name only. Still missing: the main article's Experimental Section (conversion protocol), barcode overhangs, read lengths. Chemistry 0/0/8/0; guard 0 exact (3 symbolic).

**scRepli-seq** (`screpli-seq__10.1038+s41596-020-0378-5`) - checked_with_corrections. The defining Nature Protocols PDF is now on disk (hand-placed; identity verified). Read: Procedure Steps 17–79, Reagents, Steps 82/88 and Troubleshooting. Library PCR is now drawn: TPC1/TPC2 phosphorothioate primers, 5–7 cycles chosen by qPCR, exact sites on inferred TruSeq-type ends, then 0.9x AMPure dimer removal. Added the lysis/heat-fragmentation step. SeqPlex restored as DOP-PCR-based. Eco57I-released ~40-bp adaptors are removed by a represented 1.8x AMPure step. Adaptor is NEXTflex Barcodes – 48; reads are HiSeq 1500 80-bp single-read. Chemistry 2 conditional/23 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 4 exact/4 unrepresented. Still open: SeqPlex/SEQXE and NEXTflex sequences, index length, second reader.

**RiboMethSeq** (`ribomethseq-illumina__10.1093+nar+gkw547`) - deferred (was deferred_no_original). The Birkedal 2015 Wiley SI is now on disk (hand-placed; identity verified) and was read: Methods l.1–175 and Fig. S2–S5 legends. The Birkedal article main text is still missing. The page caveat now gives the SI's own description of the undrawn Ion Torrent original. That covers carbonate hydrolysis, 20–40-nt PAGE, kinase-dead AtRNL S701A, CR30/CR35 and P1 IVT adaptors verbatim, PNK/FastAP steps and the barcoded Ion A RT primer. The SI-internal P1 IVT T/U inconsistency is shown. Drawn Marchand 2016 graph unchanged. Chemistry 9 unresolved/0 contradictory; guard 0 exact/5 unrepresented. Still open: article main text, RT primer bases, NEBNext manual, catalogue identity.

SPLASH (splash__10.1016+j.molcel.2016.04.028): the defining Aw 2016 Molecular Cell article was finally read from a hand-placed publisher PDF (mmc7) that contains both the article and its supplement. Its identity was checked against the DOI, title and authors. The Experimental Procedures confirm the modelled reaction order and photoreversal at 254 nm. Where the model had followed the 2017 JoVE protocol, it now uses the 2016 values: Q5 polymerase with 9-12 cycles and NEB Primers Set 1, a 200-220 nt cDNA band, 100 mM NaOH, plus the ligase and RT conditions, NextSeq 500 and 2x150 reads. The page shows the 2016-versus-2017 differences (linker gel, cDNA gel, polymerase) as source notices. Still deferred: base-resolved circle and amplicon geometry from the Fig S2F panel, the gel selections not yet drawn, and the E7335 index and lot. Status upgraded from deferred to checked_with_corrections.

sci-ATAC-seq (sci-atac-seq__10.1126+science.aab1601): the defining 2015 Science Supplementary Materials and Table S2 were hand-placed and their identity checked (Table S2 is differential accessibility and has no oligos). Sample Processing and Sequencing give the tagmentation, stop, FACS sort, 20 PCR cycles with standard Nextera barcodes (10-bp barcodes after the first three experiments) and MiSeq v2 paired 51-bp reads with custom primers from refs 17/18. The graph labels are now source-qualified, and Tn5 release and NPM conditions are marked as inherited from the cited Amini 2014 protocol. Reads are 51+51. The 2015 primer set no longer borrows the 2018 custom Index 2 primer; it draws an inferred P5 forward-strand i5 read instead, which matches the GEO barcode order. The caveat explains which index variant is drawn. Still deferred: the Nextera index-kit bases, the 10-bp set, Adey 2014, the index cycle numbers, and the same-end fate convention.

## Shared-code and catalogue follow-up queue

Reported by round workers, who may not edit shared code or catalogue tables. Each item is a
lead to verify, not an established defect. Remove an item once it is handled.

Three parallel fix rounds (2026-10-17) cleared the original 312-item queue and its follow-ups: round 1 (12 file-disjoint packages) fixed 230, round 2 (10 packages) fixed 62, round 3 (7 packages) fixed 26; the rest were closed as already handled or not defects. After round 3 every suite passes, the source-oligo guard checks 1000 oligos with 5 failures (Atrandi x3: vendor guides reCAPTCHA-gated; ChAR-seq bridge x2: figure/preprint-only, a decision below), the site builds 407/407 and both lints are clean. Pre-fix queues and per-round worker reports are kept only in the session scratchpad. That is the pre-review snapshot. The critical diff review below resolves the ChAR-seq figure question using the published original, without a preprint exception. What remains:

### Blocked retrievals (manual download or no public original)

Script access is refused by challenge or paywall; do not work around it. A polite sequential Springer Nature retry (round 3) found nature.com subscription pages, not rate blocks.

- [A-guard-scraper-mdfacts] PARIS/PEM-seq oligo split across adjacent PDF table cells — *In PEM-seq MOESM1 lines 7085-7110 the P5-I5 pieces are separated by other cells ('Hiseq tail', 'Oligos for tagging PCR', 'P5-I5'). No layout-free rule joins them without new false joins. Documented this limitation in the scrape_primers docstring: join by hand from the rendered table, then confirm with --find. The oligo is not in the PARIS bundle at all.*
- [B-fetch-doctext] rnascope: Bio-Techne curl error 92, HTTP/1.1 fallback — *Implemented: curl is retried once with --http1.1 on error 92, in both get() and download(). Downloads that receive nothing for 60 s are now abandoned and not retried, so stalled hosts no longer hang runs. Bio-Techne still blocks: over HTTP/1.1 it sends 0 bytes until timeout (tested directly), so both manuals remain (manual) with 'timed out or stalled'. Changing the User-Agent to get past the bot wall was not attempted.*
- [B-fetch-doctext] botseqs: PNAS SI Appendix and sd01.xlsx acquisition attempt — *One plain GET each to the pnas.org suppl_file URLs returned HTTP 403 Cloudflare 'Just a moment...'. Recorded as (manual) rows with date and reason. The PMC routes remain gated.*
- [B-fetch-doctext] microwell-seq: Cell 2018 article acquisition attempt — *http://www.cell.com/article/S0092867418301168/pdf and the showPdf?pii URL both returned HTTP 403 Cloudflare challenges, even from the university network. Recorded as a (manual) row. Not solved by script.*

A row pointing at the homepage would cache an HTML page, not the originals. The guard still has 3 failures. Fixing it needs locator handling for hand-added manifest originals (package A) or a real URL.*

The tecan.com/hubfs UG/QP name patterns, including a search-indexed QP URL, return 404, and lifesciences.tecan.com returns 404. No working official locator found; the row is unchanged. Owned by B.*

## Critical diff review — 2026-10-17

Scope: high-impact shared-code changes and source-evidence boundaries in the
uncommitted diff; this is not a re-review of all 410 experimental recipes.

- Corrected protocols.io cache/export acceptance: a different accession, contradictory
  DOI/Version labels or changed cached bytes must not establish the cited original.
  Rejected downloads cannot supply attachments.
- Removed automatic bioRxiv/medRxiv authority when a linked publication exists.
  Byte-identical files identified as published originals still qualify. Published
  Genome Research DOIs (`10.1101/gr.*`) remain eligible.
- Corrected cycle arithmetic: known molecular lengths override schematic widths, and
  features beyond an unknown-length insert are not assigned exact cycles.
- Corrected branch rendering: sharing an output drawing with one input does not
  justify hiding another contributing input's preparation.
- Mixed exact/unresolved sequencing plans now show each terminal separately;
  all-unresolved pages retain their existing source-declared views.
- Small API/HTML requests now trigger the same challenge cool-down as downloads.
- Index check summaries require a successful exit as well as a pass banner.
- ChAR-seq: visually verified the original published figure from the cached
  PMC5962340 supplementary ZIP. Existing bridge strings agree; the guard records
  two explicit image-transcription exemptions, not text-match passes. No preprint
  exception needed. Earlier reports describing a preprint-only question are historical.
- Atrandi: the three existing locator-filter failures remain explicit; no blanket
  acceptance of hand-added manifests or unrecorded retrieval routes was introduced.

Verification: 401 legacy + 6 wrapper-free suites pass; shared 1272/source-fetcher
226/catalogue 83 checks pass; all review schemas pass. Final build 407/407 diagrams
and all relative links pass; pairing and P5 lints have no new findings. Scoped
source guards checked 63 strands, with 3 unchanged Atrandi failures, 3 explicit
exemptions and 6 unrepresented inputs. The default chemistry pilot checks have
0 contradictory claims; this does not validate all catalogue chemistry. Details
and remaining limits are recorded in `HANDOVER-REVIEW.md`.

## How to resume

1. Re-read the current worktree, `AGENTS.md`, `DESIGN.md` and this file.
2. Every catalogue protocol now has a recorded capped pass or attempt. Remaining work:
   (a) obtain by hand the originals listed under "Deferred protocols — originals still
   to obtain", drop them into `$CHEM_DATA/sources/<slug>/`, run
   `tools/get_sources.py --revalidate`, and finish those passes; (b) work through the
   shared-code and catalogue follow-up queue; (c) second independent readers where a
   missing-information claim is wanted (the two-reader gate is not met by any capped
   pass); (d) item (d) of the previous snapshot — catalogue identity decisions — is resolved.
3. Cap investigation at ten minutes and three original documents per protocol.
   Published articles and their supplements supersede preprints.
4. Read and write source bundles directly under
   `/husky/henriksson/NGS101_papers/sources/<slug>/`. Run the oligo scraper as a
   reading aid; verify source passages.
5. Record inspected sections, corrections and deferrals in a new round ledger and
   `catalogue/source_reviews/<slug>.json`. Keep historical readings intact.
6. Verify changed suites/pages, pairing, chemistry, transcription guards and review
   schemas; record runtime caps rather than calling them passes. Update this snapshot.
   Stage named files only; do not commit unless requested.


## Critical re-review of deferred follow-up 03 (2026-10-17)

The 31-protocol batch was screened for critical declaration/source discrepancies;
ten protocols received corrections, recorded in
[capped review ledger](catalogue/curation_rounds/2026-10-17-critical-rereview-followup-03.tsv)
and appended bounded checks. TAIL-seq's modified adapter bases, variable native
tails and RNA/RT states were corrected; SMART-seq2 no longer treats processed
43-base reads as acquired cycles; SureSelect's known two-base non-MBC span is
explicit. Unsupported physical-discard claims were replaced by unresolved fates
in SMART-seq2, sci-CAR, CoBATCH, Slide-seq, scCARE, inDrop v2 and ancient ssDNA.
cfMeDIP retains both denatured polarities in its input-control/IP population.

Thirty applicable suites, all 31 scoped chemistry/source guards, shared 1275 and
source-fetcher 226 checks pass. Guards checked 66 exact strands, with 8 declared
exemptions and 60 inputs without exact states; there were no timeouts. See
[review handover](HANDOVER-REVIEW.md) for limits and final site verification.
This critical review does not change the 404 inspected / 6 deferred census or
complete any linked-source absence audit.


## Initial validation migration inventory (2026-10-18)

The initial cohort contained 182 protocols: 181 with references to known queued
shared recipes/defaults and CRISPR-MIP, which then lacked a native typed graph.
The [current migration worklist](catalogue/validation_migration_pending.tsv)
tracks the remaining cohort; see the progress section below. The
[whole-catalogue snapshot](catalogue/validation_migration.tsv) records all 410
protocols, including six stale ledger-only ownership entries (migrated 2026-10-19; see the Batch14 checkpoint).
[Molecular-coverage follow-ups](catalogue/validation_migration_coverage_followup.tsv)
initially listed 190 graph-backed protocols emitting only unresolved claims; this overlaps
the ownership worklist and is not a separate migration count.

All 402 native graphs were instantiated and audited without contradictions.
Only 11 declared an explicit input/output transition relation, and all 402 still
had unresolved claims. Existing PCR/pairing checks are valid narrow checks;
absence of a transition flag alone does not establish an architecture defect.
See [scope, method and reconciliation](catalogue/chemistry_decoupling.md) before
assigning migrations. This snapshot does not accept missing-information claims
or change the capped-source-curation census.


## Protocol migration progress (2026-10-18)

The initial 182-protocol migration cohort now has **182 verified completions and
0 pending** (Batch13 checkpoint, 2026-10-19). The authoritative current worklist is
[catalogue/validation_migration_pending.tsv](catalogue/validation_migration_pending.tsv);
[completed records](catalogue/validation_migration_completed.tsv) preserve former
dependencies and scoped verification. Batch13 finished the last five (meta3C, PTA, scRepli-seq, scRRBS, REPLI-g Single Cell); see the Batch13 checkpoint. Batch03 adds native declarations for DNA Prep shotgun metagenomics, NanoSeq, NG Capture-C, INVADEseq, OAK, Paired-seq, OTTR-seq, PANDORA-seq, PARS, oxBS-seq and Ovation RNA-Seq V2.
The [migration round](catalogue/migration_rounds/2026-10-18-01.tsv)
and protocol bounded checks record actual source scope and corrections.

CRISPR-MIP's native graph is an implementation migration using its existing
source-derived molecular constructors, not a finding of missing published data.
Independent reviews corrected ss-circle PCR phase geometry and VDJ RT/template-
switch state ordering. Whole-protocol chemistry coverage and prior source
obligations remain distinct from ownership migration completion. Ownership migration remains
incomplete for the remaining cohort. See [current migration progress](catalogue/chemistry_decoupling.md).

### Batch04 migration checkpoint

Nine more native migrations are verified: MALBAC-DT, Paired-tag, PARIS, R-ChIP,
RAGE-seq, RAISIN RNA-seq, RamDA-seq, ReDeeM and RIC-seq. Source-version boundaries,
actual native PCR inputs, independent molecular populations, identifier lineage,
chemical modifications and physical versus computational fates were reviewed.
ReDeeM RNA now declares the known reaction sequence rather than hiding it in one
commercial-kit boundary. See [batch04 ledger](catalogue/migration_rounds/2026-10-18-04.tsv).

The DNA worker was stopped twice by a biological-risk tool filter. LAMP-seq, PTA,
REPLI-g Single Cell and scRepli-seq remain pending and unchanged; no retry or bypass
was attempted after the second stop. This is a task-execution deferral, not a finding
about source disclosure or scientific validity, and the filter did not identify a
specific causative protocol within its task context. Other pending migrations remain
available, so the overall goal stays active.


### Batch05 migration checkpoint

Nine native migrations are verified: scCARE-seq, sci-CAR-seq, scRICA-seq,
